imā kira catassaupi gāthā thairau supinantaina rañ‍ñau kathaisi| rājā supinaṃ disvā thairaṃ namassantauyaiva pabujjhitvā pabhātāya rattiyā thairaṃ upasaṅkamitvā vanditvā attanā diṭṭhaniyāmaina supinaṃ kathaisi| taṃ sutvā thairau tā gāthā pac‍cunubhāsitvā ‘‘sabbaṃ suṇātī’’tiādinā dvīhi gāthāhi rājānaṃ auvadi| tattha sabbaṃ suṇāti sautaināti idha sautabbaṃ saddaṃ āpāthagataṃ sabbaṃ subhāsitaṃ dubbhāsitañ‍ca abadhirau sautaina suṇāti| tathā sabbaṃ rūpaṃ sundaraṃ asundarampi cakkhunā anandhau passati, ayamindriyānaṃ sabhāvau| tattha pana na ca diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ dhīrau, sabbaṃ ujjhitunti ca nidassanamattamaitaṃ| yañhi taṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ vā, na taṃ sabbaṃ dhīrau sappañ‍ñau ujjhituṃ paric‍cajituṃ gahaituṃ vā arahati| guṇāguṇaṃ pana tattha upaparikkhitvā ujjhitabbamaiva ujjhituṃ gahaitabbañ‍ca gahaituṃ arahati, tasmā cakkhumāssa yathā andhau cakkhumāpi samānau ujjhitabbai diṭṭhai andhau yathā assa apassantau viya bhavaiyya, tathā ujjhitabbai sutai sautavāpi badhirau yathā assa asuṇantau viya bhavaiyya| pañ‍ñavāssa yathā mūgauti vicāraṇapañ‍ñāya pañ‍ñavā vacanakusalaupi avattabbai mūgau viya bhavaiyya| balavā thāmasampan‍naupi akattabbai dubbalauriva, rakārau padasandhikarau, asamatthau viya bhavaiyya| atha atthai samuppan‍nai, sayaitha matasāyikanti attanā kātabbakic‍cai uppan‍nai upaṭṭhitai matasāyikaṃ sayaitha, matasāyikaṃ sayitvāpi taṃ kic‍caṃ tīraitabbamaiva, na virādhaitabbaṃ| atha vā atha atthai samuppan‍naiti attanā akaraṇīyai atthai kic‍cai uppan‍nai upaṭṭhitai matasāyikaṃ sayaitha, matasāyikaṃ sayitvāpi taṃ na kātabbamaiva| na hi paṇḍitau ayuttaṃ kātumarahatīti aivaṃ thairaina auvaditau rājā akattabbaṃ pahāya kātabbaiyaiva yuttappayuttau ahausīti|

mahākac‍cāyanatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. sirimittatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

ak‍kaudhanautiādikā  āyasmatau sirimittatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rājagahai mahaddhanakuṭumbikassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, sirimittauti laddhanāmau| tassa kira mātā siriguttassa bhaginī| tassa vatthu dhammapadavaṇṇanāyaṃ (dha॰ pa॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.garahadin‍navatthu) āgatamaiva| sau siriguttassa bhāginaiyyau sirimittau vayappattau satthu dhanapāladamanai laddhappasādau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pattau| aikadivasaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ uddisituṃ āsanaṃ abhiruhitvā cittabījaniṃ gahaitvā nisin‍nau bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ kathaisi| kathaintau ca uḷāratarai guṇai vibhajitvā dassaintau –

502.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

sa vai tādisakau bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

503.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

guttadvārau sadā bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

504.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

kalyāṇasīlau sau bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

505.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

kalyāṇamittau sau bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

506.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

kalyāṇapañ‍ñau sau bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

507.

 ‘‘yassa saddhā tathāgatai, acalā suppatiṭṭhitā|

sīlañ‍ca yassa kalyāṇaṃ, ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ||

508.

 ‘‘saṅghai pasādau yassatthi, ujubhūtañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

adaliddauti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

509.

 ‘‘tasmā  saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha ak‍kaudhanauti akujjhanasīlau| upaṭṭhitai hi kaudhuppattinimittai adhivāsanakhantiyaṃ ṭhatvā  kaupassa anuppādakau| anupanāhīti na upanāhakau, paraihi kataṃ aparādhaṃ paṭic‍ca ‘‘ak‍kaucchi maṃ avadhi maṃ, ajini maṃ ahāsi mai’’tiādinā (dha॰ pa॰ 3; mahāva॰ 464; ma॰ ni॰ 3.237) kaudhassa anupanayhanasīlau| santadausapaṭicchādanalakkhaṇāya māyāya abhāvatau amāyau| pisuṇavācāvirahitatau rittapaisuṇau, sa vai tādisakau bhikkhūti sau tathārūpau tathājātikau yathāvuttaguṇasaman‍nāgatau bhikkhu| aivaṃ yathāvuttapaṭipattiyā paic‍ca paralaukai na saucati saukanimittassa abhāvatau| cakkhudvārādayau kāyadvārādayau ca guttā pihitā saṃvutā aitassāti guttadvārau| kalyāṇasīlauti sundarasīlau suvisuddhasīlau| kalyāṇamittauti –

‘‘piyau garubhāvaniyau, vattā ca vacanakkhamau|

gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, nau caṭṭhānai niyaujayai’’ti|| (a॰ ni॰ 7.37) –

aivaṃ vibhāvitalakkhaṇau kalyāṇamittau aitassāti kalyāṇamittau| kalyāṇapañ‍ñauti sundarapañ‍ñau| yadipi pañ‍ñā nāma asundarā natthi, niyyānikāya pana pañ‍ñāya vasaina aivaṃ vuttaṃ

aivamaittha kaudhādīnaṃ vikkhambhanavasaina samucchaidavasaina ca ak‍kaudhanādimukhaina, puggalādhiṭṭhānāya gāthāya sammāpaṭipattiṃ dassaitvā idāni nipphattitalaukuttarasaddhādikai uddharitvā puggalādhiṭṭhānāya aiva gāthāya sammāpaṭipattiṃ dassaintau ‘‘yassa saddhā’’tiādimāha| tassatthau – yassa puggalassa tathāgatai sammāsambuddhai ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinayappavattā maggaināgatasaddhā, tatau aiva acalā avikampā suṭṭhu patiṭṭhitā| ‘‘atthī’’ti, padaṃ ānaitvā sambandhitabbaṃ| ariyakantanti ariyānaṃ kantaṃ piyāyitaṃ bhavantaraipi avijahanatau| pasaṃsitanti buddhādīhi pasaṭṭhaṃ, vaṇṇitaṃ thaumitaṃ atthīti yaujanā| taṃ panaitaṃ sīlaṃ gahaṭṭhasīlaṃ pabbajitasīlanti  duvidhaṃ| tattha gahaṭṭhasīlaṃ nāma pañ‍casikkhāpadasīlaṃ, yaṃ gahaṭṭhaina rakkhituṃ sak‍kā| pabbajitasīlaṃ nāma dasasikkhāpadasīlaṃ upādāya sabbaṃ catupārisuddhisīlaṃ, tayidaṃ sabbampi akhaṇḍādibhāvaina aparāmaṭṭhatāya ‘‘kalyāṇa’’nti vaiditabbaṃ|

saṅghai pasādau yassatthīti ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’’tiādinā ariyasaṅghai pasādau saddhā yassa puggalassa atthi acalau suppatiṭṭhitauti ānaitvā yaujaitabbaṃ| ujubhūtañ‍ca dassananti diṭṭhivaṅkābhāvatau kilaisavaṅkābhāvatau ca ujubhūtaṃ| akuṭilaṃ ajimhaṃ kammassakatādassanañ‍caiva sappac‍cayanāmarūpadassanañ‍cāti duvidhampi dassanaṃ yassa atthi acalaṃ suppatiṭṭhitanti  yaujanā| adaliddauti taṃ āhu saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, sutadhanaṃ, cāgadhanaṃ, pañ‍ñādhananti imaisaṃ suvisuddhānaṃ dhanānaṃ atthitāya ‘‘adaliddau’’ti taṃ tādisaṃ puggalaṃ buddhādayau ariyā āhu| amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ tassa tathārūpassa jīvitaṃ diṭṭhadhammikādiatthādhigamaina amaughaṃ avañjhaṃ saphalamaivāti āhūti atthau|

tasmāti, yasmā yathāvuttasaddhādiguṇasaman‍nāgatau puggalau ‘‘adaliddau amaughajīvitau’’ti vuc‍cati, tasmā ahampi tathārūpau bhavaiyyanti| saddhañ‍ca…pai॰… sāsananti ‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇa’’ntiādinā (dha॰ pa॰ 183; dī॰ ni॰ 2.90) vuttaṃ buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ anussarantau kulaputtau vuttappabhaidaṃ saddhañ‍caiva sīlañ‍ca dhammadassanahaitukaṃ dhammai sunicchayā vimaukkhabhūtaṃ pasādañ‍ca anuyuñ‍jaiyya vaḍḍhaiyyāti|

aivaṃ thairau bhikkhūnaṃ dhammadaisanāmukhaina attani vij‍jamānai guṇai pakāsaintau añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi|

sirimittatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. mahāpanthakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

yadā  paṭhamamaddakkhintiādikā āyasmatau mahāpanthakatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai haṃsavatīnagarai vibhavasampan‍nau kuṭumbiyau hutvā aikadivasaṃ satthu santikai dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭakusalānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā sayampi taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaintau buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ pavattaitvā, ‘‘bhantai, yaṃ bhikkhuṃ tumhai itau sattadivasamatthakai – ‘sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭakusalānaṃ ayaṃ mama sāsanai aggau’ti aitadaggai ṭhapayittha, ahampi imassa adhikārakammassa balaina sau bhikkhu viya anāgatai aikassa buddhassa sāsanai aggau bhavaiyya’’nti patthanaṃ akāsi| kaniṭṭhabhātā panassa tathaiva bhagavati adhikārakammaṃ katvā manaumayassa kāyassābhinimmānaṃ caitauvivaṭṭakausal‍lanti dvin‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ vasaina vuttanayainaiva paṇidhānaṃ akāsi| bhagavā dvin‍nampi patthanaṃ anantarāyaina samijjhanabhāvaṃ disvā ‘‘anāgatai kappasatasahassamatthakai gautamassa nāma sammāsambuddhassa sāsanai tumhākaṃ patthanā samijjhissatī’’ti byākāsi|

tai  ubhaupi janā tattha yāvajīvaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā tatau cutau daivalaukai nibbattiṃsu| tattha mahāpanthakassa antarākataṃ kalyāṇadhammaṃ na kathīyati| cūḷapanthakau pana kassapassa bhagavatau sāsanai pabbajitvā vīsati vassasahassāni audātakasiṇakammaṃ katvā daivapurai nibbatti| apadānai pana ‘‘cūḷapanthakau padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai tāpasau hutvā himavantai vasantau tattha bhagavantaṃ disvā pupphacchattaina pūjaṃ akāsī’’ti āgataṃ| taisaṃ daivamanussaisu  saṃsarantānaṃyaiva kappasatasahassaṃ atik‍kantaṃ| atha amhākaṃ satthā abhisambaudhiṃ patvā pavattitavaradhammacak‍kau rājagahaṃ upanissāya vaiḷuvanai mahāvihārai viharati|

taina ca samayaina rājagahai dhanasaiṭṭhissa dhītā attanau dāsaina saddhiṃ santhavaṃ katvā ñātakaihi bhītā hatthasāraṃ gahaitvā taina saddhiṃ palāyitvā añ‍ñattha vasantī taṃ paṭic‍ca gabbhaṃ labhitvā paripak‍kagabbhā ‘‘ñātigharaṃ  gantvā vijāyissāmī’’ti gacchantī antarāmaggaiyaiva puttaṃ vijāyitvā sāminā nivattitā pubbai vasitaṭṭhānai vasantī puttassa panthai jātattā panthakauti, nāmaṃ akāsi| tasmiṃ ādhāvitvā vidhāvitvā vicaraṇakālai tamaiva paṭic‍ca dutiyaṃ gabbhaṃ paṭilabhitvā paripak‍kagabbhā pubbai vuttanayainaiva antarāmaggai puttaṃ vijāyitvā sāminā nivattitā jaiṭṭhaputtassa mahāpanthakauti kaniṭṭhassa cūḷapanthakauti nāmaṃ katvā yathāvasitaṭṭhānaiyaiva vasantī anuk‍kamaina dārakaisu vaḍḍhantaisu taihi, ‘‘amma, ayyakakulaṃ nau dassaihī’’ti nibundhiyamānā dārakai mātāpitūnaṃ santikaṃ paisaisi| tatau paṭṭhāya dārakā dhanasaiṭṭhinau gaihai vaḍḍhanti| taisu cūḷapanthakau atidaharau| mahāpanthakau pana ayyakaina saddhiṃ bhagavatau santikaṃ gatau satthāraṃ disvā saha dassanaina paṭiladdhasaddhau dhammaṃ sutvā upanissayasampan‍natāya pabbajitukāmau hutvā pitāmahaṃ āpucchi| sau satthu tamatthaṃ āraucaitvā taṃ pabbājaisi| sau pabbajitvā bahuṃ buddhavacanaṃ uggaṇhitvā paripuṇṇavassau upasampaj‍jitvā yaunisaumanasikārai kammaṃ karauntau visaisatau catun‍naṃ arūpajjhānānaṃ lābhī hutvā tatau vuṭṭhāya vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| iti sau sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭakusalānaṃ aggau jātau| sau jhānasukhaina phalasukhaina vītināmaintau aikadivasaṃ attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā adhigatasampattiṃ paṭic‍ca sañ‍jātasaumanassau sīhanādaṃ nadantau –

510.

 ‘‘yadā paṭhamamaddakkhiṃ, satthāramakutaubhayaṃ|

tatau mai ahu saṃvaigau, passitvā purisuttamaṃ||

511.

 ‘‘siriṃ hatthaihi pādaihi, yau paṇāmaiyya āgataṃ|

aitādisaṃ sau satthāraṃ, ārādhaitvā virādhayai||

512.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ  puttadārañ‍ca, dhanadhañ‍ñañ‍ca chaḍḍayiṃ|

kaisamassūni chaidaitvā, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

513.

 ‘‘sikkhāsājīvasampan‍nau, indriyaisu susaṃvutau|

namassamānau sambuddhaṃ, vihāsiṃ aparājitau||

514.

 ‘‘tatau  mai paṇidhī āsi, caitasau abhipatthitau|

na nisīdai muhuttampi, taṇhāsal‍lai anūhatai||

515.

 ‘‘tassa maivaṃ viharatau, passa vīriyaparak‍kamaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

516.

 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ jānāmi, dibbacakkhu visaudhitaṃ|

arahā dakkhiṇaiyyaumhi, vippamuttau nirūpadhi||

517.

 ‘‘tatau ratyāvivasānai, sūriyuggamanaṃ pati|

sabbaṃ taṇhaṃ visausaitvā, pal‍laṅkaina upāvisi’’nti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha yadāti yasmiṃ kālai| paṭhamanti āditau| addakkhinti passiṃ, satthāranti, bhagavantaṃ| akutaubhayanti nibbhayaṃ| ayañhaittha atthau – sabbaisaṃ bhayahaitūnaṃ baudhimūlaiyaiva pahīnattā kutaucipi bhayābhāvatau akutaubhayaṃ nibbhayaṃ, catuvaisāraj‍javisāradaṃ diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaparamatthaihi vainaiyyānaṃ yathārahamanusāsanatau satthāraṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ mayhaṃ pitāmahaina saddhiṃ gantvā yāya vailāya sabbapaṭhamaṃ passiṃ, taṃ purisuttamaṃ sadaivakai laukai aggapuggalaṃ passitvā tatau dassanahaitu tatau dassanatau pacchā ‘‘aittakaṃ kālaṃ satthāraṃ daṭṭhuṃ dhammañ‍ca sautuṃ nālattha’’nti mayhaṃ saṃvaigau ahu sahauttappaṃ ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji| uppan‍nasaṃvaigau panāhaṃ aivaṃ cintaisinti dassaiti siriṃ hatthaihīti gāthāya| tassatthau – yau vibhavatthikau purisau ‘‘upaṭṭhāyikau hutvā tava santikai vasissāmī’’ti saviggahaṃ siriṃ sayanai upagataṃ hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca kauṭṭaintau paṇāmaiyya nīharaiyya, sau tathārūpau alakkhikapurisau aitādisaṃ satthāraṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ ārādhaitvā imasmiṃ navamai khaṇai paṭilabhitvā virādhayai tassa auvādākaraṇaina taṃ virajjhaiyya, ahaṃ panaivaṃ na karaumīti adhippāyau| taināha ‘‘tadāhaṃ…pai॰… anagāriya’’nti| tattha chaḍḍayinti, pajahiṃ| ‘‘chaḍḍiya’’ntipi pāṭhau| nanu ayaṃ thairau dārapariggahaṃ akatvāva pabbajitau, sau kasmā ‘‘puttadārañ‍ca  chaḍḍayi’’nti avaucāti? yathā nāma purisau anibbattaphalamaiva rukkhaṃ chindantau acchin‍nai tatau laddhaphalaihi parihīnau nāma hauti| aivaṃsampadamidaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nauti yā adhisīlasikkhā, tāya ca, yattha bhikkhū saha jīvanti, aikajīvikā  sabhāgavuttinau haunti, taina bhagavatā pañ‍ñattasikkhāpadasaṅkhātaina sājīvaina ca saman‍nāgatau sikkhanabhāvaina  samaṅgībhūtau, sikkhaṃ paripūraintau sājīvañ‍ca avītik‍kamantau hutvā tadubhayaṃ sampādaintauti atthau| taina suvisuddhai pātimaukkhai sīlai patiṭṭhitabhāvaṃ dassaiti| indriyaisu susaṃvutauti manacchaṭṭhaisu indriyaisu suṭṭhu saṃvutau| rūpādivisayaisu uppaj‍janakānaṃ abhijjhādīnaṃ pavattinivāraṇavasaina satikavāṭaina supihitacakkhādidvārauti atthau| aivaṃ pātimaukkhasaṃvaraindriyasaṃvarasīlasampattidassanaina itarasīlampi atthatau dassitamaiva hautīti thairau attanau catupārisuddhisīlasampadaṃ dassaitvā ‘‘namassamānau sambuddha’’nti iminā buddhānussatibhāvanānuyaugamāha| vihāsiṃ aparājitauti kilaisamārādīhi aparājitau aiva hutvā vihariṃ, yāva arahattappatti, tāva taihi anabhibhūtau, añ‍ñadatthu tai abhibhavantau aiva vihāsinti atthau|

tatauti tasmā, yasmā suvisuddhasīlau satthari abhippasan‍nau kilaisābhibhavanapaṭipattiyañ‍ca ṭhitau, tasmā| paṇidhīti paṇidhānaṃ| tatau vā cittābhinīhārau| āsīti ahausi| caitasau abhipatthitauti, mama cittaina icchitau| kīdisau pana sauti āha ‘‘na nisīdai muhuttampi, taṇhāsal‍lai anūhatai’’ti| ‘‘aggamaggasaṇḍāsaina mama hadayatau taṇhāsal‍lai anuddhaṭai muhuttampi na nisīdai, nisaj‍jaṃ na kappaiyya’’nti aivaṃ mai cittābhinīhārau ahausīti atthau|

aivaṃ pana cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāya bhāvanaṃ bhāvayitvā ṭhānacaṅkamaihaiva rattiṃ vītināmaintau arūpasamāpattitau vuṭṭhāya jhānaṅgamukhaina vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā arahattaṃ sacchākāsi| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘tassa mai’’tiādi| nirūpadhīti kilaisupadhiādīnaṃ abhāvaina nirupadhi| ratyāvivasānaiti rattibhāgassa vigamanai  vibhātāya rattiyā| sūriyuggamanaṃ patīti sūriyuggamanaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ katvā| sabbaṃ taṇhanti kāmataṇhādibhaidaṃ sabbaṃ taṇhāsautaṃ aggamaggaina visausaitvā sukkhāpaitvā ‘‘taṇhāsal‍lai anūhatai na nisīdai’’ti, paṭiñ‍ñāya maucitattā| pal‍laṅkaina upāvisinti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā nisīdinti| saisaṃ uttānatthamaiva|

mahāpanthakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

aṭṭhakanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9. navakanipātau

1. bhūtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

navakanipātai  yadā dukkhantiādikā āyasmatau bhūtatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau siddhatthassa bhagavatau kālai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā  sainauti laddhanāmau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau ‘‘usabhaṃ pavara’’ntiādinā catūhi gāthāhi abhitthavi|

sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sākaitanagarassa dvāragāmai mahāvibhavassa saiṭṭhissa puttau hutvā nibbatti| tassa kira saiṭṭhinau jātā jātā dārakā baddhāghātaina aikaina yakkhaina khāditā, imassa pana pacchimabhavikattā bhūtā ārakkhaṃ gaṇhiṃsu| yakkhau pana vaissavaṇassa upaṭṭhānaṃ gatau, puna nāgamāsi| nāmakaraṇadivasai cassa ‘‘aivaṃ katai amanussā anukampantā pariharaiyyu’’nti bhūtauti nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau pana attanau puñ‍ñabalaina anantarāyau vaḍḍhi, tassa ‘‘tayau pāsādā ahaisu’’ntiādi sabbaṃ yasassa kulaputtassa vibhavakittanai viya vaiditabbaṃ| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthari sākaitai vasantai upāsakaihi saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gatau| satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā ajakaraṇiyā nāma nadiyā tīrai laiṇai vasantau vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.7.20-28) –

‘‘usabhaṃ pavaraṃ vīraṃ, mahaisiṃ vijitāvinaṃ|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, kau disvā nappasīdati||

‘‘himavā vāparimaiyyau, sāgarauva duruttarau|

tathaiva jhānaṃ buddhassa, kau disvā nappasīdati||

‘‘vasudhā  yathāppamaiyyā, cittā vanavaṭaṃsakā|

tathaiva sīlaṃ buddhassa, kau disvā nappasīdati||

‘‘anilañ‍jasāsaṅkhubbhau, yathākāsau asaṅkhiyau|

tathaiva ñāṇaṃ buddhassa, kau disvā nappasīdati||

‘‘imāhi catugāthāhi, brāhmaṇau sainasavhayau|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ thavitvāna, siddhatthaṃ aparājitaṃ||

‘‘catun‍navutikappāni, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jatha|

sugatiṃ sukhasampattiṃ, anubhausimanappakaṃ||

‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, thavitvā laukanāyakaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thaumanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘cātuddasamhi kappamhi, caturau āsumuggatā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā aparaina samayaina ñātīnaṃ anukampāya sākaitaṃ gantvā katipāhaṃ taihi upaṭṭhiyamānau añ‍janavanai vasitvā puna attanā vasitaṭṭhānamaiva gantukāmau gamanākāraṃ dassaisi| ñātakā ‘‘idhaiva, bhantai, vasatha, tumhaipi na kilamissatha, mayampi puñ‍ñaina vaḍḍhissāmā’’ti thairaṃ yāciṃsu| thairau attanau vivaikābhiratiṃ tattha ca phāsuvihāraṃ pakāsaintau –

518.

 ‘‘yadā dukkhaṃ jarāmaraṇanti paṇḍitau, aviddasū yattha sitā puthuj‍janā|

dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñāya satauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

519.

 ‘‘yadā dukkhassāvahaniṃ visattikaṃ, papañ‍casaṅghātadukhādhivāhiniṃ|

taṇhaṃ pahantvāna satauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

520.

 ‘‘yadā  sivaṃ dvaicaturaṅgagāminaṃ, magguttamaṃ sabbakilaisasaudhanaṃ|

pañ‍ñāya passitva satauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

521.

 ‘‘yadā  asaukaṃ virajaṃ asaṅkhataṃ, santaṃ padaṃ sabbakilaisasaudhanaṃ|

bhāvaiti sañ‍ñaujanabandhanacchidaṃ, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

522.

 ‘‘yadā nabhai gaj‍jati maighadundubhi, dhārākulā vihagapathai samantatau|

bhikkhū ca pabbhāragatauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

523.

 ‘‘yadā  nadīnaṃ kusumākulānaṃ, vicitta-vānaiyya-vaṭaṃsakānaṃ|

tīrai nisin‍nau sumanauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

524.

 ‘‘yadā nisīthai rahitamhi kānanai, daivai gaḷantamhi nadanti dāṭhinau|

bhikkhū ca pabbhāragatauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

525.

 ‘‘yadā vitak‍kai uparundhiyattanau, nagantarai nagavivaraṃ samassitau|

vītaddarau vītakhilauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

526.

 ‘‘yadā sukhī malakhilasaukanāsanau,

niraggaḷau nibbanathau visal‍lau|

sabbāsavai byantikatauva jhāyati,

tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindatī’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tatthāyaṃ  padayaujanāmukhaina paṭhamagāthāya atthavaṇṇanā – khandhānaṃ paripākau jarā| bhaidau maraṇaṃ| jarāmaraṇasīsaina caittha jarāmaraṇavantau dhammā gahitā| ‘‘tayidaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkha’’nti aviddasū yathābhūtaṃ ajānantā puthuj‍janā yattha yasmiṃ upādānakkhandhapañ‍cakai sitā paṭibandhā al‍līnā, taṃ ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ, aittakaṃ dukkhaṃ, na itau bhiyyau’’ti vipassanāpañ‍ñāsahitāya maggapañ‍ñāya parijānitvā, idha imasmiṃ sāsanai satau sampajānau, paṇḍitau bhikkhu, yadā yasmiṃ kālai lakkhaṇūpanijjhānaina jhāyati| tatau vipassanāratitau maggaphalaratitau ca paramataraṃ uttamataraṃ ratiṃ na vindati nappaṭilabhati| taināha bhagavā –

‘‘yatau yatau sammasati, khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ|

labhatī pītipāmauj‍jaṃ, amataṃ taṃ vijānataṃ||

‘‘paṭhabyā aikaraj‍jaina, saggassa gamanaina vā|

sabbalaukādhipac‍caina, sautāpattiphalaṃ vara’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 374, 178)|

aivaṃ  pariñ‍ñābhisamayamukhaina vivaikaratiṃ dassaitvā idāni pahānābhisamayādimukhainapi taṃ dassaituṃ dutiyādikā tissau gāthā abhāsi| tattha dukkhassāvahaninti dukkhassa āyatiṃ pavattiṃ, dukkhassa nipphattikanti atthau| visattikanti taṇhaṃ| sā hi visatāti visattikā, visālāti visattikā, visaṭāti visattikā, visak‍katīti visattikā, visaṃ haratīti visattikā, visaṃvādikāti visattikā, visamūlāti visattikā, visaphalāti visattikā, visaparibhaugāti visattikā, visālā vā pana sā taṇhā rūpai saddai gandhai rasai phauṭṭhabbai dhammai kulai  gaṇai vitthaṭāti visattikāti vuc‍cati| papañ‍casaṅghātadukhādhivāhininti sattasantānaṃ saṃsārai papañ‍cainti vitthāraintīti papañ‍cā, rāgādayau mānādayau ca| tai aiva pavattidukkhassa saṅghātaṭṭhaina saṅghātā, sadarathapariḷāhasabhāvattā dukkhañ‍cāti papañ‍casaṅghātadukhaṃ, tassa adhivāhatau nibbattanatau papañ‍casaṅghātadukhādhivāhinī| taṃ taṇhaṃ pahantvānāti ariyamaggaina samucchinditvā|

sivanti  khaimaṃ, akhaimakarānaṃ kilaisānaṃ samucchindanaina taihi anupaddutanti atthau| sammādiṭṭhiādīnaṃ vasaina dvicaturaṅgau hutvā ariyai nibbānaṃ gamaitīti dvaicaturaṅgagāminaṃ, gāthāsukhatthañ‍caittha vibhattialaupau katauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| rūpūpapattimaggādīsu sabbaisu maggaisu uttamattā magguttamaṃ| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘maggānaṭṭhaṅgikau saiṭṭhau’’tiādi (dha॰ pa॰ 273)| sabbaihi kilaisamalaihi sattānaṃ saudhanatau sabbakilaisasaudhanaṃ| pañ‍ñāya passitvāti paṭivaidhapañ‍ñāya bhāvanābhisamayavasaina abhisamaic‍ca|

saukahaitūnaṃ abhāvatau puggalassa ca saukābhāvahaitutau natthi aittha saukauti asaukaṃ| tathā vigatarāgādirajattā virajaṃ| na kainaci pac‍cayaina saṅkhatanti asaṅkhataṃ| sabbaisaṃ kilaisānaṃ sabbassa ca dukkhassa vūpasamabhāvatau, saṃsāradukkhadditaihi paj‍jitabbatau adhigantabbatau ca santaṃ padaṃ| sabbaihi kilaisamalaihi sattasantānassa saudhananimittatau sabbakilaisasaudhanaṃ| bhāvaitīti sacchikiriyābhisamayavasaina abhisamaiti| bahukkhattuñhi nibbānaṃ ārabbha sacchikiriyābhisamayaṃ pavattaintassa ālambakai labbhamānavisaisakaṃ ālambitabbai āraupaitvā aivaṃ vuttaṃ| saṃyaujanasaṅkhātānaṃ bandhanānaṃ chaidanatau saṃyaujanabandhanacchidaṃ| nimittañhaittha kattubhāvaina upacāritaṃ, yathā ariyabhāvakarāni sac‍cāni ariyasac‍cānīti| yathā purimagāthāsu yadā jhāyati, tadā  tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindatīti yaujanā| aivaṃ idha yadā bhāvaiti, tadā tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindatīti yaujanā|

aivaṃ thairau catūhi gāthāhi attānaṃ anupanaitvāva catusac‍capaṭivaidhakittanaina añ‍ñaṃ byākaritvā idāni attanā vasitaṭṭhānassa vivittabhāvaina phāsutaṃ dassaintau ‘‘yadā nabhai’’tiādikā gāthā abhāsi| tattha nabhaiti ākāsai| siniddhagambhīranigghausatāya maighauyaiva dundubhi maighadundubhi| samantatau paggharantīhi dhārāhi ākulāti dhārākulā| vihagānaṃ pakkhīnaṃ gamanamaggattā vihagapathai nabhaiti yaujanā| tatauti jhānaratitau|

kusumākulānanti  tarūhi gaḷitakusumaihi samauhitānaṃ| vicittavānaiyyavaṭaṃsakānanti vanai jātattā  vānaiyyāni vanapupphāni, vicittāni vānaiyyāni vaṭaṃsakāni aitāsanti vicittavānaiyyavaṭaṃsakā nadiyau, tāsaṃ nānāvidhavanapupphavaṭaṃsakānanti atthau| uttarimanussadhammavasaina sundarau manau aitassāti sumanau jhāyati|

nisīthaiti rattiyaṃ| rahitamhīti, janasambādhavirahitai vivittai| daivaiti maighai| gaḷantamhīti vuṭṭhidhārāyau paggharantai vassantai| dāṭhinauti sīhabyagghādayau paṭipakkhasattā| tai hi dāṭhāvudhāti ‘‘dāṭhinau’’ti vuc‍canti, nadanti dāṭhinauti idampi janavivaikadassanatthamaiva gahitaṃ|

vitak‍kai uparundhiyattanauti attasantānapariyāpan‍natāya attanau kāmavitak‍kādikai micchāvitak‍kai paṭipakkhabalaina nisaidhaitvā| attanauti vā idaṃ vindatīti iminā yaujaitabbaṃ ‘‘tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ attanā na vindatī’’ti| nagantaraiti pabbatantarai| nagavivaranti pabbataguhaṃ pabbhāraṃ vā| samassitauti nissitau upagatau| vītaddarauti vigatakilaisadarathau| vītakhilauti pahīnacaitaukhilau|

sukhīti jhānādisukhaina sukhitau| malakhilasaukanāsanauti rāgādīnaṃ malānaṃ pañ‍can‍nañ‍ca caitaukhilānaṃ ñātiviyaugādihaitukassa saukassa ca pahāyakau| niraggaḷauti, aggaḷaṃ vuc‍cati avij‍jā nibbānapurapavaisanivāraṇatau, tadabhāvatau niraggaḷau| nibbanathauti nitaṇhau| visal‍lauti, vigatarāgādisal‍lau| sabbāsavaiti, kāmāsavādikai sabbaipi āsavai| byantikatauti byantikatāvī ariyamaggaina vigatantai katvā ṭhitau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthaṃ yadā jhāyati, tatau jhānaratitau paramataraṃ ratiṃ na vindatīti yaujanā| aivaṃ pana vatvā thairau ajakaraṇītīramaiva gatau|

bhūtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

navakanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

10. dasakanipātau

1. kāḷudāyitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

dasakanipātai  aṅgārinautiādikā āyasmatau kāḷudāyitthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi padumuttarabuddhassa kālai haṃsavatīnagarai kulagaihai nibbattau satthu dhammadaisanaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ kulappasādakānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā taj‍jaṃ abhinīhārakammaṃ katvā taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaisi|

sau yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau amhākaṃ baudhisattassa mātukucchiyaṃ paṭisandhiggahaṇadivasai kapilavatthusmiṃyaiva amac‍cagaihai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| baudhisattaina saddhiṃ aikadivasaṃyaiva jātauti taṃdivasaṃyaiva naṃ dukūlacumbaṭai nipaj‍jāpaitvā baudhisattassa upaṭṭhānaṃ nayiṃsu| baudhisattaina hi saddhiṃ baudhirukkhau, rāhulamātā, cattārau nidhī, ārauhaniyahatthī, assakaṇḍakau, chan‍nau kāḷudāyīti imai satta aikadivasaṃyaiva jātattā sahajātā nāma ahaisuṃ| athassa nāmaggahaṇadivasai sakalanagarassa udaggacittadivasai jātattā udāyītvaiva nāmaṃ akaṃsu, thaukaṃ kāḷadhātukattā pana kāḷudāyīti pañ‍ñāyittha| sau baudhisattaina saddhiṃ kumārakīḷaṃ kīḷantau vuddhiṃ agamāsi|

aparabhāgai laukanāthai mahābhinikkhamanaṃ nikkhamitvā anuk‍kamaina sabbañ‍ñutaṃ patvā pavattitavaradhammacak‍kai rājagahaṃ upanissāya vaiḷuvanai viharantai suddhaudanamahārājā taṃ pavattiṃ sutvā purisasahassaparivāraṃ aikaṃ amac‍caṃ ‘‘puttaṃ mai idhānaihī’’ti paisaisi| sau dhammadaisanāvailāya satthu santikaṃ gantvā parisapariyantai ṭhitau dhammaṃ sutvā saparisau arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| atha nai satthā ‘‘aitha, bhikkhavau’’ti hatthaṃ pasāraiti| sabbai taṅkhaṇaṃyaiva iddhimayapattacīvaradharā vassasaṭṭhikatthairā viya ahaisuṃ| arahattaṃ pattatau paṭṭhāya pana ariyā majjhattāva haunti, tasmā rañ‍ñā pahitasāsanaṃ dasabalassa na kathaisi| rājā ‘‘naiva gatabalakauṭṭhakau āgacchati, na sāsanaṃ suyyatī’’ti aparampi amac‍caṃ purisasahassaina  paisaisi| tasmimpi tathā paṭipan‍nai aparanti aivaṃ navahi amac‍caihi saddhiṃ nava purisasahassāni paisaisi sabbai arahattaṃ patvā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ|

atha rājā cintaisi – ‘‘aittakā janā mayi sinaihābhāvaina dasabalassa idhāgamanatthāya na kiñ‍ci kathayiṃsu, ayaṃ khau pana udāyī dasabalaina samavayau sahapaṃsukīḷikau, mayi ca sinaihavā, imaṃ paisaissāmī’’ti taṃ pak‍kausāpaitvā, ‘‘tāta, tvaṃ purisasahassaparivārau rājagahaṃ gantvā dasabalaṃ ānaihī’’ti vatvā paisaisi| sau pana gacchantau ‘‘sacāhaṃ, daiva, pabbajituṃ labhissāmi, aivāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ idhānaissāmī’’ti  vatvā ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci katvā mama puttaṃ dassaihī’’ti vuttau rājagahaṃ gantvā satthu dhammadaisanāvailāya parisapariyantai ṭhitau dhammaṃ sutvā saparivārau arahattaṃ patvā aihibhikkhubhāvai patiṭṭhāsi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.4.48-63) –

‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

addhānaṃ paṭipan‍nassa, caratau cārikaṃ tadā||

‘‘suphul‍laṃ padumaṃ gayha, uppalaṃ mal‍likañ‍cahaṃ|

paraman‍naṃ gahaitvāna, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

‘‘paribhuñ‍ji mahāvīrau, paraman‍naṃ subhaujanaṃ|

tañ‍ca pupphaṃ gahaitvāna, janassa sampadassayi||

‘‘iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ piyaṃ laukai, jalajaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ|

suduk‍karaṃ kataṃ taina, yau mai pupphaṃ adāsidaṃ||

‘‘yau pupphamabhiraupaisi, paraman‍nañ‍cadāsi mai|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘dasa aṭṭha cakkhattuṃ sau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

uppalaṃ padumañ‍cāpi, mal‍likañ‍ca taduttari||

‘‘assa puñ‍ñavipākaina, dibbagandhasamāyutaṃ|

ākāsai chadanaṃ katvā, dhārayissati tāvadai||

‘‘pañ‍cavīsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ pañ‍casataṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati||

‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘sakakammābhiraddhau  sau, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

sakyānaṃ nandijananau, ñātibandhu bhavissati||

‘‘sau pacchā pabbajitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

‘‘paṭisambhidamanuppattaṃ, katakic‍camanāsavaṃ|

gautamau laukabandhu taṃ, aitadaggai ṭhapaissati||

‘‘padhānapahitattau sau, upasantau nirūpadhi|

udāyī nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

‘‘rāgau dausau ca mauhau ca, mānau makkhau ca dhaṃsitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

‘‘tausayiñ‍cāpi sambuddhaṃ, ātāpī nipakau ahaṃ|

pasāditau ca sambuddhau, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā ‘‘na tāvāyaṃ dasabalassa kulanagaraṃ gantuṃ kālau, vasantai pana upagatai pupphitaisu vanasaṇḍaisu haritatiṇasañchan‍nāya bhūmiyā gamanakālau bhavissatī’’ti kālaṃ paṭimānaintau vasantai sampattai satthu kulanagaraṃ gantuṃ gamanamaggavaṇṇaṃ saṃvaṇṇaintau –

527.

 ‘‘aṅgārinau dāni dumā bhadantai, phalaisinau chadanaṃ vippahāya|

tai ac‍cimantauva pabhāsayanti, samayau mahāvīra bhāgīrasānaṃ||

528.

 ‘‘dumāni  phul‍lāni manauramāni, samantatau sabbadisā pavanti|

pattaṃ pahāya phalamāsasānā, kālau itau pak‍kamanāya vīra||

529.

 ‘‘naivātisītaṃ na panātiuṇhaṃ, sukhā utu addhaniyā bhadantai|

passantu taṃ sākiyā kauḷiyā ca, pacchāmukhaṃ rauhiniyaṃ tarantaṃ||

530.

 ‘‘āsāya kasatai khaittaṃ, bījaṃ āsāya vappati|

āsāya vāṇijā yanti, samuddaṃ dhanahārakā|

yāya āsāya tiṭṭhāmi, sā mai āsā samijjhatu||

531.

 ‘‘punappunañ‍caiva vapanti bījaṃ, punappunaṃ vassati daivarājā|

punappunaṃ khaittaṃ kasanti kassakā, punappunaṃ dhañ‍ñamupaiti raṭṭhaṃ||

532.

 ‘‘punappunaṃ yācanakā caranti, punappunaṃ dānapatī dadanti|

punappunaṃ dānapatī daditvā, punappunaṃ saggamupainti ṭhānaṃ||

533.

 ‘‘vīrau  havai sattayugaṃ punaiti, yasmiṃ kulai jāyati bhūripañ‍ñau|

mañ‍ñāmahaṃ sak‍kati daivadaivau, tayā hi jātau muni sac‍canāmau||

534.

 ‘‘suddhaudanau nāma pitā mahaisinau, buddhassa mātā pana māyanāmā|

yā baudhisattaṃ parihariya kucchinā, kāyassa bhaidā tidivamhi maudati||

535.

 ‘‘sā gautamī kālakatā itau cutā, dibbaihi kāmaihi samaṅgibhūtā|

sā maudati kāmaguṇaihi pañ‍cahi, parivāritā daivagaṇaihi taihi||

536.

 ‘‘buddhassa  puttaumhi asayhasāhinau, aṅgīrasassappaṭimassa tādinau|

pitupitā mayhaṃ tuvaṃsi sak‍ka, dhammaina mai gautama ayyakausī’’ti|| –

imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha aṅgārinauti aṅgārāni viyāti aṅgārāni, rattapavāḷavaṇṇāni rukkhānaṃ pupphapal‍lavāni, tāni aitaisaṃ santīti aṅgārinau, atilauhitakusumakisalayaihi aṅgāravuṭṭhisaṃparikiṇṇā viyāti atthau| idānīti imasmiṃ kālai| dumāti rukkhā| bhadantaiti, bhaddaṃ antai aitassāti bhadantaiti aikassa dakārassa laupaṃ katvā vuc‍cati, guṇavisaisayuttau, guṇavisaisayuttānañ‍ca aggabhūtau satthā| tasmā bhadantaiti satthu ālapanaṃ| pac‍cattavacanañ‍caitaṃ aikārantaṃ ‘‘sukaṭai paṭikammai sukhai dukkhaipi cai’’tiādīsu viya| idha pana sambaudhanatthai daṭṭhabbaṃ| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘bhadantaiti ālapana’’nti| ‘‘bhaddasaddasamānatthaṃ padantaramaika’’nti kaici| phalāni aisantīti phalaisinau| acaitanaipi hi sacaitanakiriyamāraupaitvā vauharanti, yathā kulaṃ patitukāmanti, phalāni gahaitumāraddhā sampattiphalagahaṇakālāti atthau| chadanaṃ vippahāyāti purāṇapaṇṇāni pajahitvā sampan‍napaṇḍupalāsāti atthau| taiti dumā| ac‍cimantauva pabhāsayantīti dīpasikhāvantau viya jalitaaggī viya vā aubhāsayanti sabbā disāti adhippāyau| samayauti kālau, ‘‘anuggahāyā’’ti vacanasaisau| mahāvīrāti mahāvik‍kanta| bhāgī rasānanti attharasādīnaṃ bhāgī| vuttañhaitaṃ dhammasaināpatinā – ‘‘bhāgī vā bhagavā attharasassa dhammarasassā’’tiādi (cūḷani॰ ajitamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 2)| mahāvīra, bhāgīti ca idampi dvayaṃ sambaudhanavacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| bhāgīrathānanti pana pāṭhai bhagīrathau nāma  ādirājā| tabbaṃsajātatāya sākiyā bhāgīrathā, taisaṃ bhāgīrathānaṃ upakāratthanti adhippāyau|

dumānīti liṅgavipal‍lāsaina vuttaṃ, dumā rukkhāti atthau| samantatau sabbadisā pavantīti, samantatau sabbabhāgatau sabbadisāsu ca phul‍lāni, tathā phul‍lattā aiva sabbadisā pavanti gandhaṃ vissaj‍jainti| āsamānāti āsīsantā  gahitukāmā| aivaṃ rukkhasaubhāya gamanamaggassa  rāmaṇaiyyataṃ dassaitvā idāni ‘‘naivātisīta’’ntiādinā utusampattiṃ dassaiti| sukhāti nātisītanātiuṇhabhāvainaiva sukhā iṭṭhā| utu addhaniyāti addhānagamanayauggā utu| passantu taṃ sākiyā kauḷiyā ca, pacchāmukhaṃ rauhiniyaṃ tarantanti rauhinī nāma nadī sākiyakauḷiyajanapadānaṃ antarai uttaradisatau dakkhiṇamukhā sandati, rājagahaṃ cassā puratthimadakkhiṇāya disāya, tasmā rājagahatau kapilavatthuṃ gantuṃ taṃ nadiṃ tarantā pacchāmukhā hutvā taranti| taināha ‘‘passantu taṃ…pai॰… taranta’’nti| ‘‘bhagavantaṃ pacchāmukhaṃ rauhiniṃ nāma nadiṃ atik‍kamantaṃ sākiyakauḷiyajanapadavāsinau passantū’’ti kapilavatthugamanāya bhagavantaṃ āyācantau ussāhaiti|

idāni attanau patthanaṃ upamāhi pakāsaintau ‘‘āsāya kasatai’’ti gāthamāha| āsāya kasatai khaittanti kassakau kasantau khaittaṃ phalāsāya kasati| bījaṃ āsāya vappatīti kasitvā ca vapantaina phalāsāya aiva bījaṃ vappati nikkhipīyati| āsāya vāṇijā yantīti dhanahārakā vāṇijā dhanāsāya samuddaṃ tarituṃ daisaṃ upagantuṃ samuddaṃ nāvāya yanti gacchanti| yāya āsāya tiṭṭhāmīti aivaṃ ahampi yāya āsāya patthanāya bhagavā tumhākaṃ kapilapuragamanapatthanāya idha tiṭṭhāmi| sā mai āsā samijjhatu, tumhaihi ‘‘kapilavatthu gantabba’’nti vadati, āsāya sadisatāya caittha kattukamyatāchandaṃ āsāti āha|

gamanamaggasaṃvaṇṇanādinā anaikavāraṃ yācanāya kāraṇaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘punappuna’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tassatthau – sakiṃ vuttamattaina vappai asampaj‍jamānai kassakā punappunaṃ dutiyampi tatiyampi bījaṃ vapanti| paj‍jun‍nau daivarājāpi aikavāramaiva avassitvā punappunaṃ kālaina kālaṃ vassati| kassakāpi aikavāramaiva akasitvā sassasampattiatthaṃ paṃsuṃ kaddamaṃ vā muduṃ kātuṃ khaittaṃ punappunaṃ kasanti| aikavāramaiva dhañ‍ñaṃ saṅgahaṃ katvā  ‘‘alamaittāvatā’’ti aparitussanatau kauṭṭhāgārādīsu paṭisāmanavasaina manussaihi upanīyamānaṃ punappunaṃ sāliādidhañ‍ñaṃ raṭṭhaṃ upaiti upagacchati|

yācanakāpi  yācantā punappunaṃ kulāni caranti upagacchanti, na aikavāramaiva, yācitā pana taisaṃ punappunaṃ dānapatī dadanti, na sakiṃyaiva| tathā pana daiyyadhammaṃ punappunaṃ dānapatī daditvā dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinitvā punappunaṃ aparāparaṃ saggamupainti ṭhānaṃ paṭisandhivasaina daivalaukaṃ upagacchanti| tasmā ahampi punappunaṃ yācāmi bhagavā mayhaṃ manaurathaṃ matthakaṃ pāpaihīti adhippāyau|

idāni  yadatthaṃ satthāraṃ kapilavatthugamanaṃ yācati, taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘vīrau havai’’tigāthamāha| tassatthau – vīrau vīriyavā mahāvik‍kantau bhūripañ‍ñau mahāpañ‍ñau purisau yasmiṃ kulai jāyati nibbattati, tattha havai aikaṃsaina sattayugaṃ sattapurisayugaṃ yāvasattamaṃ pitāmahayugaṃ sammāpaṭipattiyā punaiti saudhaitīti laukavādau ativādau añ‍ñaisu| bhagavā pana sabbaisaṃ daivānaṃ uttamadaivatāya daivadaivau pāpanivāraṇaina kalyāṇapatiṭṭhāpanaina tatau parampi saudhaituṃ sak‍kati sak‍kautīti mañ‍ñāmi ahaṃ| kasmā? tayā hi jātau muni sac‍canāmau yasmā tayā satthārā ariyāya jātiyā jātau munibhāvau, muni vā samānau attahitaparahitānaṃ idhalaukaparalaukānañ‍ca munanaṭṭhaina ‘‘munī’’ti avitathanāmau, maunavā vā muni, ‘‘samaṇau pabbajitau isī’’ti avitathanāmau tayā jātau| tasmā sattānaṃ aikantahitapaṭilābhahaitubhāvatau bhagavā tava tattha gamanaṃ yācāmāti atthau|

idāni ‘‘sattayuga’’nti vuttai pituyugaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘suddhaudanau nāmā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| suddhaṃ audanaṃ jīvanaṃ aitassāti suddhaudanau| buddhapitā hi aikaṃsatau suvisuddhakāyavacīmanausamācārau suvisuddhājīvau hauti tathā abhinīhārasampan‍nattā| māyanāmāti kularūpasīlācārādisampattiyā ñātimittādīhi ‘‘mā yāhī’’ti vattabbaguṇatāya ‘‘māyā’’ti laddhanāmā| parihariyāti dhāraitvā| kāyassa bhaidāti sadaivakassa laukassa caitiyasadisassa attanau kāyassa vināsatau uddhaṃ| tidivamhīti tusitadaivalaukai|

sāti māyādaivī| gautamīti gauttaina taṃ kittaiti| dibbaihi kāmaihīti, tusitabhavanapariyāpan‍naihi dibbaihi vatthukāmaihi| samaṅgibhūtāti saman‍nāgatā| kāmaguṇaihīti kāmakauṭṭhāsaihi, ‘‘kāmaihī’’ti vatvā  puna ‘‘kāmaguṇaihī’’ti vacanaina anaikabhāgaihi vatthukāmaihi paricāriyatīti dīpaiti| taihīti yasmiṃ daivanikāyai nibbatti, taihi tusitadaivagaṇaihi, taihi vā kāmaguṇaihi| ‘‘samaṅgibhūtā parivāritā’’ti ca  itthiliṅganiddaisau purimattabhāvasiddhaṃ itthibhāvaṃ, daivatābhāvaṃ vā sandhāya katau, daivūpapatti pana purisabhāvainaiva jātā|

aivaṃ thairaina yācitau bhagavā tattha gamanai bahūnaṃ visaisādhigamaṃ disvā vīsatisahassa khīṇāsavaparivutau rājagahatau aturitacārikāvasaina kapilavatthugāmimaggaṃ paṭipaj‍ji| thairau iddhiyā kapilavatthuṃ gantvā rañ‍ñau puratau ākāsai ṭhitau adiṭṭhapubbaṃ vaisaṃ disvā rañ‍ñā ‘‘kausi  tva’’nti pucchitau, ‘‘sacai amac‍caputtaṃ tayā bhagavatau santikaṃ paisitaṃ maṃ na jānāsi, aivaṃ pana jānāhī’’ti dassaintau –

‘‘buddhassa puttaumhi asayhasāhinau, aṅgīrasassappaṭimassa tādinau|

pitupitā mayhaṃ tuvaṃsi sak‍ka, dhammaina mai gautama ayyakausī’’ti|| –

ausānagāthamāha|

tattha buddhassa puttaumhīti, sabbañ‍ñubuddhassa urai jātatāya aurasaputtau amhi| asayhasāhinauti, abhisambaudhitau pubbai ṭhapaitvā mahābaudhisattaṃ añ‍ñaihi sahituṃ vahituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyattā asayhassa sakalassa baudhisambhārassa mahākāruṇikādhikārassa ca sahanatau vahanatau, tatau parampi añ‍ñaihi sahituṃ abhibhavituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyattā asayhānaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ mārānaṃ sahanatau abhibhavanatau, āsayānusayacaritādhimuttiādivibhāgāvabaudhanaina yathārahaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaparamatthaihi anusāsanīsaṅkhātassa añ‍ñaihi asayhassa buddhakic‍cassa ca sahanatau, tattha vā sādhukārībhāvatau asayhasāhinau| aṅgīrasassāti aṅgīkatasīlādisampattikassa| ‘‘aṅgamaṅgaihi niccharaṇakaaubhāsassā’’ti aparai| kaici pana ‘‘aṅgīrasau, siddhatthauti dvai  nāmāni pitarāyaiva gahitānī’’ti vadanti| appaṭimassāti anūpamassa| iṭṭhāniṭṭhaisu tādilakkhaṇappattiyā tādinau| pitupitā mayhaṃ tuvaṃsīti ariyajātivasaina mayhaṃ pitu sammāsambuddhassa laukavauhāraina tvaṃ pitā asi| sak‍kāti jātivasaina rājānaṃ ālapati| dhammaināti sabhāvaina ariyajāti laukiyajātīti dvin‍naṃ jātīnaṃ sabhāvasamaudhānaina gautamāti rājānaṃ gauttaina ālapati| ayyakausīti pitāmahau asi| aittha ca ‘‘buddhassa puttaumhī’’tiādiṃ vadantau thairau añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi|

aivaṃ  pana attānaṃ jānāpaitvā haṭṭhatuṭṭhaina rañ‍ñā mahārahai pal‍laṅkai nisīdāpaitvā attanau paṭiyāditassa nānaggarasassa bhaujanassa pattaṃ pūraitvā din‍nai gamanākāraṃ dassaiti| ‘‘kasmā gantukāmattha, bhuñ‍jathā’’ti ca vuttai, ‘‘satthu santikaṃ gantvā bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti| ‘‘kahaṃ pana satthā’’ti? ‘‘vīsatisahassabhikkhuparivārau tumhākaṃ dassanatthāya maggaṃ paṭipan‍nau’’ti| ‘‘tumhai imaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā yāva mama puttau imaṃ nagaraṃ sampāpuṇāti, tāvassa itauva piṇḍapātaṃ harathā’’ti| thairau bhattakic‍caṃ katvā rañ‍ñau parisāya ca dhammaṃ kathaitvā satthu āgamanatau puraitaramaiva sakalaṃ rājanivaisanaṃ ratanattayai abhippasan‍naṃ karauntau sabbaisaṃ passantānaṃyaiva satthu āharitabbabhattapuṇṇaṃ pattaṃ ākāsai vissaj‍jaitvā sayampi vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā  piṇḍapātaṃ upanaitvā satthu hatthai ṭhapaisi| satthā taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍ji| aivaṃ saṭṭhiyaujanaṃ maggaṃ divasai divasai yaujanaṃ gacchantassa satthu rājagaihatauva bhattaṃ āharitvā adāsi| atha naṃ bhagavā ‘‘mayhaṃ pitu mahārājassa sakalanivaisanaṃ pasādaisī’’ti kulappasādakānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisīti|

kāḷudāyitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. aikavihāriyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

puratau  pacchatau vātiādikā āyasmatau aikavihāriyatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau kassapadasabalassa kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā arañ‍ñaṃ pavisitvā vivaikavāsaṃ vasi|

sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina aikaṃ buddhantaraṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai bhagavati parinibbutai dhammāsaukarañ‍ñau kaniṭṭhabhātā hutvā nibbatti| asaukamahārājā kira satthu parinibbānatau dvin‍naṃ vassasatānaṃ upari aṭṭhārasamai vassai sakalajambudīpai aikaraj‍jābhisaikaṃ patvā attanau kaniṭṭhaṃ tissakumāraṃ auparaj‍jai ṭhapaitvā aikaina upāyaina taṃ sāsanai abhippasan‍naṃ akāsi| sau aikadivasaṃ migavaṃ gatau arañ‍ñai yaunakamahādhammarakkhitatthairaṃ hatthināgaina sālasākhaṃ gahaitvā bījiyamānaṃ nisin‍naṃ disvā sañ‍jātapasādau ‘‘ahau vatāhampi ayaṃ mahāthairau viya pabbajitvā arañ‍ñai viharaiyya’’nti cintaisi| thairau  tassa cittācāraṃ ñatvā tassa passantassaiva ākāsaṃ abbhuggantvā asaukārāmai paukkharaṇiyā abhij‍jamānai udakai ṭhatvā cīvarañ‍ca uttarāsaṅgañ‍ca ākāsai aulaggaitvā nhāyituṃ ārabhi| kumārau thairassa ānubhāvaṃ disvā abhippasan‍nau arañ‍ñatau nivattitvā rājagaihaṃ gantvā ‘‘pabbajissāmī’’ti rañ‍ñau āraucaisi| rājā taṃ anaikappakāraṃ yācitvā pabbaj‍jādhippāyaṃ nivattaituṃ nāsakkhi| sau upāsakau hutvā pabbaj‍jāsukhaṃ patthaintau –

537.

 ‘‘puratau pacchatau vāpi, aparau cai na vij‍jati|

atīva phāsu bhavati, aikassa vasatau vanai||

538.

 ‘‘handa  aikau gamissāmi, arañ‍ñaṃ buddhavaṇṇitaṃ|

phāsu aikavihārissa, pahitattassa bhikkhunau||

539.

 ‘‘yaugī-pītikaraṃ rammaṃ, mattakuñ‍jarasaivitaṃ|

aikau atthavasī khippaṃ, pavisissāmi kānanaṃ||

540.

 ‘‘supupphitai  sītavanai, sītalai girikandarai|

gattāni parisiñ‍citvā, caṅkamissāmi aikakau||

541.

 ‘‘aikākiyau adutiyau, ramaṇīyai mahāvanai|

kadāhaṃ viharissāmi, katakic‍cau anāsavau||

542.

 ‘‘aivaṃ mai kattukāmassa, adhippāyau samijjhatu|

sādhayissāmahaṃyaiva, nāñ‍ñau añ‍ñassa kārakau’’ti|| –

imā cha gāthā abhāsi|

tattha puratau pacchatau vāti attanau puratau vā pacchatau vā, vā-saddassa vikappatthattā passatau vā aparau añ‍ñau janau na vij‍jati cai, atīva ativiya phāsu cittasukhaṃ bhavati| aikavihārībhāvaina aikassa asahāyassa| vanai vasatauti ciraparicitaina vivaikajjhāsayaina ākaḍḍhiyamānahadayau sau rattindivaṃ mahājanaparivutassa vasatau saṅgaṇikavihāraṃ nibbindantau vivaikasukhañ‍ca bahuṃ mañ‍ñantau vadati|

handāti vaussaggatthai nipātau, taina idāni karīyamānassa arañ‍ñagamanassa nicchitabhāvamāha| aikau gamissāmīti ‘‘suñ‍ñāgārai khau, gahapati, tathāgatā abhiramantī’’tiādivacanatau (cūḷava॰ 306) buddhaihi vaṇṇitaṃ pasaṭṭhaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ aikau asahāyau gamissāmi vāsādhippāyaina upagacchāmi| yasmā aikavihārissa ṭhānādīsu asahāyabhāvaina aikavihārissa nibbānaṃ paṭipaisitacittatāya pahitattassa adhisīlasikkhādikā tissau sikkhā sikkhatau bhikkhunau arañ‍ñaṃ phāsu iṭṭhaṃ sukhāvahanti atthau|

yaugī-pītikaranti yaugīnaṃ bhāvanāya yuttappayuttānaṃ appasaddādibhāvaina jhānavipassanādipītiṃ āvahanatau yaugī-pītikaraṃ| visabhāgārammaṇābhāvaina paṭisal‍lānasāruppatāya rammaṃ| mattakuñ‍jarasaivitanti  mattavaravāraṇavicaritaṃ, imināpi brahārañ‍ñabhāvaina janavivaikaṃyaiva dassaiti| atthavasīti idha atthauti samaṇadhammau adhippaitau| ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau sau mai bhavaiyyā’’ti tassa vasaṃ gatau|

supupphitaiti  suṭṭhu pupphitai| sītavanaiti chāyūdakasampattiyā sītai vanai| ubhayainapi tassa ramaṇīyataṃyaiva vibhāvaiti| girikandaraiti girīnaṃ abbhantarai kandarai| kanti hi udakaṃ, taina dāritaṃ nin‍naṭṭhānaṃ kandaraṃ nāma| tādisai sītalai girikandarai ghammaparitāpaṃ vinaudaitvā attanau gattāni parisiñ‍citvā nhāyitvā caṅkamissāmi aikakauti katthaci anāyattavuttitaṃ dassaiti|

aikākiyauti aikākī asahāyau| adutiyauti taṇhāsaṅkhātadutiyābhāvaina adutiyau| taṇhā hi purisassa sabbadā avijahanaṭṭhaina dutiyā nāma| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘taṇhādutiyau purisau, dīghamaddhāna saṃsara’’nti (itivu॰ 15, 105)|

aivaṃ mai kattukāmassāti ‘‘handa aikau gamissāmī’’tiādinā vuttavidhinā arañ‍ñaṃ gantvā bhāvanābhiyaugaṃ kattukāmassa mai| adhippāyau samijjhatūti ‘‘kadāhaṃ viharissāmi, katakic‍cau anāsavau’’ti aivaṃ pavattau manaurathau ijjhatu siddhiṃ pāpuṇātu| arahattappatti ca yasmā na āyācanamattaina sijjhati, nāpi añ‍ñaina sādhaitabbā, tasmā āha ‘‘sādhayissāmahaṃyaiva, nāñ‍ñau añ‍ñassa kārakau’’ti|

aivaṃ uparājassa pabbaj‍jāya daḷhanicchayataṃ ñatvā rājā asaukārāmagamanīyaṃ maggaṃ alaṅkārāpaitvā kumāraṃ sabbālaṅkāravibhūsitaṃ mahatiyā saināya mahac‍carājānubhāvaina vihāraṃ naisi| kumārau padhānagharaṃ gantvā mahādhammarakkhitatthairassa santikai pabbaji, anaikasatā manussā taṃ anupabbajiṃsu| rañ‍ñau bhāginaiyyau saṅghamittāya sāmikau aggibrahmāpi tamaiva anupabbaji| sau pabbajitvā haṭṭhatuṭṭhau attanā kātabbaṃ pakāsaintau –

543.

 ‘‘aisa bandhāmi san‍nāhaṃ, pavisissāmi kānanaṃ|

na tatau nikkhamissāmi, appattau āsavakkhayaṃ||

544.

 ‘‘mālutai upavāyantai, sītai surabhigandhikai|

avij‍jaṃ dālayissāmi, nisin‍nau nagamuddhani||

545.

 ‘‘vanai  kusumasañchan‍nai, pabbhārai nūna sītalai|

vimuttisukhaina sukhitau, ramissāmi giribbajai’’ti|| –

tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha aisa bandhāmi san‍nāhanti aisāhaṃ vīriyasaṅkhātaṃ san‍nāhaṃ bandhāmi, kāyai ca  jīvitai ca nirapaikkhau vīriyasan‍nāhaina san‍nayhāmi| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yathā nāma sūrau purisau pac‍catthikai pac‍cupaṭṭhitai  taṃ jaitukāmau añ‍ñaṃ kic‍caṃ pahāya kavacapaṭimuc‍canādinā yuddhāya san‍nayhati, yuddhabhūmiñ‍ca gantvā pac‍catthikai ajaitvā tatau na nivattati, aivamahampi kilaisapac‍catthikai jaituṃ ādittampi sīsaṃ cailañ‍ca ajjhupaikkhitvā catubbidhasammappadhānavīriyasan‍nāhaṃ san‍nayhāmi, kilaisai ajaitvā kilaisavijayayauggaṃ vivaikaṭṭhānaṃ na vissaj‍jaimīti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘pavisissāmi kānanaṃ na tatau nikkhamissāmi, appattau āsavakkhaya’’nti|

‘‘mālutai upavāyantai’’tiādinā arañ‍ñaṭṭhānassa kammaṭṭhānabhāvanāyaugyataṃ vadati, ramissāmi nūna giribbajaiti yaujanā| pabbataparikkhaipai abhiramissāmi mañ‍ñaiti anāgatatthaṃ parikappaintau vadati| saisaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

aivaṃ vatvā thairau arañ‍ñaṃ pavisitvā samaṇadhammaṃ karauntau upajjhāyaina saddhiṃ kaliṅgaraṭṭhaṃ agamāsi| tatthassa pādai cammikābādhau uppaj‍ji, taṃ disvā aikau vaij‍jau ‘‘sappiṃ, bhantai, pariyaisatha, tikicchissāmi na’’nti āha| thairau sappipariyaisanaṃ akatvā vipassanāya aiva kammaṃ karauti, raugau vaḍḍhati, vaij‍jau thairassa tattha appaussuk‍kataṃ disvā sayamaiva sappiṃ pariyaisitvā thairaṃ araugaṃ akāsi| sau araugau hutvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.44.1-12) –

‘‘imamhi bhaddakai kappai, brahmabandhu mahāyasau|

kassapau nāma gauttaina, uppaj‍ji vadataṃ varau||

‘‘nippapañ‍cau nirālambau, ākāsasamamānasau|

suñ‍ñatābahulau tādī, animittaratau vasī||

‘‘asaṅgacittau  nik‍laisau, asaṃsaṭṭhau kulai gaṇai|

mahākāruṇikau vīrau, vinayaupāyakauvidau||

‘‘uyyuttau parakic‍caisu, vinayantau sadaivakai|

nibbānagamanaṃ maggaṃ, gatiṃ paṅkavisausanaṃ||

‘‘amataṃ paramassādaṃ, jarāmac‍cunivāraṇaṃ|

mahāparisamajjhai sau, nisin‍nau laukatārakau||

‘‘karavīkarutau  nāthau, brahmaghausau tathāgatau|

uddharantau mahāduggā, vippanaṭṭhai anāyakai||

‘‘daisaintau virajaṃ dhammaṃ, diṭṭhau mai laukanāyakau|

tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

‘‘pabbajitvā tadāpāhaṃ, cintaintau jinasāsanaṃ|

aikakauva vanai rammai, vasiṃ saṃsaggapīḷitau||

‘‘sak‍kāyavūpakāsau mai, haitubhūtau mamābhavī|

manasau vūpakāsassa, saṃsaggabhayadassinau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā thairai tattha viharantai rājā kauṭidhanaparic‍cāgaina bhaujakagirivihāraṃ nāma kāraitvā thairaṃ tattha vāsaisi| sau tattha viharantau parinibbānakālai –

546.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ paripuṇṇasaṅkappau, candau pan‍narasau yathā|

sabbāsavaparikkhīṇau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti|| –

ausānagāthamāha| sā uttānatthāva| tadaiva ca thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausīti|

aikavihāriyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. mahākappinatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

anāgataṃ  yau paṭikac‍ca passatītiādikā āyasmatau mahākappinatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarabuddhakālai haṃsavatīnagarai kulagharai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthu santikai dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ bhikkhuauvādakānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ  disvā taj‍jaṃ adhikārakammaṃ katvā taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaisi|

sau tattha yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau kassapasammāsambuddhakālai bārāṇasiyaṃ kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau purisasahassagaṇajaiṭṭhakau hutvā gabbhasahassapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ mahantaṃ parivaiṇaṃ kārāpaisi| tai sabbaipi janā yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā taṃ  upāsakaṃ jaiṭṭhakaṃ katvā saputtadārā daivalaukai nibbattitvā aikaṃ buddhantaraṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsariṃsu| taisu gaṇajaiṭṭhakau amhākaṃ satthu nibbattitau puraitaramaiva pac‍cantadaisai kuk‍kuṭanāmakai nagarai rājagaihai nibbatti, tassa kappinauti nāmaṃ ahausi| saisapurisā tasmiṃyaiva nagarai amac‍cakulai nibbattiṃsu| kappinakumārau pitu ac‍cayaina chattaṃ ussāpaitvā mahākappinarājā nāma jātau| sau sutavittakatāya pātauva catūhi dvāraihi sīghaṃ dūtai paisaisi – ‘‘yattha bahussutai passatha, tatau nivattitvā mayhaṃ āraucaithā’’ti|

taina ca samayaina amhākaṃ satthā laukai uppaj‍jitvā sāvatthiṃ upanissāya viharati| tasmiṃ kālai sāvatthivāsinau vāṇijā sāvatthiyaṃ uṭṭhānakabhaṇḍaṃ gahaitvā taṃ nagaraṃ gantvā bhaṇḍaṃ paṭisāmaitvā ‘‘rājānaṃ passissāmā’’ti paṇṇākārahatthā rañ‍ñau āraucāpaisuṃ| tai rājā pak‍kausāpaitvā niyyāditapaṇṇākārai vanditvā ṭhitai ‘‘kutau āgatatthā’’ti pucchi| ‘‘sāvatthitau, daivā’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci vau raṭṭhaṃ subhikkhaṃ, dhammikau rājā’’ti? ‘‘āma, daivā’’ti| ‘‘kīdisau dhammau tumhākaṃ daisai idāni pavattatī’’ti? ‘‘taṃ, daiva, na sak‍kā ucchiṭṭhamukhaihi kathaitu’’nti| rājā suvaṇṇabhiṅgāraina udakaṃ dāpaisi| tai mukhaṃ vikkhālaitvā dasabalābhimukhā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā, ‘‘daiva, amhākaṃ daisai buddharatanaṃ nāma uppan‍na’’nti  āhaṃsu| rañ‍ñau ‘‘buddhau’’ti vacanai sutamattaiyaiva sakalasarīraṃ pharamānā pīti uppaj‍ji| tatau ‘‘buddhauti, tātā, vadaithā’’ti āha| ‘‘buddhauti, daiva, vadāmā’’ti| aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ vadāpaitvā ‘‘buddhauti padaṃ aparimāṇa’’nti tasmiṃyaiva padai pasan‍nau satasahassaṃ datvā ‘‘aparaṃ vadaithā’’ti pucchi| ‘‘daiva, laukai dhammaratanaṃ nāma uppan‍na’’nti| tampi  sutvā tathaiva satasahassaṃ datvā ‘‘aparaṃ vadaithā’’ti pucchi| ‘‘daiva, saṅgharatanaṃ nāma uppan‍na’’nti| tampi sutvā tathaiva satasahassaṃ datvā ‘‘buddhassa bhagavatau santikai pabbajissāmī’’ti tatauva nikkhami| amac‍cāpi tathaiva nikkhamiṃsu| sau amac‍casahassaina saddhiṃ gaṅgātīraṃ patvā ‘‘sacai satthā sammāsambuddhau, imaisaṃ assānaṃ khuramattampi mā taimaitū’’ti sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ katvā udakapiṭṭhainaiva pūraṃ gaṅgānadiṃ atik‍kamitvā aparampi aḍḍhayaujanavitthāraṃ nadiṃ tathaiva atik‍kamitvā tatiyaṃ candabhāgaṃ nāma mahānadiṃ patvā tampi tāya aiva sac‍cakiriyāya atik‍kami|

satthāpi taṃdivasaṃ pac‍cūsasamayaṃyaiva mahākaruṇāsamāpattitau vuṭṭhāya laukaṃ vaulaukaintau ‘‘aj‍ja mahākappinau tiyaujanasatikaṃ raj‍jaṃ pahāya amac‍casahassaparivārau mama santikai pabbajituṃ āgamissatī’’ti disvā ‘‘mayā taisaṃ pac‍cuggamanaṃ kātuṃ yutta’’nti pātauva sarīrapaṭijagganaṃ katvā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau  sayamaiva ākāsaina gantvā candabhāgāya nadiyā tīrai taisaṃ uttaraṇatitthassābhimukhaṭṭhānai mahānigraudhamūlai pal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau chabbaṇṇabuddharasmiyau vissaj‍jaisi| tai taina titthaina uttarantā buddharasmiyau itau citau ca vidhāvantiyau aulaukaintau bhagavantaṃ disvā ‘‘yaṃ satthāraṃ uddissa mayaṃ āgatā, addhā sau aisau’’ti dassanainaiva niṭṭhaṃ gantvā diṭṭhaṭṭhānatau paṭṭhāya aunamitvā paramanipac‍cākāraṃ karauntā bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamiṃsu| rājā bhagavatau gaupphakaisu gahaitvā satthāraṃ vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi saddhiṃ amac‍casahassaina| satthā taisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi| daisanāpariyausānai saddhiṃ parisāya arahattai patiṭṭhāsi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.54.66-107) –

‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

uditau ajaṭākāsai, ravīva saradambarai||

‘‘vacanābhāya baudhaiti, vainaiyyapadumāni sau|

kilaisapaṅkaṃ sausaiti, matiraṃsīhi nāyakau||

‘‘titthiyānaṃ yasai hanti, khaj‍jautābhā yathā ravi|

sac‍catthābhaṃ pakāsaiti, ratanaṃva divākarau||

‘‘guṇānaṃ āyatibhūtau, ratanānaṃva sāgarau|

paj‍jun‍nauriva bhūtāni, dhammamaighaina vassati||

‘‘akkhadassau tadā āsiṃ, nagarai haṃsasavhayai|

upaic‍ca dhammamassausiṃ, jalajuttamanāminau||

‘‘auvādakassa bhikkhūnaṃ, sāvakassa katāvinau|

guṇaṃ pakāsayantassa, tappayantassa mai manaṃ||

‘‘sutvā patītau sumanau, nimantaitvā tathāgataṃ|

sasissaṃ bhaujayitvāna, taṃ ṭhānamabhipatthayiṃ||

‘‘tadā haṃsasamabhāgau, haṃsadundubhinissanau|

passathaitaṃ mahāmattaṃ, vinicchayavisāradaṃ||

‘‘patitaṃ pādamūlai mai, samuggatatanūruhaṃ|

jīmūtavaṇṇaṃ pīṇaṃsaṃ, pasan‍nanayanānanaṃ||

‘‘parivāraina mahatā, rājayuttaṃ mahāyasaṃ|

aisau katāvinau ṭhānaṃ, patthaiti muditāsayau||

‘‘iminā paṇipātaina, cāgaina paṇidhīhi ca|

kappasatasahassāni, nupapaj‍jati duggatiṃ||

‘‘daivaisu  daivasaubhaggaṃ, manussaisu mahantataṃ|

anubhautvāna saisaina, nibbānaṃ pāpuṇissati||

‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘tassa  dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

kappinau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

‘‘tatauhaṃ sukataṃ kāraṃ, katvāna jinasāsanai|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tusitaṃ agamāsahaṃ||

‘‘daivamānusaraj‍jāni, satasau anusāsiya|

bārāṇasiyamāsan‍nai, jātau kaiṇiyajātiyaṃ||

‘‘sahassaparivāraina, sapajāpatikau ahaṃ|

pañ‍capac‍caikabuddhānaṃ, satāni samupaṭṭhahiṃ||

‘‘taimāsaṃ bhaujayitvāna, pacchādamha ticīvaraṃ|

tatau cutā mayaṃ sabbai, ahumha tidasūpagā||

‘‘punau sabbai manussattaṃ, agamimha tatau cutā|

kuk‍kuṭamhi purai jātā, himavantassa passatau||

‘‘kappinau nāmahaṃ āsiṃ, rājaputtau mahāyasau|

saisāmac‍cakulai jātā, mamaiva parivārayuṃ||

‘‘mahāraj‍jasukhaṃ pattau, sabbakāmasamiddhimā|

vāṇijaihi samakkhātaṃ, buddhuppādamahaṃ suṇiṃ||

‘‘buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, asamau aikapuggalau|

sau pakāsaiti saddhammaṃ, amataṃ sukhamuttamaṃ||

‘‘suyuttā tassa sissā ca, sumuttā ca anāsavā|

sutvā naisaṃ suvacanaṃ, sak‍karitvāna vāṇijai||

‘‘pahāya raj‍jaṃ sāmac‍cau, nikkhamiṃ buddhamāmakau|

nadiṃ disvā mahācandaṃ, pūritaṃ samatittikaṃ||

‘‘appatiṭṭhaṃ anālambaṃ, duttaraṃ sīghavāhiniṃ|

guṇaṃ saritvā buddhassa, sautthinā samatik‍kamiṃ||

‘‘bhavasautaṃ sacai buddhau, tiṇṇau laukantagū vidū|

aitaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina, gamanaṃ mai samijjhatu||

‘‘yadi  santigamau maggau, maukkhau cac‍cantikaṃ sukhaṃ|

aitaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina, gamanaṃ mai samijjhatu||

‘‘saṅghau  cai tiṇṇakantārau, puñ‍ñakkhaittau anuttarau|

aitaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina, gamanaṃ mai samijjhatu||

‘‘saha katai sac‍cavarai, maggā apagataṃ jalaṃ|

tatau sukhaina uttiṇṇau, nadītīrai manauramai||

‘‘nisin‍naṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ, udaintaṃva pabhaṅkaraṃ|

jalantaṃ haimasailaṃva, dīparukkhaṃva jautitaṃ||

‘‘sasiṃva tārāsahitaṃ, sāvakaihi purakkhataṃ|

vāsavaṃ viya vassantaṃ, daisanājaladantaraṃ||

‘‘vanditvāna sahāmac‍cau, aikamantamupāvisiṃ|

tatau nau āsayaṃ ñatvā, buddhau dhammamadaisayi||

‘‘sutvāna dhammaṃ vimalaṃ, avaucumha mayaṃ jinaṃ|

pabbājaihi mahāvīra, nibbindāmha mayaṃ bhavai||

‘‘svakkhātau bhikkhavai dhammau, dukkhantakaraṇāya vau|

caratha brahmacariyaṃ, ic‍cāha munisattamau||

‘‘saha vācāya sabbaipi, bhikkhuvaisadharā mayaṃ|

ahumha upasampan‍nā, sautāpan‍nā ca sāsanai||

‘‘tatau jaitavanaṃ gantvā, anusāsi vināyakau|

anusiṭṭhau jinaināhaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

‘‘tatau bhikkhusahassāni, anusāsimahaṃ tadā|

mamānusāsanakarā, taipi āsuṃ anāsavā||

‘‘jinau tasmiṃ guṇai tuṭṭhau, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ|

bhikkhuauvādakānaggau, kappinauti mahājanai||

‘‘satasahassai kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha|

pamuttau saravaigauva, kilaisai jhāpayiṃ mama||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ  patvā pana tai sabbaiva satthāraṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ yāciṃsu| satthā tai ‘‘aitha, bhikkhavau’’ti āha| sā aiva taisaṃ pabbaj‍jā upasampadā ca ahausi| satthā taṃ bhikkhusahassaṃ ādāya ākāsaina jaitavanaṃ agamāsi| athaikadivasaṃ bhagavā tassantaivāsikai bhikkhū āha – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhikkhavai, kappinau bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti? ‘‘na, bhagavā, daisaiti| appaussuk‍kau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāramanuyuttau viharati, auvādamattampi na daitī’’ti| satthā  thairaṃ pak‍kausāpaitvā – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, kappina, antaivāsikānaṃ auvādamattampi na daisī’’ti? ‘‘sac‍caṃ, bhagavā’’ti| ‘‘brāhmaṇa, mā aivaṃ kari, aj‍ja paṭṭhāya upagatānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaihī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti thairau satthu vacanaṃ sirasā sampaṭicchitvā aikauvādainaiva samaṇasahassaṃ arahattai patiṭṭhāpaisi| taina naṃ satthā paṭipāṭiyā attanau sāvakai thairai ṭhānantarai ṭhapaintau bhikkhuauvādakānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi| athaikadivasaṃ thairau bhikkhuniyau auvadantau –

547.

 ‘‘anāgataṃ yau paṭikac‍ca passati, hitañ‍ca atthaṃ ahitañ‍ca taṃ dvayaṃ|

viddaisinau tassa hitaisinau vā, randhaṃ na passanti samaikkhamānā||

548.

 ‘‘ānāpānasatī yassa, paripuṇṇā subhāvitā|

anupubbaṃ paricitā, yathā buddhaina daisitā|

saumaṃ laukaṃ pabhāsaiti, abbhā muttauva candimā||

549.

 ‘‘audātaṃ vata mai cittaṃ, appamāṇaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

nibbiddhaṃ paggahītañ‍ca, sabbā aubhāsatai disā||

550.

 ‘‘jīvatai vāpi sappañ‍ñau, api vittaparikkhayau|

pañ‍ñāya ca alābhaina, vittavāpi na jīvati||

551.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñā sutavinicchinī, pañ‍ñā kittisilaukavaddhanī|

pañ‍ñāsahitau narau idha, api dukkhaisu sukhāni vindati||

552.

 ‘‘nāyaṃ  aj‍jatanau dhammau, nacchairau napi abbhutau|

yattha jāyaitha mīyaitha, tattha kiṃ viya abbhutaṃ||

553.

 ‘‘anantarañhi jātassa, jīvitā maraṇaṃ dhuvaṃ|

jātā jātā marantīdha, aivaṃ dhammā hi pāṇinau||

554.

 ‘‘na haitadatthāya matassa hauti, yaṃ jīvitatthaṃ parapaurisānaṃ|

matamhi ruṇṇaṃ na yasau na laukyaṃ, na vaṇṇitaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi||

555.

 ‘‘cakkhuṃ sarīraṃ upahanti taina, nihīyati vaṇṇabalaṃ matī ca|

ānandinau tassa disā bhavanti, hitaisinau nāssa sukhī bhavanti||

556.

 ‘‘tasmā hi icchaiyya kulai vasantai, maidhāvinau caiva bahussutai ca|

yaisañhi pañ‍ñāvibhavaina kic‍caṃ, taranti nāvāya nadiṃva puṇṇa’’nti|| –

imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha  anāgatanti na āgataṃ, avindanti, atthau| paṭikac‍cāti putaitaraṃyaiva| passatīti aulaukaiti| atthanti kic‍caṃ| taṃ dvayanti hitāhitaṃ| viddaisinauti amittā| hitaisinauti mittā| randhanti chiddaṃ| samaikkhamānāti gavaisantā| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yau puggalau attanau hitāvahaṃ ahitāvahaṃ tadubhayañ‍ca atthaṃ kic‍caṃ anāgataṃ asampattaṃ puraitaraṃyaiva pañ‍ñācakkhunā ahaṃ viya passati vīmaṃsati vicāraiti, tassa amittā vā ahitajjhāsayaina mittā vā hitajjhāsayaina randhaṃ gavaisantā na passanti, tādisau pañ‍ñavā puggalau acchiddavutti, tasmā tumhaihi tathārūpaihi bhavitabbanti|

idāni ānāpānasatibhāvanāya guṇaṃ dassaintau tattha tāni yaujaituṃ ‘‘ānāpānasatī yassā’’ti dutiyaṃ gāthamāha| tattha ānanti  assāsau| apānanti passāsau| assāsapassāsanimittārammaṇā sati ānāpānasati| satisīsaina caittha taṃsampayuttasamādhibhāvanā adhippaitā| yassāti, yassa yauginau| paripuṇṇā subhāvitāti catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ sauḷasan‍nañ‍ca ākārānaṃ pāripūriyā sabbasau puṇṇā sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ vij‍jāvimuttīnañ‍ca pāripūriyā suṭṭhu bhāvitā vaḍḍhitā| anupubbaṃ paricitā, yathā buddhaina daisitāti ‘‘sau satauva assasatī’’tiādinā (dī॰ ni॰ 2.374; ma॰ ni॰ 1.107) yathā bhagavatā daisitā, tathā anupubbaṃ anuk‍kamaina paricitā āsaivitā bhāvitā| saumaṃ laukaṃ pabhāsaiti, abbhā muttauva candimāti sau yaugāvacarau yathā abbhādiupak‍kilaisā vimuttau candau candālaukaina imaṃ aukāsalaukaṃ pabhāsaiti, aivaṃ avij‍jādiupak‍kilaisavimuttau ñāṇālaukaina attasantānapatitaṃ parasantānapatitañ‍ca saṅkhāralaukaṃ pabhāsaiti pakāsaiti| tasmā tumhaihi ānāpānasatibhāvanā bhāvaitabbāti adhippāyau|

idāni attānaṃ nidassanaṃ katvā bhāvanābhiyaugassa saphalataṃ dassaintau ‘‘audātaṃ vata mai citta’’nti tatiyaṃ gāthamāha| tassatthau – nīvaraṇamalavigamatau audātaṃ suddhaṃ vata mama cittaṃ| yathā pamāṇakarā rāgādayau pahīnā, appamāṇañ‍ca nibbānaṃ pac‍cakkhaṃ kataṃ ahausi, tathā bhāvitattā appamāṇaṃ subhāvitaṃ, tatau aiva catusac‍caṃ nibbiddhaṃ paṭivijjhitaṃ, sakalasaṃkilaisapakkhatau paggahitañ‍ca hutvā dukkhādikā pubbantādikā ca disā aubhāsatai tattha vitiṇṇakaṅkhattā sabbadhammaisu vigatasammauhattā ca| tasmā tumhaihipi aivaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaitabbanti dassaiti|

yathā bhāvanāmayā pañ‍ñā cittamalavisaudhanādinā purisassa bahupakārā, aivaṃ itarāpīti dassaintau  ‘‘jīvatai vāpi sappañ‍ñau’’ti catutthagāthamāha| tassatthau – parikkhīṇadhanaupi sappañ‍ñajātikau itarītarasantausaina santuṭṭhau anavaj‍jāya jīvikāya jīvatiyaiva| tassa hi jīvitaṃ jīvitaṃ nāma| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘pañ‍ñājīviṃ jīvitamāhu saiṭṭha’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.73, 246; su॰ ni॰ 184)| dummaidhapuggalau pana pañ‍ñāya alābhaina  diṭṭhadhammikaṃ samparāyikañ‍ca atthaṃ  virādhaintau vittavāpi na jīvati, garahādippattiyā jīvantau nāma na tassa hauti, anupāyañ‍ñutāya vā yathādhigataṃ dhanaṃ nāsaintau jīvitampi sandhāraituṃ na sak‍kautiyaiva, tasmā pārihāriyapañ‍ñāpi tumhaihi appamattaihi sampādaitabbāti adhippāyau|

idāni pañ‍ñāya ānisaṃsai dassaituṃ ‘‘pañ‍ñā sutavinicchinī’’ti pañ‍camaṃ gāthamāha| tattha pañ‍ñā sutavinicchinīti pañ‍ñā nāmaisā sutassa vinicchayinī, yathāsutai sautapathamāgatai atthai ‘‘ayaṃ akusalau, ayaṃ kusalau, ayaṃ sāvaj‍jau, ayaṃ anavaj‍jau’’tiādinā vinicchayajananī| kittisilaukavaddhanīti kittiyā sammukhā pasaṃsāya silaukassa patthaṭayasabhāvassa vaddhanī, pañ‍ñavatauyaiva hi kittiādayau viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsabhāvatau| pañ‍ñāsahitauti pārihāriyapañ‍ñāya, vipassanāpañ‍ñāya ca yuttau| api dukkhaisu sukhāni vindatīti aikantadukkhasabhāvaisu khandhāyatanādīsu sammāpaṭipattiyā yathābhūtasabhāvāvabaudhaina nirāmisānipi sukhāni paṭilabhati|

idāni tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ anic‍catāpaṭisaṃyuttaṃ dhīrabhāvāvahaṃ dhammaṃ kathaintau ‘‘nāyaṃ aj‍jatanau dhammau’’tiādinā saisagāthā abhāsi| tatrāyaṃ saṅkhaipatthau – yvāyaṃ sattānaṃ jāyanamīyanasabhāvau, ayaṃ dhammau aj‍jatanau adhunāgatau na hauti, abhiṇhapavattikatāya na acchariyau, abbhutapubbatābhāvatau nāpi abbhutau| tasmā yattha jāyaitha mīyaitha, yasmiṃ laukai sattau jāyaiyya, sau aikaṃsaina mīyaitha, tattha kiṃ viya? kiṃ nāma abbhutaṃ siyā? sabhāvikattā maraṇassa – na hi khaṇikamaraṇassa kiñ‍ci kāraṇaṃ atthi| yatau anantarañhi jātassa, jīvitā maraṇaṃ dhuvaṃ jātassa jātisamanantaraṃ jīvitatau maraṇaṃ aikantikaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ khandhānaṃ aikaṃsaina bhij‍janatau| yau panaittha jīvatīti laukavauhārau, sau tadupādānassa anaikapac‍cayāyattatāya anaikantikau, yasmā aitadaivaṃ, tasmā jātā marantīdha, aivaṃdhammā hi pāṇinauti ayaṃ sattānaṃ pakati, yadidaṃ jātānaṃ maraṇanti jātiyā maraṇānubandhanataṃ āha|

idāni  yasmā tāsu bhikkhunīsu kāci saukabandhitacittāpi atthi, tasmā tāsaṃ saukavinaudanaṃ kātuṃ ‘‘na haitadatthāyātiādi vuttaṃ| tattha na haitadatthāya matassa hautīti yaṃ matassa  jīvitatthaṃ jīvitanimittaṃ parapaurisānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ ruṇṇaṃ, aitaṃ tassa matassa sattassa jīvitatthaṃ tāva tiṭṭhatu, kassacipi atthāya na hauti, yai pana rudanti, taisampi matamhi matapuggalanimittaṃ ruṇṇaṃ, na yasau na laukyaṃ yasāvahaṃ visuddhāvahañ‍ca na hauti| na vaṇṇitaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇaihīti viñ‍ñuppasaṭṭhampi na hauti, atha khau viñ‍ñugarahitamaivāti atthau|

na kaivalamaitaiva yai rudatau ādīnavā, atha khau imaipīti dassaintau ‘‘cakkhuṃ sarīraṃ upahantī’’ti gāthaṃ vatvā tatau paraṃ saukādianatthapaṭibāhanatthaṃ kalyāṇamittapayirupāsanāyaṃ tā niyaujaintau ‘‘tasmā’’tiādinā ausānagāthamāha| tattha tasmāti yasmā ruṇṇaṃ rudantassa puggalassa cakkhuṃ sarīrañ‍ca upahanti vibādhati, taina ruṇṇaina vaṇṇau balaṃ mati ca nihīyati parihāyati, tassa rudantassa puggalassa disā sapattā ānandinau pamaudavantau pītivantau bhavanti| hitaisinau mittā dukkhī dukkhitā bhavanti tasmā dhammaujapañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatattā maidhāvinau diṭṭhadhammikādiatthasan‍nissitassa bāhusac‍cassa pāripūriyā bahussutai, attanau kulai vasantai icchaiyya pāṭikaṅkhaiyya kulūpakai karaiyya| yaisanti yaisaṃ maidhāvīnaṃ bahussutānaṃ paṇḍitānaṃ pañ‍ñāvibhavaina pañ‍ñābalaina yathā mahaughassa puṇṇaṃ nadiṃ nāvāya taranti, aivaṃ kulaputtā attanau atthakic‍caṃ taranti pāraṃ pāpuṇanti| tai icchaiyya kulai vasantaiti yaujanā|

aivaṃ thairau tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ dhammaṃ kathaitvā vissaj‍jaisi| tā thairassa auvādai ṭhatvā saukaṃ vinaudaitvā yaunisau paṭipaj‍jantiyau sadatthaṃ paripūraisuṃ|

mahākappinatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. cūḷapanthakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

dandhā  mayhaṃ gatītiādikā āyasmatau cūḷapanthakatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? yadaittha aṭṭhuppattivasaina vattabbaṃ, taṃ aṭṭhakanipātai mahāpanthakavatthusmiṃ (thairagā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.mahāpanthakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā) vuttamaiva| ayaṃ pana visaisau – mahāpanthakatthairau arahattaṃ patvā aggaphalasukhaina vītināmaintau cintaisi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau sak‍kā cūḷapanthakampi imasmiṃ sukhai patiṭṭhapaitu’’nti? sau attanau ayyakaṃ dhanasaiṭṭhiṃ upasaṅkamitvā āha – ‘‘sacai, mahāsaiṭṭhi, anujānātha, ahaṃ cūḷapanthakaṃ pabbājaiyya’’nti| ‘‘pabbājaitha, bhantai’’ti| thairau taṃ pabbājaisi| sau dasasu sīlaisu patiṭṭhitau bhātu santikai –

‘‘padumaṃ yathā kaukanadaṃ sugandhaṃ, pātau siyā phul‍lamavītagandhaṃ|

aṅgīrasaṃ passa viraucamānaṃ, tapantamādic‍camivantalikkhai’’ti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.123; a॰ ni॰ 5.195) –

gāthaṃ  uggaṇhantau catūhi māsaihi gahaituṃ nāsakkhi, gahitagahitaṃ padaṃ hadayai na tiṭṭhati| atha naṃ mahāpanthakau āha – ‘‘panthaka, tvaṃ imasmiṃ sāsanai abhabbau, catūhi māsaihi aikagāthampi gahaituṃ na sak‍kausi| pabbajitakic‍caṃ pana tvaṃ kathaṃ matthakaṃ pāpaissasi? nikkhama itau’’ti| sau thairaina paṇāmitau dvārakauṭṭhakasamīpai raudamānau aṭṭhāsi|

taina ca samayaina satthā jīvakambavanai viharati| atha jīvakau purisaṃ paisaisi, ‘‘pañ‍cahi bhikkhusataihi saddhiṃ satthāraṃ nimantaihī’’ti| taina ca samayaina āyasmā mahāpanthakau bhattuddaisakau hauti| sau ‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ bhikkhaṃ paṭicchatha, bhantai’’ti vuttau ‘‘cūḷapanthakaṃ ṭhapaitvā saisānaṃ paṭicchāmī’’ti āha| taṃ sutvā cūḷapanthakau bhiyyausaumattāya daumanassappattau ahausi| satthā tassa cittakkhaidaṃ ñatvā, ‘‘cūḷapanthakau mayā kataina upāyaina bujjhissatī’’ti tassa avidūrai ṭhānai attānaṃ dassaitvā ‘‘kiṃ, panthaka, raudasī’’ti pucchi| ‘‘bhātā maṃ, bhantai, paṇāmaitī’’ti āha| ‘‘panthaka, mā cintayi, mama sāsanai tuyhaṃ pabbaj‍jā, aihi, imaṃ gahaitvā ‘rajauharaṇaṃ, rajauharaṇa’nti manasi karauhī’’ti iddhiyā suddhaṃ cauḷakkhaṇḍaṃ abhisaṅkharitvā adāsi| sau satthārā  din‍naṃ cauḷakkhaṇḍaṃ ‘‘rajauharaṇaṃ, rajauharaṇa’’nti hatthaina parimaj‍jantau nisīdi| tassa taṃ parimaj‍jantassa kiliṭṭhadhātukaṃ jātaṃ, puna parimaj‍jantassa ukkhaliparipuñchanasadisaṃ jātaṃ| sau ñāṇassa paripak‍kattā aivaṃ cintaisi – ‘‘idaṃ cauḷakkhaṇḍaṃ pakatiyā parisuddhaṃ, imaṃ upādiṇṇakasarīraṃ nissāya kiliṭṭhaṃ añ‍ñathā jātaṃ, tasmā anic‍caṃ yathāpaitaṃ, aivaṃ cittampī’’ti khayavayaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā tasmiṃyaiva nimittai jhānāni nibbattaitvā jhānapādakaṃ vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.2.35-54) –

‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

gaṇamhā vūpakaṭṭhau sau, himavantai vasī tadā||

‘‘ahampi  himavantamhi, vasāmi assamai tadā|

acirāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ, upaisiṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘pupphacchattaṃ gahaitvāna, upagacchiṃ narāsabhaṃ|

samādhiṃ samāpaj‍jantaṃ, antarāyamakāsahaṃ||

‘‘ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, pupphacchattaṃ adāsahaṃ|

paṭiggahaisi bhagavā, padumuttarau mahāmuni||

‘‘sabbai daivā attamanā, himavantaṃ upainti tai|

sādhukāraṃ pavattaisuṃ, anumaudissati cakkhumā||

‘‘idaṃ vatvāna tai daivā, upagacchuṃ naruttamaṃ|

ākāsai dhārayantassa, padumacchattamuttamaṃ||

‘‘satapattachattaṃ paggayha, adāsi tāpasau mama|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘pañ‍cavīsatikappāni, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

catuttiṃsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yauniṃ saṃsarati, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

abbhaukāsai patiṭṭhantaṃ, padumaṃ dhārayissati||

‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘pakāsitai pāvacanai, manussattaṃ labhissati|

manaumayamhi kāyamhi, uttamau sau bhavissati||

‘‘dvai bhātarau bhavissanti, ubhaupi panthakavhayā|

anubhautvā uttamatthaṃ, jautayissanti sāsanaṃ||

‘‘sauhaṃ aṭṭhārasavassau, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

visaisāhaṃ na vindāmi, sakyaputtassa sāsanai||

‘‘dandhā mayhaṃ gatī āsi, paribhūtau purai ahuṃ|

bhātā ca maṃ paṇāmaisi, gaccha dāni sakaṃ gharaṃ||

‘‘sauhaṃ paṇāmitau santau, saṅghārāmassa kauṭṭhakai|

dummanau tattha aṭṭhāsiṃ, sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ apaikkhavā||

‘‘bhagavā tattha āgacchi, sīsaṃ mayhaṃ parāmasi|

bāhāya maṃ gahaitvāna, saṅghārāmaṃ pavaisayi||

‘‘anukampāya mai satthā, adāsi pādapuñchaniṃ|

aivaṃ suddhaṃ adhiṭṭhaihi, aikamantamadhiṭṭhahaṃ||

‘‘hatthaihi  tamahaṃ gayha, sariṃ kaukanadaṃ ahaṃ|

tattha cittaṃ vimuc‍ci mai, arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ||

‘‘manaumayaisu kāyaisu, sabbattha pāramiṃ gatau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattamaggainaivassa taipiṭakaṃ pañ‍cābhiñ‍ñā ca āgamiṃsu| satthā aikaina ūnaihi pañ‍cahi bhikkhusataihi saddhiṃ gantvā jīvakassa nivaisanai pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| cūḷapanthakau pana attanau bhikkhāya appaṭicchitattā aiva na gatau| jīvakau yāguṃ dātuṃ ārabhi, satthā pattaṃ hatthaina pidahi| ‘‘kasmā, bhantai, na gaṇhathā’’ti vuttai – ‘‘vihārai aikau bhikkhu atthi, jīvakā’’ti| sau purisaṃ pahiṇi, ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai, vihārai nisin‍naṃ ayyaṃ gahaitvā  aihī’’ti| cūḷapanthakatthairaupi rūpaina kiriyāya ca aikampi aikaina asadisaṃ bhikkhusahassaṃ nimminitvā nisīdi| sau purisau vihārai bhikkhūnaṃ bahubhāvaṃ disvā gantvā jīvakassa kathaisi – ‘‘imasmā bhikkhusaṅghā vihārai bhikkhusaṅghau bahutarau, pak‍kausitabbaṃ ayyaṃ na jānāmī’’ti| jīvakau satthāraṃ paṭipucchi – ‘‘kaunāmau, bhantai, vihārai nisin‍nau bhikkhū’’ti? ‘‘cūḷapanthakau nāma, jīvakā’’ti| ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai, ‘cūḷapanthakau nāma katarau’ti pucchitvā taṃ ānaihī’’ti| sau vihāraṃ gantvā ‘‘cūḷapanthakau nāma katarau, bhantai’’ti pucchi| ‘‘ahaṃ cūḷapanthakau’’,‘‘ahaṃ cūḷapanthakau’’ti aikapahārainaiva bhikkhusahassampi kathaisi| sau punāgantvā taṃ pavattiṃ jīvakassa āraucaisi| jīvakau paṭividdhasac‍cattā ‘‘iddhimā mañ‍ñai, ayyau’’ti nayatau ñatvā ‘‘gaccha, bhaṇai, paṭhamaṃ kathanakamayyamaiva ‘tumhai satthā pak‍kausatī’ti vatvā cīvarakaṇṇai gaṇhā’’ti āha| sau vihāraṃ gantvā tathā akāsi, tāvadaiva nimmitabhikkhū antaradhāyiṃsu| sau thairaṃ gahaitvā agamāsi|

satthā tasmiṃ khaṇai yāguñ‍ca khaj‍jakādibhaidañ‍ca paṭiggaṇhi| dasabalai bhattakic‍caṃ katvā vihāraṃ gatai dhammasabhāyaṃ kathā udapādi – ‘‘ahau buddhānaṃ ānubhāvau, yatra hi nāma cattārau māsai aikagāthaṃ gahaituṃ asak‍kauntampi lahukaina khaṇainaiva aivaṃ mahiddhikaṃ akaṃsū’’ti| satthā taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kathāsal‍lāpaṃ sutvā āgantvā buddhāsanai nisaj‍ja, ‘‘kiṃ vadaitha, bhikkhavai’’ti pucchitvā, ‘‘imaṃ nāma, bhantai’’ti vuttai, ‘‘bhikkhavai, cūḷapanthakaina idāni mayhaṃ auvādai ṭhatvā laukuttaradāyaj‍jaṃ laddhaṃ, pubbai pana laukiyadāyaj‍ja’’nti vatvā taihi yācitau cūḷasaiṭṭhijātakaṃ (jā॰ 1.1.4) kathaisi| aparabhāgai taṃ satthā ariyagaṇaparivutau dhammāsanai nisin‍nau manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ  abhinimminantānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ caitauvivaṭṭakusalānañ‍ca aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi| sau aparaina samayaina bhikkhūhi ‘‘tathā dandhadhātukaina kathaṃ tayā sac‍cāni paṭividdhānī’’ti puṭṭhau bhātu paṇāmanatau paṭṭhāya attanau paṭipattiṃ pakāsaintau –

557.

 ‘‘dandhā mayhaṃ gatī āsi, paribhūtau purai ahaṃ|

bhātā ca maṃ paṇāmaisi, gaccha dāni tuvaṃ gharaṃ||

558.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ  paṇāmitau santau, saṅghārāmassa kauṭṭhakai|

dummanau tattha aṭṭhāsiṃ, sāsanasmiṃ apaikkhavā||

559.

 ‘‘bhagavā  tattha āgacchi, sīsaṃ mayhaṃ parāmasi|

bāhāya maṃ gahaitvāna, saṅghārāmaṃ pavaisayi||

560.

 ‘‘anukampāya mai satthā, pādāsi pādapuñchaniṃ|

aitaṃ suddhaṃ adhiṭṭhaihi, aikamantaṃ svadhiṭṭhitaṃ||

561.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, vihāsiṃ sāsanai ratau|

samādhiṃ paṭipādaisiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā||

562.

 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ jānāmi, dibbacakkhu visaudhitaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

563.

 ‘‘sahassakkhattumattānaṃ, nimminitvāna panthakau|

nisīdambavanai rammai, yāva kālappavaidanā||

564.

 ‘‘tatau mai satthā pāhaisi, dūtaṃ kālappavaidakaṃ|

pavaiditamhi kālamhi, vaihāsādupasaṅkamiṃ||

565.

 ‘‘vanditvā satthunau pādai, aikamantaṃ nisīdahaṃ|

nisin‍naṃ maṃ viditvāna, attha satthā paṭiggahi||

566.

 ‘‘āyāgau sabbalaukassa, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ manussānaṃ, paṭiggaṇhittha dakkhiṇa’’nti|| –

imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha dandhāti, mandā, catuppadikaṃ gāthaṃ catūhi māsaihi gahaituṃ asamatthabhāvaina dubbalā| gatīti ñāṇagati| āsīti, ahausi| paribhūtauti, tatau aiva ‘‘muṭṭhassati asampajānau’’ti hīḷitau| puraiti, pubbai puthuj‍janakālai| bhātā cāti samuc‍cayatthau ca-saddau, na kaivalaṃ paribhūtauva, atha khau bhātāpi maṃ paṇāmaisi, ‘‘panthaka, tvaṃ duppañ‍ñau ahaitukau mañ‍ñai, tasmā pabbajitakic‍caṃ  matthakaṃ pāpaituṃ asamatthau, na imassa sāsanassa anucchavikau, gaccha dāni tuyhaṃ ayyakaghara’’nti nik‍kaḍḍhaisi| bhātāti, bhātarā|

kauṭṭhakaiti, dvārakauṭṭhakasamīpai| dummanauti, daumanassitau| sāsanasmiṃ apaikkhavāti, sammāsambuddhassa sāsanai sāpaikkhau avibbhamitukāmau|

bhagavā tattha āgacchīti, mahākaruṇāsañ‍cauditamānasau maṃ anuggaṇhantau bhagavā yatthāhaṃ ṭhitau, tattha āgacchi| āgantvā ca, ‘‘panthaka, ahaṃ tai satthā, na mahāpanthakau, maṃ uddissa tava pabbaj‍jā’’ti samassāsaintau sīsaṃ mayhaṃ parāmasi jālābandhanamudutalunapīṇavarāyataṅgulisamupasaubhitaina vikasitapadumasassirīkaina cak‍kaṅkitaina hatthatalaina ‘‘idāniyaiva mama puttau bhavissatī’’ti dīpaintau mayhaṃ sīsaṃ parāmasi| bāhāya maṃ gahaitvānāti, ‘‘kasmā tvaṃ, idha tiṭṭhasī’’ti candanagandhagandhinā attanau hatthaina maṃ bhujai gahaitvā antausaṅghārāmaṃ pavaisaisi| pādāsi  pādapuñchaninti pādapuñchaniṃ katvā pādāsi ‘‘rajauharaṇanti manasi karauhī’’ti adāsīti atthau| ‘‘adāsī’’ti ‘‘pādapuñchani’’nti ca paṭhanti| kaici pana ‘‘pādapuñchani’’nti pādapuñchanacauḷakkhaṇḍaṃ pādāsī’’ti vadanti| tadayuttaṃ iddhiyā abhisaṅkharitvā cauḷakkhaṇḍassa din‍nattā| aitaṃ suddhaṃ adhiṭṭhaihi, aikamantaṃ svadhiṭṭhitanti, aitaṃ suddhaṃ cauḷakkhaṇḍaṃ ‘‘rajauharaṇaṃ, rajauharaṇa’’nti manasikāraina svadhiṭṭhitaṃ katvā aikamantaṃ aikamantai vivittai gandhakuṭipamukhai nisin‍nau adhiṭṭhaihi tathā cittaṃ samāhitaṃ katvā pavattaihi|

tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvāti, tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ auvādaṃ ahaṃ sutvā tasmiṃ sāsanai auvādai ratau abhiratau hutvā vihāsiṃ yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃ| paṭipaj‍jantau ca samādhiṃ paṭipādaisiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyāti, uttamatthau nāma arahattaṃ, tassa adhigamāya kasiṇaparikammavasaina rūpajjhānāni nibbattaitvā jhānapādakaṃ vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā maggapaṭipāṭiyā aggamaggasamādhiṃ sampādaisinti atthau| aittha hi samādhīti upacārasamādhitau paṭṭhāya yāva catutthamaggasamādhi, tāva samādhisāmañ‍ñaina gahitau, aggaphalasamādhi pana uttamatthaggahaṇaina, sātisayaṃ caivāyaṃ samādhikusalau, tasmā ‘‘samādhiṃ paṭipādaisi’’nti āha| samādhikusalatāya  hi ayamāyasmā caitauvivaṭṭakusalau nāma jātau, mahāpanthakatthairau pana vipassanākusalatāya sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭakusalau nāma| aikau caittha samādhilakkhaṇai chaikau, aikau vipassanālakkhaṇai, aikau samādhigāḷhau, aikau vipassanāgāḷhau aikau aṅgasaṃkhittai chaikau, aikau ārammaṇasaṃkhittai, aikau aṅgavavatthānai, aikau ārammaṇavavatthānaiti vaṇṇainti| apica cūḷapanthakatthairau  sātisayaṃ catun‍naṃ rūpāvacarajjhānānaṃ lābhitāya caitauvivaṭṭakusalau vuttau, mahāpanthakatthairau sātisayaṃ catun‍naṃ arūpāvacarajjhānānaṃ lābhitāya sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭakusalau| paṭhamau vā rūpāvacarajjhānalābhī hutvā jhānaṅgaihi vuṭṭhāya arahattaṃ pattauti caitauvivaṭṭakusalau, itarau arūpāvacarajjhānalābhī hutvā jhānaṅgaihi vuṭṭhāya arahattaṃ pattauti sañ‍ñāvivaṭṭakusalau| manaumayaṃ pana kāyaṃ nibbattaintau añ‍ñai tayau vā cattārau vā nibbattanti, na bahukai, aikasadisaiyaiva ca katvā nibbattainti, aikavidhamaiva kammaṃ kurumānai| ayaṃ pana thairau aikāvaj‍janaina samaṇasahassaṃ māpaisi, dvaipi na kāyaina aikasadisai akāsi, na aikavidhaṃ kammaṃ kurumānai| tasmā manaumayaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimminantānaṃ aggau nāma jātau|

idāni  attanau adhigatavisaisaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ jānāmī’’tiādimāha| kāmañ‍cāyaṃ thairau chaḷabhiñ‍ñau, yā pana abhiñ‍ñā āsavakkhayañāṇādhigamassa bahūpakārā, taṃ dassanatthaṃ ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ jānāmi, dibbacakkhu visaudhita’’nti vatvā ‘‘tissau vij‍jā anuppattā’’ti vuttaṃ| pubbainivāsayathākammupagaanāgataṃsañāṇāni hi vipassanācārassa bahūpakārāni, na tathā itarañāṇāni|

sahassakkhattunti sahassaṃ| ‘‘sahassavāra’’nti kaici vadanti| aikāvaj‍janaina pana thairau sahassai manaumayai kāyai nimmini, na vāraina| tai ca khau añ‍ñamañ‍ñamasadisai vividhañ‍ca kammaṃ karauntai| ‘‘kiṃ pana sāvakānampi aivarūpaṃ iddhinimmānaṃ sambhavatī’’ti? na sambhavati sabbaisaṃ, abhinīhārasampattiyā pana ayamaiva thairau aivamakāsi, tathā haisa iminā aṅgaina aitadaggai ṭhapitau| panthakau nisīdīti attānamaiva paraṃ viya vadati| ambavanaiti, ambavanai jīvakaina  katavihārai| vaihāsādupasaṅkaminti vaihāsāti karaṇai nissak‍kavacanaṃ, vaihāsaināti atthau, da-kārau padasandhikarau| athāti, mama nisaj‍jāya pacchā| paṭiggahīti dakkhiṇaudakaṃ paṭiggaṇhi| āyāgau sabbalaukassāti, sabbassa sadaivakassa laukassa aggadakkhiṇaiyyatāya daiyyadhammaṃ ānaitvā yajitabbaṭṭhānabhūtau| āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahauti, mahāphalabhāvakaraṇaina dakkhiṇāhutīnaṃ paṭiggaṇhakau| paṭiggaṇhittha dakkhiṇanti jīvakaina upanītaṃ yāgukhaj‍jādibhaidaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ paṭiggahaisi|

atha khau bhagavā katabhattakic‍cau āyasmantaṃ cūḷapanthakaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘anumaudanaṃ karauhī’’ti| sau sinairuṃ gahaitvā mahāsamuddaṃ manthaintau viya pabhin‍napaṭisambhidāppattatāya taipiṭakaṃ buddhavacanaṃ saṅkhaubhaintau satthu ajjhāsayaṃ gaṇhantau anumaudanaṃ akāsi| tathā upanissayasampan‍naupi  cāyamāyasmā tathārūpāya kammapilautikāya paribādhitau catuppadikaṃ gāthaṃ catūhipi māsaihi gahaituṃ nāsakkhi| taṃ panassa upanissayasampattiṃ aulaukaitvā satthā pubbacariyānurūpaṃ yaunisaumanasikārai niyaujaisi| tathā hi bhagavā tadā jīvakassa nivaisanai nisin‍nau aiva ‘‘cūḷapanthakassa cittaṃ samāhitaṃ, vīthipaṭipan‍nā vipassanā’’ti ñatvā yathānisin‍nauva attānaṃ dassaitvā, ‘‘panthaka, yadipāyaṃ pilautikā saṃkiliṭṭhā rajānukiṇṇā, itau pana añ‍ñau aiva ariyassa vinayai saṃkilaisau rajau cāti dassaintau –

‘‘rāgau rajau na ca pana raiṇu vuc‍cati, rāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ rajauti|

aitaṃ  rajaṃ vippajahitvā bhikkhavau, viharanti tai vītarajassa sāsanai||

‘‘dausau rajau…pai॰… sāsanai||

‘‘mauhau rajau…pai॰… vītarajassa sāsanai’’ti|| –

imā tissau aubhāsagāthā abhāsi| gāthāpariyausānai cūḷapanthakau abhiñ‍ñāpaṭisambhidāparivāraṃ arahattaṃ pāpuṇīti|

cūḷapanthakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. kappatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

nānākulamalasampuṇṇautiādikā  āyasmatau kappatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau siddhatthassa bhagavatau kālai vibhavasampan‍nai kulai nibbattitvā pitu ac‍cayaina viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau nānāvirāgavaṇṇavicittaihi vatthaihi anaikavidhaihi ābharaṇaihi nānāvidhaihi maṇiratanaihi bahuvidhaihi pupphadāmamālādīhi ca kapparukkhaṃ nāma alaṅkaritvā taina satthu thūpaṃ pūjaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai magadharaṭṭhai maṇḍalikarājakulai nibbattitvā pitu ac‍cayaina raj‍jai patiṭṭhitau kāmaisu ativiya rattau giddhau hutvā viharati| taṃ satthā mahākaruṇāsamāpattitau vuṭṭhāya laukaṃ vaulaukaintau ñāṇajālai pañ‍ñāyamānaṃ disvā, ‘‘kiṃ nu khau bhavissatī’’ti āvaj‍jaintau, ‘‘aisa mama santikai asubhakathaṃ sutvā kāmaisu virattacittau hutvā pabbajitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇissatī’’ti ñatvā ākāsaina tattha gantvā –

567.

 ‘‘nānākulamalasampuṇṇau, mahāuk‍kārasambhavau|

candanikaṃva paripak‍kaṃ, mahāgaṇḍau mahāvaṇau||

568.

 ‘‘pubbaruhirasampuṇṇau, gūthakūpaina gāḷhitau|

āpaupaggharaṇau kāyau, sadā sandati pūtikaṃ||

569.

 ‘‘saṭṭhikaṇḍarasambandhau, maṃsalaipanalaipitau|

cammakañ‍cukasan‍naddhau, pūtikāyau niratthakau||

570.

 ‘‘aṭṭhisaṅghātaghaṭitau, nhārusuttanibandhanau|

naikaisaṃ saṃgatībhāvā, kappaiti iriyāpathaṃ||

571.

 ‘‘dhuvappayātau maraṇāya, mac‍curājassa santikai|

idhaiva chaḍḍayitvāna, yainakāmaṅgamau narau||

572.

 ‘‘avij‍jāya  nivutau kāyau, catuganthaina ganthitau|

aughasaṃsīdanau kāyau, anusayājālamautthatau||

573.

 ‘‘pañ‍canīvaraṇai yuttau, vitak‍kaina samappitau|

taṇhāmūlainānugatau, mauhacchādanachāditau||

574.

 ‘‘aivāyaṃ  vattatai kāyau, kammayantaina yantitau|

sampatti ca vipatyantā, nānābhāvau vipaj‍jati||

575.

 ‘‘yaimaṃ kāyaṃ mamāyanti, andhabālā puthuj‍janā|

vaḍḍhainti kaṭasiṃ ghauraṃ, ādiyanti punabbhavaṃ||

576.

 ‘‘yaimaṃ kāyaṃ vivaj‍jainti, gūthalittaṃva pan‍nagaṃ|

bhavamūlaṃ vamitvāna, parinibbissantināsavā’’ti|| –

imāhi gāthāhi tassa asubhakathaṃ kathaisi| sau satthu sammukhā anaikākāravaukāraṃ yāthāvatau sarīrasabhāvavibhāvanaṃ asubhakathaṃ sutvā sakaina kāyaina aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau saṃviggahadayau satthāraṃ vanditvā, ‘‘labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍ja’’nti pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| satthā samīpai ṭhitamañ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘gaccha, bhikkhu, imaṃ pabbājaitvā upasampādaitvā ānaihī’’ti| sau taṃ tacapañ‍cakakammaṭṭhānaṃ datvā pabbājaisi| sau khuraggaiyaiva saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ taira 1.4.102-107) –

‘‘siddhatthassa bhagavatau, thūpasaiṭṭhassa sammukhā|

vicittadussai lagaitvā, kapparukkhaṃ ṭhapaisahaṃ||

‘‘yaṃ  yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

saubhayantau mama dvāraṃ, kapparukkhau patiṭṭhati||

‘‘ahañ‍ca parisā caiva, yai kaici mamavassitā|

tamhā dussaṃ gahaitvāna, nivāsaima mayaṃ sadā||

‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ rukkhaṃ ṭhapayiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kapparukkhassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘itau ca sattamai kappai, sucaiḷā aṭṭha khattiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ  pana patvā laddhūpasampadau satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau tā aiva gāthā abhāsi| tainaiva tā thairagāthā nāma jātā|
