tattha nānākulamalasampuṇṇauti, nānākulaihi nānābhāgaihi malaihi sampuṇṇau, kaisalaumādinānāvidhaasucikauṭṭhāsabharitauti atthau| mahāuk‍kārasambhavauti, uk‍kārau vuc‍cati vac‍cakūpaṃ| yattakavayā mātā, tattakaṃ kālaṃ kāraparisaiditavac‍cakūpasadisatāya mātu kucchi idha ‘‘mahāuk‍kārau’’ti adhippaitau| sau kucchi sambhavau uppattiṭṭhānaṃ aitassāti mahāuk‍kārasambhavau| candanikaṃvāti candanikaṃ nāma ucchiṭṭhaudakagabbhamalādīnaṃ chaḍḍanaṭṭhānaṃ, yaṃ jaṇṇumattaṃ asucibharitampi hauti, tādisanti atthau| paripak‍kanti, pariṇataṃ purāṇaṃ| taina yathā caṇḍālagāmadvārai nidāghasamayai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai udakaina samupabyūḷhamuttakarīsaaṭṭhicammanhārukhaṇḍakhaiḷasiṅghāṇikādinānākuṇapabharitaṃ kaddamaudakāluḷitaṃ  katipayadivasātik‍kamaina saṃjāta kimikulākulaṃ sūriyātapasantāpakuthitaṃ upari phaiṇapubbuḷakāni muñ‍cantaṃ abhinīlavaṇṇaṃ paramaduggandhaṃ jaigucchaṃ candanikāvāṭaṃ naiva upagantuṃ, na daṭṭhuṃ araharūpaṃ hutvā tiṭṭhati, tathārūpauyaṃ kāyauti dassaiti| sadā dukkhatāmūlayaugatau asucipaggharaṇatau uppādajarāmaraṇaihi uddhumāyanaparipac‍canabhij‍janasabhāvattā ca mahantau gaṇḍau viyāti mahāgaṇḍau| sabbatthakamaiva dukkhavaidanānubaddhattā gaṇḍānaṃ sahanatau asucivissandanatau ca mahantau vaṇau viyāti mahāvaṇau gūthakūpaina gāḷitauti, vac‍cakūpaina vac‍cainaiva vā bharitau| ‘‘gūthakūpanigāḷhitau’’tipi pāḷi, vac‍cakūpatau nikkhantauti atthau| āpaupaggharaṇau kāyau, sadā sandati pūtikanti, ayaṃ kāyau āpaudhātuyā sadā paggharaṇasīlau, tañ‍ca khau pittasaimhasaidamuttādikaṃ pūtikaṃ asuciṃyaiva sandati, na kadāci sucinti atthau|

saṭṭhikaṇḍarasambandhauti, gīvāya uparimabhāgatau paṭṭhāya sarīraṃ vinaddhamānā sarīrassa purimapacchimadakkhiṇavāmapassaisu pac‍caikaṃ pañ‍ca pañ‍ca katvā vīsati, hatthapādai vinaddhamānā taisaṃ purimapacchimapassaisu pañ‍ca pañ‍ca katvā cattālīsāti  saṭṭhiyā kaṇḍaraihi mahānhārūhi sabbasau baddhau vinaddhauti saṭṭhikaṇḍarasambandhau| maṃsalaipanalaipitauti, maṃsasaṅkhātaina laipanaina littau, navamaṃsapaisisatānulittauti atthau| cammakañ‍cukasan‍naddhauti, cammasaṅkhātaina kañ‍cukaina sabbasau aunaddhau pariyaunaddhau paricchin‍nau| pūtikāyauti, sabbasau pūtigandhikau kāyau| niratthakauti, nippayaujanau| añ‍ñaisañhi pāṇīnaṃ kāyau cammādiviniyaugaina siyā sappayaujanau, na tathā manussakāyauti| aṭṭhisaṅghātaghaṭitauti, atiraikatisatānaṃ aṭṭhīnaṃ saṅghātaina ghaṭitau sambandhau| nhārusuttanibandhanauti, suttasadisaihi navahi nhārusataihi nibandhitau| naikaisaṃ saṃgatībhāvāti, catumahābhūtajīvitindriyaassāsapassāsaviñ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ samavāyasambandhaina suttamairakasamavāyaina yantaṃ viya ṭhānādiiriyāpathaṃ kappaiti|

dhuvappayātau maraṇāyāti, maraṇassa atthāya aikantagamanau, nibbattitau paṭṭhāya maraṇaṃ pati pavattau| tatau aiva mac‍curājassa maraṇassa  santikai ṭhitau| idhaiva chaḍḍayitvānāti, imasmiṃyaiva laukai kāyaṃ chaḍḍaitvā, yathārucitaṭṭhānagāmī ayaṃ sattau, tasmā ‘‘pahāya gamanīyau ayaṃ kāyau’’ti aivampi saṅgau na kātabbauti dassaiti|

avij‍jāya nivutauti, avij‍jānīvaraṇaina nivutau paṭicchāditādīnavau, añ‍ñathā kau aittha saṅgaṃ janaiyyāti adhippāyau| catuganthaināti, abhijjhākāyaganthādinā catubbidhaina ganthaina ganthitau, ganthaniyabhāvaina vinaddhitau| aughasaṃsīdanauti, aughaniyabhāvaina kāmaughādīsu catūsu aughaisu saṃsīdanakau| appahīnabhāvaina santānai anu anu saintīti anusayā, kāmarāgādayau anusayā| taisaṃ jālaina autthatau abhibhūtauti anusayājālamautthatau| makārau padasandhikarau, gāthāsukhatthaṃ dīghaṃ katvā vuttaṃ| kāmacchandādinā pañ‍cavidhaina nīvaraṇaina yuttau adhimuttauti pañ‍canīvaraṇai yuttau, karaṇatthai bhummavacanaṃ|

kāmavitak‍kādinā  micchāvitak‍kaina samappitau samassitauti vitak‍kaina samappitau| taṇhāmūlainānugatauti, taṇhāsaṅkhātaina bhavamūlaina anubaddhau| mauhacchādanachāditauti, sammauhasaṅkhātaina āvaraṇaina paliguṇṭhitau| sabbamaitaṃ saviñ‍ñāṇakaṃ karajakāyaṃ sandhāya vadati| saviñ‍ñāṇakau hi attabhāvau ‘‘ucchin‍nabhavanaittikau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa kāyau tiṭṭhati, ayañ‍caiva kāyau bahiddhā ca nāmarūpa’’ntiādīsu (dī॰ ni॰ 1.1.147) kāyauti vuc‍cati, aivāyaṃ vattatai kāyauti  aivaṃ ‘‘nānākulamalasampuṇṇau’’tiādinā ‘‘avij‍jāya nivutau’’tiādinā ca vuttappakāraina ayaṃ kāyau vattati, vattantau ca kammayantaina sukataduk‍kaṭaina kammasaṅkhātaina yantaina yantitau saṅghaṭitau| yathā vā khaimantaṃ gantuṃ na sak‍kauti, tathā saṅkhaubhitau sugatiduggatīsu vattati paribbhamati| sampatti ca vipatyantāti yā aittha sampatti, sā vipattipariyausānā| sabbañhi yaubbanaṃ jarāpariyausānaṃ, sabbaṃ āraugyaṃ byādhipariyausānaṃ, sabbaṃ jīvitaṃ maraṇapariyausānaṃ, sabbau samāgamau viyaugapariyausānau| taināha ‘‘nānābhāvau vipaj‍jatī’’ti| nānābhāvauti, vinābhāvau vippayaugau, sau kadāci vippayuñ‍jakassa vasaina, kadāci vippayuñ‍jitabbassa vasaināti vividhaṃ paj‍jati pāpuṇīyati|

yaimaṃ kāyaṃ mamāyantīti yai andhabālā puthuj‍janā aivaṃ asubhaṃ anic‍caṃ adhuvaṃ dukkhaṃ asāraṃ imaṃ kāyaṃ ‘‘mama ida’’nti gaṇhantā mamāyanti chandarāgaṃ uppādainti, tai jātiādīhi nirayādīhi ca ghauraṃ bhayānakaṃ apaṇḍitaihi abhiramitabbatau kaṭasisaṅkhātaṃ saṃsāraṃ punappunaṃ jananamaraṇādīhi vaḍḍhainti, taināha ‘‘ādiyanti punabbhava’’nti|

yaimaṃ kāyaṃ vivaj‍jainti, gūthalittaṃva pan‍naganti yathā nāma  purisau sukhakāmau jīvitukāmau gūthagataṃ āsīvisaṃ disvā jigucchaniyatāya vā sappaṭibhayatāya vā vivaj‍jaiti na al‍līyati, aivamaivaṃ yai paṇḍitā kulaputtā asucibhāvaina jaigucchaṃ anic‍cādibhāvaina sappaṭibhayaṃ imaṃ kāyaṃ vivaj‍jainti chandarāgappahānaina pajahanti| tai bhavamūlaṃ avij‍jaṃ bhavataṇhañ‍ca vamitvā chaḍḍaitvā ac‍cantamaiva pahāya tatau aiva sabbasau anāsavā saupādisaisāya anupādisaisāya ca nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyissantīti|

kappatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. vaṅgantaputtaupasainatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

vivittaṃ  appanigghausantiādikā āyasmatau upasainatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai haṃsavatīnagarai kulagaihai nibbattitvā vayappattau satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ suṇamānau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ samantapāsādikānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā satthu adhikārakammaṃ katvā taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaitvā yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ  buddhuppādai nālakagāmai rūpasārībrāhmaṇiyā kucchimhi nibbatti, upasainautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau tayau vaidai uggahaitvā satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā upasampadāya aikavassikau ‘‘ariyagabbhaṃ vaḍḍhaimī’’ti aikaṃ kulaputtaṃ attanau santikai upasampādaitvā taina saddhiṃ satthu santikaṃ gatau| satthārā cassa tassa avassikassa bhikkhunau saddhivihārikabhāvaṃ sutvā, ‘‘atilahuṃ khau tvaṃ, maughapurisa, bāhul‍lāya āvattau’’ti (mahāva॰ 75) garahitau| ‘‘idānāhaṃ yadipi parisaṃ nissāya satthārā garahitau, parisaṃyaiva pana nissāya satthu pāsaṃsaupi bhavissāmī’’ti vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.2.86-96) –

‘‘padumuttaraṃ bhagavantaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

pabbhāramhi nisīdantaṃ, upagacchiṃ naruttamaṃ||

‘‘kaṇikārapupphaṃ disvā, vaṇṭai chaitvānahaṃ tadā|

alaṅkaritvā chattamhi, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

‘‘piṇḍapātañ‍ca pādāsiṃ, paraman‍naṃ subhaujanaṃ|

buddhaina navamai tattha, samaṇai aṭṭha bhaujayiṃ||

‘‘anumaudi mahāvīrau, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

iminā chattadānaina, paraman‍napavaicchanā||

‘‘taina cittappasādaina, sampattimanubhaussasi|

chattiṃsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

‘‘aikavīsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘sāsanai dibbamānamhi, manussattaṃ gamissati|

tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau||

‘‘upasainauti nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau|

samantapāsādikattā, aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaissati||

‘‘carimaṃ vattatai mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhanaṃ||

‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā sayampi sabbai dhutaṅgadhammai samādāya vattati, añ‍ñaipi tadatthāya samādapaiti, taina naṃ bhagavā samantapāsādikānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi| sau aparaina samayaina kausambiyaṃ kalahai  uppan‍nai bhikkhusaṅghai ca dvidhābhūtai aikaina bhikkhunā taṃ kalahaṃ parivaj‍jitukāmaina ‘‘aitarahi khau kalahau uppan‍nau, saṅghau dvidhābhūtau, kathaṃ nu khau mayā paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti puṭṭhau vivaikavāsatau paṭṭhāya tassa paṭipattiṃ kathaintau –

577.

 ‘‘vivittaṃ appanigghausaṃ, vāḷamiganisaivitaṃ|

saivai saināsanaṃ bhikkhu, paṭisal‍lānakāraṇā||

578.

 ‘‘saṅkārapuñ‍jā āhatvā, susānā rathiyāhi ca|

tatau saṅghāṭikaṃ katvā, lūkhaṃ dhāraiyya cīvaraṃ||

579.

 ‘‘nīcaṃ manaṃ karitvāna, sapadānaṃ kulā kulaṃ|

piṇḍikāya carai bhikkhu, guttadvārau susaṃvutau||

580.

 ‘‘lūkhainapi vā santussai, nāñ‍ñaṃ patthai rasaṃ bahuṃ|

rasaisu anugiddhassa, jhānai na ramatī manau||

581.

 ‘‘appicchau caiva santuṭṭhau, pavivittau vasai muni|

asaṃsaṭṭhau gahaṭṭhaihi, anāgāraihi cūbhayaṃ||

582.

 ‘‘yathā  jaḷau va mūgau va, attānaṃ dassayai tathā|

nātivailaṃ sambhāsaiyya, saṅghamajjhamhi paṇḍitau||

583.

 ‘‘na sau upavadai kañ‍ci, upaghātaṃ vivaj‍jayai|

saṃvutau pātimaukkhasmiṃ, mattañ‍ñū cassa bhaujanai||

584. ‘‘suggahītanimittassa, cittassuppādakauvidau|

samathaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, kālaina ca vipassanaṃ||

585.

 ‘‘vīriyasātac‍casampan‍nau, yuttayaugau sadā siyā|

na ca appatvā dukkhantaṃ, vissāsaṃ aiyya paṇḍitau||

586.

 ‘‘aivaṃ viharamānassa, suddhikāmassa bhikkhunau|

khīyanti āsavā sabbai, nibbutiñ‍cādhigacchatī’’ti|| –

imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha vivittanti, janavivittaṃ suñ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñādiṃ| appanigghausanti, nissaddaṃ saddasaṅghaṭṭanarahitaṃ| vāḷamiganisaivitanti, sīhabyagghadīpivāḷamigaihi caritaṃ| imināpi janavivaikaṃyaiva dassaiti pantasaināsanabhāvadīpanatau| saināsananti, sayituṃ āsayituñ‍ca yuttabhāvaina  vasanaṭṭhānaṃ idha saināsananti adhippaitaṃ| paṭisal‍lānakāraṇāti, paṭisal‍lānanimittaṃ, nānārammaṇatau nivattaitvā kammaṭṭhānaiyaiva cittassa paṭi paṭi sammadaiva al‍līyanatthaṃ|

aivaṃ bhāvanānurūpaṃ saināsanaṃ niddisantau saināsanai santausaṃ dassaitvā idāni cīvarādīsupi taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘saṃkārapuñ‍jā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha  saṃkārapuñ‍jāti saṃkārānaṃ puñ‍jaṃ saṃkārapuñ‍jaṃ, tatau kacavaraṭṭhānā| āhatvāti āharitvā| tatauti tathā āhaṭacauḷakkhaṇḍaihi| karaṇai hi idaṃ nissak‍kavacanaṃ lūkhanti satthalūkharajanalūkhādinā lūkhaṃ avaṇṇāmaṭṭhaṃ| dhāraiyyāti nivāsanādivasaina pariharaiyya, aitaina cīvarasantausaṃ vadati|

nīca manaṃ karitvānāti ‘‘antamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, jīvikāna’’ntiādikaṃ (itivu॰ 91; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.80) sugatauvādaṃ anussaritvā nihatamānadappaṃ cittaṃ katvā| sapadānanti gharaisu  avakhaṇḍarahitaṃ; anugharanti atthau| taināha ‘‘kulā kula’’nti| kulā kulanti kulatau kulaṃ, kulānupubbiyā gharapaṭipāṭiyāti atthau| piṇḍikāyāti missakabhikkhāya, iminā piṇḍapātasantausaṃ vadati| guttadvārauti supihitacakkhādidvārau| susaṃvutauti hatthakuk‍kuc‍cādīnaṃ abhāvaina suṭṭhu saṃvutau|

lūkhainapi vāti apisaddau samuc‍cayai, vā-saddau vikappai| ubhayainapi lūkhainapi appainapi yaina kainaci sulabhaina itarītaraina santussai samaṃ sammā tussaiyya| taināha ‘‘nāñ‍ñaṃ patthai rasaṃ bahu’’nti| nāñ‍ñaṃ patthai rasaṃ bahunti attanā yathāladdhatau añ‍ñaṃ madhurādirasaṃ bahuṃ paṇītañ‍ca na patthaiyya na pihaiyya, iminā gilānapac‍cayaipi santausau dassitau hauti| rasaisu gaidhavāraṇatthaṃ pana kāraṇaṃ vadantau rasaisu anugiddhassa, jhānai na ramatī manau’’ti āha| indriyasaṃvarampi aparipūraintassa kutau vikkhittacittasamādhānanti adhippāyau|

aivaṃ catūsu pac‍cayaisu sal‍laikhapaṭipattiṃ dassaitvā idāni avasiṭṭhakathāvatthūni dassaituṃ ‘‘appicchau caivā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha appicchauti, anicchau catūsu pac‍cayaisu icchārahitau, taina catubbidhapac‍cayaisu taṇhuppādavikkhambhanamāha| santuṭṭhauti, catūsu pac‍cayaisu yathālābhasantausādinā santuṭṭhau| yau hi –

‘‘atītaṃ nānusaucaiyya, nappajappaiyyanāgataṃ|

pac‍cuppan‍naina yāpaiyya, sau ‘santuṭṭhau’ti pavuc‍catī’’ti||

pavivittauti  gaṇasaṅgaṇikaṃ pahāya kāyaina pavivittau vūpakaṭṭhau| cittavivaikādikai hi paratau vakkhati| vasaiti sabbattha yaujaitabbaṃ| maunaiyyadhammasaman‍nāgamaina muni| asaṃsaṭṭhauti dassanasavanasamul‍lapanasambhaugakāyasaṃsaggānaṃ abhāvaina  asaṃsaṭṭhau yathāvuttasaṃsaggarahitau| ubhayanti, gahaṭṭhaihi anāgāraihi cāti ubhayaihipi asaṃsaṭṭhau| karaṇai hi idaṃ pac‍cattavacanaṃ|

attānaṃ dassayai tathāti ajaḷau amūgaupi samānau yathā jaḷau vā mūgau vā, tathā attānaṃ dassaiyya, aitaina pāgabbiyappahānamāha| jaḷau va mūgau vāti ca gāthāsukhatthaṃ rassattaṃ kataṃ, samuc‍cayatthau ca  vāsaddau| nātivailaṃ sambhāsaiyyāti ativailaṃ atik‍kantapamāṇaṃ na bhāsaiyya, mattabhāṇī assāti atthau| saṅghamajjhamhīti bhikkhusaṅghai, janasamūhai vā|

na sau upavadai kañ‍cīti sau yathāvuttapaṭipattikau bhikkhu hīnaṃ vā majjhimaṃ vā uk‍kaṭṭhaṃ vā yaṃkiñ‍ci na vācāya upavadaiyya| upaghātaṃ vivaj‍jayaiti kāyaina upaghātaṃ parivihaiṭhanaṃ vaj‍jaiyya| saṃvutau pātimaukkhasminti pātimaukkhamhi pātimaukkhasaṃvarasīlai saṃvutau assa, pātimaukkhasaṃvaraina pihitakāyavācau siyāti atthau| mattañ‍ñū cassa bhaujanaiti pariyaisanapaṭiggahaṇaparibhaugavissaj‍janaisu bhaujanai pamāṇañ‍ñū siyā|

suggahītanimittassāti ‘‘aivaṃ mai manasi karautau cittaṃ samāhitaṃ ahausī’’ti tadākāraṃ sal‍lakkhaintau suṭṭhu gahitasamādhinimittau assa| ‘‘suggahītanimittau sau’’tipi pāṭhau, sau yaugīti atthau| cittassuppādakauvidauti aivaṃ bhāvayatau cittaṃ līnaṃ hauti, ‘‘aivaṃ uddhata’’nti līnassa uddhatassa ca cittassa uppattikāraṇai kusalau assa| līnai hi cittai dhammavicayavīriyapītisambaujjhaṅgā bhāvaitabbā, uddhatai passaddhisamādhiupaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgā| satisambaujjhaṅgau pana sabbattha icchitabbau| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘yasmiñ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, samayai līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, kālau tasmiṃ samayai dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāyā’’tiādi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.234)| samathaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyyāti samathabhāvanaṃ bhāvaiyya, anuppan‍naṃ samādhiṃ uppādaiyya, uppan‍nañ‍ca yāva vasībhāvappatti, tāva vaḍḍhaiyya byūhaiyyāti atthau| kālaina ca vipassananti yathāladdhaṃ samādhiṃ nikantiyā apariyādānaina hānabhāgiyaṃ ṭhitibhāgiyaṃ vā akatvā nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃva katvā kālaina vipassanañ‍ca anuyuñ‍jaiyya| atha vā kālaina ca vipassananti samathaṃ anuyuñ‍jantau tassa thirībhūtakālai saṅkaucaṃ anāpaj‍jitvā ariyamaggādhigamāya vipassanaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya| yathāha –

‘‘atha  vā samādhilābhaina, vivittasayanaina vā|

bhikkhu vissāsamāpādi, appattau āsavakkhaya’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 271-272)|

taina  vuttaṃ – ‘‘vīriyasātac‍casampan‍nau’’tiādi| satatabhāvau sātac‍caṃ, vīriyassa sātac‍caṃ, taina sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau, satatapavattavīriyau, nic‍capaggahitavīriyauti atthau| yuttayaugau sadā siyāti sabbakālaṃ bhāvanānuyuttau siyā| dukkhantanti vaṭṭadukkhassa antaṃ pariyausānaṃ niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ appatvā vissāsaṃ na aiyya na gacchaiyya| ‘‘ahaṃ parisuddhasīlau jhānalābhī abhiñ‍ñālābhī vipassanaṃ matthakaṃ pāpaitvā ṭhitau’’ti vā vissaṭṭhau na bhavaiyyāti atthau|

aivaṃ viharamānassāti, aivaṃ vivittasaināsanasaivanādinā vipassanāvasaina yuttayaugatāpariyausānaina vidhinā viharantassa| suddhikāmassāti, ñāṇadassanavisuddhiṃ ac‍cantavisuddhiṃ nibbānaṃ arahattañ‍ca icchantassa| saṃsārai bhayassa ikkhatau bhikkhunau, kāmāsavādayau sabbai āsavā khīyanti khayaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchanti, taisaṃ khayagamanainaiva saupādisaisaanupādisaisapabhaidaṃ duvidhampi nibbānaṃ adhigacchati pāpuṇāti|

aivaṃ thairau tassa bhikkhunau auvādadānāpadaisaina attanā tathāpaṭipan‍nabhāvaṃ dīpaintau añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi|

vaṅgantaputtaupasainatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. (apara)-gautamatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

vijānaiyya sakaṃ atthantiādikā āyasmatau aparassa gautamatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā amhākaṃ bhagavatau uppattitau puraitaramaiva sāvatthiyaṃ udic‍cabrāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā vayappattau tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū hutvā, vādamaggaṃ uggahaitvā attanau vādassa upari uttariṃ vadantaṃ alabhantau taihi taihi viggāhikakathaṃ anuyuttau vicarati| atha amhākaṃ bhagavā laukai uppaj‍jitvā  pavattitavaradhammacak‍kau anupubbaina yasādikai vainaiyyai vinaitvā anāthapiṇḍikassa abhiyācanāya sāvatthiṃ upagacchi| tadā satthu jaitavanapaṭiggahai paṭiladdhasaddhau satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci| satthā  añ‍ñataraṃ piṇḍacārikaṃ bhikkhuṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘bhikkhu, imaṃ pabbājaihī’’ti| sau taina pabbājiyamānau khuraggaiyaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇitvā kausalajanapadaṃ gantvā tattha ciraṃ vasitvā puna sāvatthiṃ pac‍cāgami| taṃ bahū ñātakā brāhmaṇamahāsālā upasaṅkamitvā payirupāsitvā nisin‍nā ‘‘imasmiṃ laukai  bahū samaṇabrāhmaṇā saṃsārai suddhivādā, taisu katamaisaṃ nu khau vādau niyyānikau, kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jantau saṃsāratau sujjhatī’’ti pucchiṃsu| thairau taisaṃ tamatthaṃ pakāsaintau –

587.

 ‘‘vijānaiyya sakaṃ atthaṃ, avalaukaiyyātha pāvacanaṃ|

yañ‍caittha assa patirūpaṃ, sāmañ‍ñaṃ ajjhūpagatassa||

588.

 ‘‘mittaṃ idha ca kalyāṇaṃ, sikkhā vipulaṃ samādānaṃ|

sussūsā ca garūnaṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

589.

 ‘‘buddhaisu sagāravatā, dhammai apaciti yathābhūtaṃ|

saṅghai ca cittīkārau, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

590.

 ‘‘ācāragaucarai yuttau, ājīvau saudhitau agārayhau|

cittassa ca saṇṭhapanaṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

591.

 ‘‘cārittaṃ atha vārittaṃ, iriyāpathiyaṃ pasādaniyaṃ|

adhicittai ca āyaugau, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

592.

 ‘‘ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanāni, pantāni appasaddāni|

bhajitabbāni muninā, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

593.

 ‘‘sīlañ‍ca bāhusac‍cañ‍ca, dhammānaṃ pavicayau yathābhūtaṃ|

sac‍cānaṃ abhisamayau, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

594.

 ‘‘bhāvaiyya ca anic‍canti, anattasañ‍ñaṃ asubhasañ‍ñañ‍ca|

laukamhi ca anabhiratiṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

595.

 ‘‘bhāvaiyya  ca baujjhaṅgai, iddhipādāni indriyāni balāni|

aṭṭhaṅgamaggamariyaṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

596.

 ‘‘taṇhaṃ pajahaiyya muni, samūlakai āsavai padālaiyya|

viharaiyya vippamuttau, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpa’’nti|| –

imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha  vijānaiyya sakaṃ atthanti, viñ‍ñūjātikau purisau attanau atthaṃ yāthāvatau vicāraitvā jānaiyya| vicāraintau ca avalaukaiyyātha pāvacanaṃ idha laukai puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇaihi sammāsambuddhaina ca pavuttaṃ pāvacanaṃ, samayau| tattha yaṃ niyyānikaṃ, taṃ aulaukaiyya pañ‍ñācakkhunā passaiyya| imai hi nānātitthiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā anic‍cai ‘‘nic‍ca’’nti, anattani ‘‘attā’’ti, asuddhimaggañ‍ca ‘‘suddhimaggau’’ti micchābhinivaisinau añ‍ñamañ‍ñañ‍ca viruddhavādā, tasmā naisaṃ vādau aniyyānikau| sammāsambuddhau pana ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā, sabbai dhammā anattā, santaṃ nibbāna’’nti sayambhūñāṇaina yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāya pavaidaiti, tasmā ‘‘tassa  vādau niyyānikau’’ti satthu sāsanamahantataṃ aulaukaiyyāti atthau| yañ‍caittha assa patirūpaṃ, sāmañ‍ñaṃ ajjhūpagatassāti, sāmañ‍ñaṃ samaṇabhāvaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ upagatassa kulaputtassa yaṃ aittha sāsanai pabbajitabhāvai vā patirūpaṃ yuttarūpaṃ sāruppaṃ assa siyā, tampi apalaukaiyya|

kiṃ pana tanti āha ‘‘mittaṃ idha ca kalyāṇa’’ntiādi| imasmiṃ sāsanai kalyāṇamittaṃ saiviyamānaṃ samaṇassa patirūpanti yaujanā| aisa nayau itaraisupi| kalyāṇamittañhi nissāya akusalaṃ pajahati, kusalaṃ bhāvaiti, suddhamattānaṃ pariharati| sikkhā vipulaṃ samādānanti vipulaṃ sikkhāsamādānaṃ, mahatiyā nibbānāvahāya adhisīlādisikkhāya anuṭṭhānanti atthau| sussūsā ca garūnanti garūnaṃ ācariyupajjhāyādīnaṃ kalyāṇamittānaṃ auvādassa sautukamyatā pāricariyā ca| aitanti kalyāṇamittasaivanādi|

buddhaisu sagāravatāti sabbañ‍ñubuddhaisu ‘‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā’’ti gāravayaugau garucittīkārau| dhammai apaciti yathābhūtanti ariyadhammai yāthāvatau  apacāyanaṃ ādaraina abhipūjanaṃ| saṅghaiti ariyasaṅghai| cittīkārauti sak‍kārau sammānanaṃ| aitanti ratanattayagarukaraṇaṃ|

ācāragaucarai yuttauti kāyikavācasikavītik‍kamanasaṅkhātaṃ anācāraṃ, piṇḍapātādīnaṃ atthāya upasaṅkamituṃ ayuttaṭṭhānabhūtaṃ vaisiyādiagaucarañ‍ca pahāya kāyikavācasikaavītik‍kamanasaṅkhātaina ācāraina piṇḍapātādīnaṃ atthāya upasaṅkamituṃ yuttaṭṭhānabhūtaina gaucaraina ca yuttau sampan‍nau, sampan‍naācāragaucarauti atthau| ājīvau saudhitauti vaiḷudānādiṃ buddhapaṭikuṭṭhaṃ anaisanaṃ pahāya anavaj‍juppādai pac‍cayai saivantassa ājīvau saudhitau hauti suvisuddhau, saudhitattā aiva agārayhau viñ‍ñūhi| cittassa ca saṇṭhapananti yathā cakkhādidvāraihi rūpādiārammaṇaisu abhijjhādayau nappavattanti, aivaṃ diṭṭhai diṭṭhamattādivasaina cittassa  sammā ṭhapanaṃ| aitanti ācāragaucarasampatti ājīvapārisuddhi indriyaisu guttadvāratāti aitaṃ tayaṃ|

cārittanti caritvā paripūraitabbasīlaṃ| vārittanti viratiyā akaraṇaina paripūraitabbasīlaṃ| iriyāpathiyaṃ pasādaniyanti paraisaṃ pasādāvahaṃ ākappasampattinimittaṃ iriyāpathanissitaṃ sampajañ‍ñaṃ| adhicittai ca āyaugauti samathavipassanāsu anuyaugau bhāvanā|

ārañ‍ñakānīti arañ‍ñai pariyāpan‍nāni| pantānīti vivittāni|

sīlanti catupārisuddhisīlaṃ| haiṭṭhā hi bhinditvā vuttaṃ, idha abhinditvā  vadati| bāhusac‍canti bahussutabhāvau| sau hi bhāvanānuyaugassa bahukārau, baujjhaṅgakausal‍laanuttarasītibhāvaadhicittayuttatādīsu sammā pavicayabahulassa samathavipassanānuyaugau sampaj‍jati| dhammānaṃ pavicayau yathābhūtanti rūpārūpadhammānaṃ aviparītasalakkhaṇatau sāmañ‍ñalakkhaṇatau ca parivīmaṃsā| iminā adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanamāha| sac‍cānaṃ abhisamayauti dukkhādīnaṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayādivasaina paṭivaidhau|

svāyaṃ sac‍cābhisamayau yathā hauti, taṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘bhāvaiyyā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha bhāvaiyya ca anic‍canti ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’tiādinā (dha॰ pa॰ 277) avibhāgatau  ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍na’’ntiādinā (vibha॰ 2; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.49) vibhāgatau vā sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaiyya uppādaiyya caiva vaḍḍhaiyya cāti atthau| anattasañ‍ñanti, ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti pavattaṃ anattasañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvaiyyāti yaujanā| aivaṃ saisaisupi| asubhasañ‍ñanti, karajakāyai sabbasmimpi vā taibhūmakasaṅkhārai kilaisāsucipaggharaṇatau ‘‘asubhā’’ti pavattasañ‍ñaṃ| dukkhasañ‍ñāparivārā hi ayaṃ, aitainaiva caittha dukkhasañ‍ñāpi gahitāti vaiditabbaṃ| laukamhi ca anabhiratinti sabbalaukai taibhūmakaisu saṅkhāraisu anābhiratisañ‍ñaṃ| aitaina ādīnavānupassanaṃ nibbidānupassanañ‍ca vadati|

aivaṃ pana vipassanābhāvanaṃ anuyuttau taṃ ussuk‍kāpaintau imai dhammai vaḍḍhaiyyāti dassaintau ‘‘bhāvaiyya ca baujjhaṅgai’’ti gāthamāha| tassatthau – baudhiyā satiādisattavidhadhammasāmaggiyā, baudhissa vā taṃsamaṅginau puggalassa aṅgāti baujjhaṅgā, satiādayau dhammā| tai satiādikai sattabaujjhaṅgai, chandaādīni cattāri iddhipādāni, saddhādīni pañ‍cindriyāni, saddhādīniyaiva pañ‍ca  balāni, sammādiṭṭhiādīnaṃ vasaina aṭṭhaṅgaariyamaggañ‍ca| ca-saddaina satipaṭṭhānāni sammappadhānāni ca gahitānīti sabbaipi sattatiṃsappabhaidai baudhipakkhiyadhammai bhāvaiyya uppādaiyya caiva vaḍḍhaiyya ca| tattha yadaitaisaṃ paṭhamamaggakkhaṇai uppādanaṃ, uparimaggakkhaṇai ca vaḍḍhanaṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa bhikkhunau sāruppanti|

aivaṃ baudhipakkhiyasattatiṃsadhammai bhāvaintau yathā maggasac‍caṃ bhāvanābhisamayavasaina abhisamaiti, aivaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ pahānābhisamayavasaina, niraudhasac‍caṃ sacchikiriyābhisamayavasaina abhisamaitīti dassaintau ‘‘taṇhaṃ pajahaiyyā’’ti ausānagāthamāha| tattha taṇhaṃ pajahaiyyāti, kāmataṇhādipabhaidaṃ sabbaṃ taṇhaṃ ariyamaggaina anavasaisatau samucchindaiyya, maunaṃ vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ, taina  saman‍nāgatattā muni| samūlakai āsavai padālaiyyāti kāmarāgānusayādisamūlakai kāmāsavādikai sabbaipi āsavai bhindaiyya samucchindaiyya| viharaiyya vippamuttauti aivaṃ sabbasau kilaisānaṃ pahīnattā sabbadhi vimuttau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggaṃ  niraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikatvā viharaiyya| aitanti yadaitaṃ viharaṇaṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa samitapāpassa bhikkhunau patirūpaṃ sāruppanti atthau|

aivaṃ thairau samaṇasāruppapaṭipattikittanamukhaina sāsanassa niyyānikabhāvaṃ tabbilaumatau bāhirakasamayassa aniyyānikatañ‍ca vibhāvaisi| taṃ sutvā tai brāhmaṇamahāsālā sāsanai abhippasan‍nā saraṇādīsu patiṭṭhahiṃsu|

(apara)-gautamatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

dasakanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

11. aikādasanipātau

1. saṃkic‍catthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

aikādasanipātai  kiṃ tavatthau vanai tātātiādikā āyasmatau saṃkic‍catthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālakulai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| tasmiṃ kucchigataiyaiva mātā byādhitā hutvā kālamakāsi| tassā susānaṃ naitvā jhāpiyamānāya gabbhāsayau na jhāyi| manussā sūlaihi kucchiṃ vijjhantā dārakassa akkhikauṭiṃ pahariṃsu| tai taṃ vijjhitvā aṅgāraihi paṭicchādaitvā pak‍kamiṃsu| kucchipadaisaupi jhāyi, aṅgāramatthakai pana suvaṇṇabimbasadisau dārakau padumagabbhai nipan‍nau viya ahausi| pacchimabhavikasattassa hi sinairunā autthariyamānassapi arahattaṃ appatvā jīvitakkhayau nāma natthi|

punadivasai āḷāhanaṭṭhānaṃ gatā manussā tathānipan‍naṃ dārakaṃ disvā acchariyabbhutacittajātā dārakaṃ ādāya gāmaṃ pavisitvā naimittakai pucchiṃsu| naimittakā ‘‘sacai ayaṃ dārakau agāraṃ ajjhāvasissati, yāva sattamā kulaparivaṭṭā duggatā bhavissanti| sacai pabbajissati, pañ‍cahi samaṇasataihi parivutau vicarissatī’’ti āhaṃsu| ñātakā ‘‘hautu, vaḍḍhitakālai amhākaṃ ayyassa sāriputtatthairassa santikai taṃ pabbājaissāmā’’ti vatvā saṅkunā chin‍nakkhikauṭitāya saṃkic‍cauti vadantā aparabhāgai saṃkic‍cauti vauhariṃsu| sau sattavassikakālai attanau gabbhagatassaiva mātu maraṇaṃ, gabbhai ca attanau  pavattiṃ sutvā saṃvaigajātau ‘‘pabbajissāmī’’ti āha| ñātakā ‘‘sādhu, tātā’’ti dhammasaināpatissa santikaṃ naitvā, ‘‘bhantai, imaṃ pabbājaithā’’ti adaṃsu| thairau taṃ tacapañ‍cakakammaṭṭhānaṃ datvā pabbājaisi| sau khuraggaiyaiva saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ patvā tiṃsamattaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ arañ‍ñai viharantau cai caurahatthatau maucaitvā sayampi tai caurai damaitvā pabbājaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vihārai bahūhi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ viharantau  tai vivādapasutai disvā ‘‘añ‍ñattha gacchāmī’’ti bhikkhū āpucchi| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau, vitthārau pana dhammapadavatthumhi (dha॰ pa॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.saṅkic‍casāmaṇairavatthu)  āgatanayainaiva vaiditabbau| atha naṃ añ‍ñatarau upāsakau upaṭṭhātukāmau āsan‍naṭṭhānai vāsaṃ yācantau –

597.

 ‘‘kiṃ tavatthau vanai tāta, uj‍juhānauva pāvusai|

vairambhā ramaṇīyā tai, pavivaikau hi jhāyina’’nti|| –

paṭhamaṃ gāthamāha| taṃ sutvā thairau –

598.

 ‘‘yathā abbhāni vairambhau, vātau nudati pāvusai|

sañ‍ñā mai abhikiranti, vivaikapaṭisañ‍ñutā||

599.

 ‘‘apaṇḍarau aṇḍasambhavau, sīvathikāya nikaitacārikau|

uppādayataiva mai satiṃ, sandaihasmiṃ virāganissitaṃ||

600.

 ‘‘yañ‍ca añ‍ñai na rakkhanti, yau ca añ‍ñai na rakkhati|

sa vai bhikkhu sukhaṃ saiti, kāmaisu anapaikkhavā||

601.

 ‘‘acchaudikā puthusilā, gaunaṅgalamigāyutā|

ambusaivālasañchan‍nā, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

602.

 ‘‘vasitaṃ mai arañ‍ñaisu, kandarāsu guhāsu ca|

saināsanaisu pantaisu, vāḷamiganisaivitai||

603.

 ‘‘‘imai hañ‍ñantu vajjhantu, dukkhaṃ pappauntu pāṇinau’|

saṅkappaṃ nābhijānāmi, anariyaṃ dausasaṃhitaṃ||

604.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, bhavanaitti samūhatā||

605.

 ‘‘yassa catthāya pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayau||

606.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi  maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, nibbisaṃ bhatakau yathā||

607.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ||

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, sampajānau patissatau’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha kiṃ tavatthau vanaiti kinti liṅgavipal‍lāsaina vuttaṃ| vanai kau tavatthau, kiṃ payaujananti atthau| tātāti daharasāmaṇairatāya naṃ attanau puttaṭṭhānai ṭhapaitvā ālapati| uj‍juhānauva pāvusaiti  uj‍juhānau kira nāma aikau pabbatau, sau pana gahanasañchan‍nau bahusauṇḍikandarau, tahaṃ tahaṃ sandamānasalilau, vassakālai asappāyau, tasmā uj‍juhānau vā pabbatau aitarahi pāvusakālai tava kimatthiyauti atthau| kaici panaittha ‘‘uj‍juhānau nāma aikau sakuṇau sītaṃ na sahati, vassakālai vanagumbai nilīnau acchatī’’ti vadanti| taisaṃ mataina uj‍juhānassa viya sakuṇassa pāvusakālai kau tava atthau vanaiti? vairambhā ramaṇīyā taiti vairambhavātā vāyantā kiṃ tai ramaṇīyāti yaujanā| kaici ‘‘vairambhā nāma aikā pabbataguhā, pabbhārau’’ti ca vadanti| tañ‍ca ṭhānaṃ gamanāgamanayuttaṃ janasambādharahitaṃ chāyūdakasampan‍nañ‍ca, tasmā vairambhā ramaṇīyā, vanai vasituṃ yuttarūpā| kasmā? pavivaikau hi jhāyinaṃ yasmā tādisānaṃ jhāyīnaṃ yattha katthaci pavivaikauyaiva icchitabbau, tasmā ‘‘dūraṃ arañ‍ñaṭṭhānaṃ agantvā vairambhāyaṃ vasa, tātā’’ti vadati| ayañhaittha adhippāyau – yasmā jhāyīnaṃ pavivaikakkhamai nivāsaphāsukai saināsanai laddhaiyaiva jhānādayau sampaj‍janti, na aladdhai, tasmā na aivarūpai sītakālai yattha katthaci vanai vasituṃ sak‍kā, guhāpabbhārādīsu pana sak‍kāti|

aivaṃ upāsakaina vuttai thairau vanādayau aiva maṃ ramaintīti dassaintau ‘‘yathā abbhānī’’tiādimāha| tassatthau – yathā pāvusai kālai abbhāni valāhakāni vairambhavātau nudati khipati nīharati, aivamaiva mai cittaṃ vivaikapaṭisañ‍ñutā sañ‍ñā abhikiranti vivaikaṭṭhānaṃyaiva ākaḍḍhanti|

kiñ‍ca? apaṇḍarau kāḷavaṇṇau, aṇḍasambhavau aṇḍajau kākau, sīvathikāya susānaṭṭhānai, nikaitacārikau tamaiva nivāsanaṭṭhānaṃ katvā vicaraṇakau uppādayataiva mai satiṃ, sandaihasmiṃ virāganissitanti, kāyasmiṃ virāgūpasaṃhitaṃ kāyagatāsatikammaṭṭhānaṃ mayhaṃ uppādayatiyaiva| aikadivasaṃ kira thairau kākaina khaj‍jamānaṃ manussakuṇapaṃ passitvā asubhasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhi, taṃ sandhāya aivamāha| taina kāyai sabbasau chandarāgassa natthitāya vanaiyaiva vasitukāmaumhīti dassaiti| yañ‍cāti ca-saddau samuc‍cayatthau, taina añ‍ñampi mama arañ‍ñavāsakāraṇaṃ suṇāhīti dassaiti| yaṃ pabbajitaṃ maittāvihāritāya alaubhaniyaparikkhāratāya ca rakkhitabbassa abhāvatau añ‍ñai saivakādayau na rakkhanti| yau ca pabbajitau añ‍ñai kainaci kiñ‍canapalibaudhabhūtai na rakkhati tādisānaṃyaiva abhāvatau| sa vai bhikkhu sukhaṃ saitīti, sau bhikkhu samucchin‍nakilaisakāmatāya sabbasau vatthukāmaisu anapaikkhavā apaikkhārahitau yattha katthaci  sukhaṃ saiti| tassa anusaṅkitaparisaṅkitābhāvatau arañ‍ñamhi gāmamhi sadisamaivāti atthau|

idāni  pabbatavanādīnaṃ ramaṇīyataṃ vasitapubbatañ‍ca dassaituṃ ‘‘acchaudikā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha vasitaṃ maiti, vuṭṭhapubbaṃ mayā| vāḷamiganisaivitaiti, sīhabyagghādīhi vāḷamigaihi upasaivitai vanai|

saṅkappaṃ nābhijānāmīti, imai yai kaici pāṇinau sattā ususattiādīhi paharaṇaihi hañ‍ñantu māriyantu muṭṭhippahārādīhi vajjhantu bādhīyantu, añ‍ñaina vā yaina kainaci ākāraina dukkhaṃ pappauntu pāpuṇantūti; aivaṃ dausasaṃhitaṃ paṭighasaṃyuttaṃ tatau aiva anariyaṃ byāpādavihiṃsādippabhaidaṃ pāpasaṅkappaṃ uppāditaṃ nābhijānāmi, micchāvitak‍kau na uppan‍napubbauti maittāvihāritaṃ dassaiti|

idāni ‘‘pariciṇṇau’’tiādinā attanau katakic‍cataṃ dassaiti| tattha pariciṇṇauti upāsitau auvādānusāsanīkaraṇavasaina| auhitauti aurauhitau| garukau bhārauti garutarau khandhabhārau|

nābhinandāmi  maraṇanti ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau mai maraṇaṃ siyā’’ti maraṇaṃ na icchāmi| nābhinandāmi jīvitanti ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau ahaṃ ciraṃ jīvaiyya’’nti jīvitampi na icchāmi| aitaina maraṇai jīvitai ca samānacittataṃ dassaiti| kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmīti parinibbānakālaṃva āgamaimi| nibbisanti nibbisantau, bhatiyā kammaṃ karauntau| bhatakau yathāti yathā bhatakau parassa kammaṃ karauntau kammasiddhiṃ anabhinandantaupi kammaṃ karauntauva divasakkhayaṃ udikkhati, aivaṃ ahampi jīvitaṃ anabhinandantaupi attabhāvassa yāpanaina maraṇaṃ anabhinandantaupi pariyausānakālaṃ paṭikaṅkhāmīti| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

saṃkic‍catthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

aikādasanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

12. dvādasakanipātau

1. sīlavatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

dvādasakanipātai  sīlamaivātiādikā āyasmatau sīlavatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rājagahai bimbisārarañ‍ñau puttau hutvā nibbatti, sīlavātissa nāmaṃ ahausi| taṃ vayappattaṃ rājā ajātasattu māraitukāmau caṇḍaṃ mattahatthiṃ āraupaitvā nānāvidhaihi upāyaihi upak‍kamantaupi māraituṃ nāsakkhi pacchimabhavikassa arahattaṃ appatvā antarā jīvitantarāyābhāvatau| tassa pavattiṃ  disvā bhagavā mahāmauggal‍lānatthairaṃ āṇāpaisi – ‘‘sīlavakumāraṃ ānaihī’’ti| thairau iddhibalaina saddhiṃ hatthinā taṃ ānaisi| kumārau hatthitau auruyha bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| bhagavā tassa ajjhāsayānurūpaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi| sau dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ patvā kausalaraṭṭhai vasati| atha naṃ ajātasattu ‘‘māraithā’’ti purisai āṇāpaisi| tai thairassa santikaṃ gantvā ṭhitā thairaina kathitaṃ dhammakathaṃ sutvā sañ‍jātasaṃvaigā pasan‍nacittā hutvā pabbajiṃsu| thairau taisaṃ –

608.

 ‘‘sīlamaividha sikkhaitha, asmiṃ laukai susikkhitaṃ||

sīlañhi sabbasampattiṃ, upanāmaiti saivitaṃ||

609.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ rakkhaiyya maidhāvī, patthayānau tayau sukhai|

pasaṃsaṃ vittilābhañ‍ca, paic‍ca saggai pamaudanaṃ||

610.

 ‘‘sīlavā hi bahū mittai, sañ‍ñamainādhigacchati|

dussīlau pana mittaihi, dhaṃsatai pāpamācaraṃ||

611.

 ‘‘avaṇṇañ‍ca akittiñ‍ca, dussīlau labhatai narau|

vaṇṇaṃ kittiṃ pasaṃsañ‍ca, sadā labhati sīlavā||

612.

 ‘‘ādi  sīlaṃ patiṭṭhā ca, kalyāṇānañ‍ca mātukaṃ|

pamukhaṃ sabbadhammānaṃ, tasmā sīlaṃ visaudhayai||

613.

 ‘‘vailā ca saṃvaraṃ sīlaṃ, cittassa abhihāsanaṃ|

titthañ‍ca sabbabuddhānaṃ, tasmā sīlaṃ visaudhayai||

614.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ balaṃ appaṭimaṃ, sīlaṃ āvudhamuttamaṃ|

sīlamābharaṇaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, sīlaṃ kavacamabbhutaṃ||

615.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ saitu mahaisakkhau, sīlaṃ gandhau anuttarau|

sīlaṃ vilaipanaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, yaina vāti disaudisaṃ||

616.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ sambalamaivaggaṃ, sīlaṃ pāthaiyyamuttamaṃ|

sīlaṃ saiṭṭhau ativāhau, yaina yāti disaudisaṃ||

617.

 ‘‘idhaiva nindaṃ labhati, paic‍cāpāyai ca dummanau|

sabbattha dummanau bālau, sīlaisu asamāhitau||

618.

 ‘‘idhaiva kittiṃ labhati, paic‍ca saggai ca summanau|

sabbattha sumanau dhīrau, sīlaisu susamāhitau||

619.

 ‘‘sīlamaiva idha aggaṃ, pañ‍ñavā pana uttamau|

manussaisu ca daivaisu, sīlapañ‍ñāṇatau jaya’’nti|| –

imāhi gāthāhi dhammaṃ daisaisi|

tattha sīlamaividha sikkhaitha, asmiṃ laukaiti idhāti, nipātamattaṃ, imasmiṃ sattalaukai atthakāmau kulaputtau cārittavārittādibhaidaṃ āditau sīlamaiva sikkhaiyya, sikkhantau ca naṃ susikkhitaṃ akhaṇḍādibhāvāpādanaina suṭṭhu sikkhitaṃ suparisuddhaṃ paripuṇṇañ‍ca katvā sikkhaiyya| asmiṃ  laukaiti vā imasmiṃ saṅkhāralaukai sikkhitabbadhammaisu sīlaṃ āditau sikkhaiyya| diṭṭhisampattiyāpi sīlassa patiṭṭhābhāvatau āha ‘‘sīlaṃ hī’’tiādi| tattha hīti kāraṇavacanaṃ| yasmā sīlaṃ saivitaṃ paricitaṃ rakkhitaṃ  manussasampatti, dibbasampatti, nibbānasampattīti aitaṃ sabbasampattiṃ taṃsamaṅginau sattassa upanāmaiti āvahati|

sīlaṃ sabbasampattiṃ upanāmaitīti saṅkhaipatau vuttamatthaṃ vitthāratau dassaintau ‘‘sīlaṃ rakkhaiyyā’’tiādimāha| tattha rakkhaiyyāti gaupaiyya| pāṇātipātāditau hi viramantau vattapaṭivattañ‍ca pūraintau paṭipakkhābhibhavanatau taṃ rakkhati nāma| maidhāvīti pañ‍ñavā, idaṃ tassa rakkhanupāyadassanaṃ ñāṇabalaina hissa samādānaṃ avikaupanañ‍ca hauti| patthayānauti icchantau| tayau sukhaiti  tīṇi sukhāni| sukhanimittaṃ vā ‘‘sukha’’nti adhippaitaṃ| pasaṃsanti kittiṃ, viñ‍ñūhi vā pasaṃsanaṃ| vittilābhanti tuṭṭhilābhaṃ| ‘‘vittalābha’’nti ca paṭhanti, dhanalābhanti atthau| sīlavā hi appamattatāya mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchati| paic‍cāti kālaṅkatvā| saggai pamaudananti daivalaukai iṭṭhaihi kāmaguṇaihi, maudanañ‍ca patthayamānauti sambandhau| idhalaukai pasaṃsaṃ vittilābhaṃ paralaukai dibbasampattiyā maudanañ‍ca icchantau sīlaṃ rakkhaiyyāti yaujanā|

sañ‍ñamaināti kāyādīnaṃ saṃyamaina| saṃyatau hi kāyaduc‍caritādīhi kañ‍ci avihaiṭhaintau abhayadānaṃ dadantau piyamanāpatāya mittāni ganthati| dhaṃsataiti apaiti| pāpamācaranti pāṇātipātādipāpakammaṃ karauntau| dussīlañhi puggalaṃ atthakāmā sattā na bhajanti, añ‍ñadatthu parivaj‍jainti|

avaṇṇanti aguṇaṃ, sammukhā garahaṃ vā| akittinti, ayasaṃ asilaukaṃ| vaṇṇanti guṇaṃ| kittinti silaukaṃ patthaṭayasataṃ| pasaṃsanti sammukhā thaumanaṃ|

ādīti mūlaṃ| sīlañhi kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādi| yathāha – ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ādimaiva visaudhaihi kusalaisu dhammaisu| kau cādi kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? sīlañ‍ca suvisuddha’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.369)| patiṭṭhāti adhiṭṭhānaṃ| sīlañhi sabbaisaṃ uttarimanussadhammānaṃ patiṭṭhā| taināha – ‘‘sīlai patiṭṭhāyā’’tiādi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.23; 192; paiṭakau॰ 22; mi॰ pa॰ 2.1.9)| kalyāṇānañ‍ca mātukanti samathavipassanādīnaṃ kalyāṇadhammānaṃ mātubhūtaṃ, janakanti, atthau| pamukhaṃ sabbadhammānanti, sabbaisaṃ pāmauj‍jādīnaṃ anavaj‍jadhammānaṃ pamukhaṃ mukhabhūtaṃ, pavattidvāranti atthau| tasmāti ādibhāvāditau| visaudhayaiti akkhaṇḍādibhāvaina sampādaiyya|

vailāti duc‍caritaihi anatik‍kamanīyaṭṭhaina vailā, sīmāti atthau| vailāyati vā dussilyaṃ calayati viddhaṃsaitīti vailā| saṃvaraṃ sīlaṃ kāyaduc‍caritādīnaṃ uppattidvārassa pidahanatau| abhihāsananti tausanaṃ avippaṭisārahaitutāya cittassābhippamaudanatau| titthañ‍ca sabbabuddhānanti sāvakabuddhā, pac‍caikabuddhā, sammāsambuddhāti sabbaisaṃ buddhānaṃ kilaisamalappavāhanai nibbānamahāsamuddāvagāhaṇai ca titthabhūtañ‍ca|

sīlaṃ balaṃ appaṭimanti mārasainappamaddanai asadisaṃ balaṃ saināthāmau ca| āvudhamuttamanti saṃkilaisadhammānaṃ  chaidanai uttamaṃ paharaṇaṃ| guṇasarīraupasaubhanaṭṭhaina ābharaṇaṃ| saiṭṭhanti sabbakālaṃ uttamaṃ dabbañ‍ca| sapāṇaparittānatau kavacamabbhutaṃ| ‘‘abbhida’’nti ca paṭhanti, abhaij‍janti atthau|

apāyamahaughātik‍kamanai saṃsāramahaughātik‍kamanai ca kilaisaihi asaṃsīdanaṭṭhaina saitu| mahaisakkhauti mahabbalau| gandhau anuttarauti paṭivātaṃ sabbadisāsu vāyanatau anuttarau gandhau sabbajanamanauharattā| taināha ‘‘yaina vāti disaudisa’’nti yaina sīlagandhaina taṃsamaṅgī disaudisaṃ sabbā disā vāyati| ‘‘disaudisā’’tipi pāḷi, dasa disāti atthau|

sambalamaivagganti sambalaṃ nāma puṭabhattaṃ| yathā puṭabhattaṃ gahaitvā maggaṃ gacchantau purisau antarāmaggai jighacchādukkhaina na kilamati, aivaṃ sīlasampan‍naupi suddhaṃ sīlasambalaṃ gahaitvā saṃsārakantāraṃ paṭipan‍nau gatagataṭṭhānai na kilamatīti sīlaṃ aggaṃ sambalaṃ nāma| tathā sīlaṃ pāthaiyyamuttamaṃ caurādīhi asādhāraṇattā tattha tattha icchitabbasampattinipphādanatau ca| atik‍kāmaintau taṃ taṃ ṭhānaṃ yathicchitaṭṭhānaṃ vā vāhaiti sampāpaitīti ativāhau, yānaṃ| kainaci anupaddutaṃ hutvā icchitaṭṭhānappattihaitutāya  sīlaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ ativāhau| yaināti yaina ativāhaina yāti disaudisanti agatiṃ gatiñ‍cāpi taṃ taṃ disaṃ sukhainaiva gacchati|

idhaiva nindaṃ labhatīti idhalaukaipi dummanau rāgādīhi dūsitacittau ‘‘dussīlau pāpadhammau’’ti nindaṃ garahaṃ labhati| paic‍ca paralaukaipi apāyai ‘‘purisattakali avajātā’’tiādinā yamapurisādīhi ca nindaṃ labhati| na kaivalaṃ nindamaiva labhati, atha khau sabbattha dummanau bālau idhalaukai duc‍caritacaraṇaina dūsitacittau paralaukai kammakāraṇādivasaina dukkhuppattiyāti sabbattha bālau dummanau hauti| kathaṃ? sīlaisu asamāhitau sammā sīlaisu na ṭhapitacittau appatiṭṭhitacittau|

idhaiva kittiṃ labhatīti idhalaukaipi sumanau ‘‘sappurisau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau’’ti kittiṃ labhati| paic‍ca paralaukaipi saggai ‘‘ayaṃ sappurisau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau| tathā hi daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapan‍nau’’tiādinā kittiṃ labhati| na kaivalaṃ kittimaiva labhati, atha khau  dhīrau dhitisampan‍nau sīlaisu suṭṭhu samāhitau appitacittau supatiṭṭhitacittau sabbattha idhalaukai sucaritacaraṇaina, paralaukai sampattipaṭilābhaina sumanau saumanassappattau hauti| sīlamaiva idha agganti duvidhaṃ sīlaṃ laukiyaṃ laukuttaranti| tattha laukiyaṃ tāva kāmalaukai khattiyamahāsālādīsu, daivalaukai brahmalaukai ca upapattivisaisaṃ āvahati, lābhībhāvādikassa ca kāraṇaṃ  hauti| laukuttaraṃ pana sakalampi vaṭṭadukkhaṃ atik‍kāmaitīti sīlaṃ aggamaiva| tathā hi vuttaṃ –

‘‘hīnaina brahmacariyaina, khattiyai upapaj‍jati|

majjhimaina ca daivattaṃ, uttamaina visujjhatī’’ti|| (jā॰ 1.8.75)|

ākaṅkhaiyya cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu – ‘‘lābhī assaṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’’nti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.65), ‘‘sīlaisvaivassa paripūrakārī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.65), ‘‘ijjhati, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau caitaupaṇidhi visuddhattā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 8.35; dī॰ ni॰ 3.337) ca|

laukuttarasīlassa  pana sabbasau pahīnapaṭipakkhassa sattamabhavatau paṭṭhāya saṃsāradukkhaṃ vinivattaintassa aggabhāvai vattabbamaiva natthi| pañ‍ñavā pana uttamauti ‘‘pañ‍ñavā pana puggalau uttamau paramau saiṭṭhauyaivā’’ti puggalādhiṭṭhānaina pañ‍ñāya aiva saiṭṭhabhāvaṃ vadati| idāni sīlapañ‍ñānaṃ saiṭṭhabhāvaṃ kic‍catau dassaintau ‘‘sīlapañ‍ñāṇatau jaya’’nti āha| jayanti ca liṅgavipal‍lāsau daṭṭhabbau, ahūti vā vacanasaisau| tattha pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñāṇaṃ, sīlatau pañ‍ñāṇatau ca paṭipakkhajayau| na hi sīlaina vinā pañ‍ñā sambhavati, pañ‍ñāya ca vinā sīlaṃ kic‍cakaraṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaupakārakañ‍caitaṃ| vuttañhi ‘‘sīlaparidhautā pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñāparidhautaṃ sīla’’nti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.317) manussaisu ca daivaisūti idaṃ naisaṃ ṭhānavisaisadassanaṃ| tattha hi tāni savisaisāni vattanti, samādhi panaittha sīlapakkhikau pañ‍ñāya adhiṭṭhānabhāvatau, pañ‍ñāpakkhikau vā bhāvaitabbatau sīlādhiṭṭhānatau ca|

aivaṃ thairau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sīlamukhaina dhammaṃ daisaintau attanau suvisuddhasīlādiguṇatādīpanaina añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi|

sīlavatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. sunītatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

nīcai  kulamhītiādikā āyasmatau sunītatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu  katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinitvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau buddhassa suñ‍ñakālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā vayappattau bālajanaihi saddhiṃ kīḷāpasutau hutvā vicarantau aikaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ gāmai piṇḍāya carantaṃ disvā, ‘‘kiṃ tuyhaṃ sabbasau vaṇitasarīrassa viya sakalaṃ kāyaṃ paṭicchādaitvā bhikkhācaraṇaina, nanu nāma kasivāṇij‍jādīhi jīvikā kappaitabbā? tāni cai kātuṃ na sak‍kausi, gharai gharai muttakarīsādīni nīharantau pacchā vatthusaudhanaina jīvāhī’’ti ak‍kausi| sau taina kammaina nirayai pac‍citvā tassaiva kammassa vipākāvasaisaina manussalaukaipi bahūni jātisatāni  pupphachaḍḍakakulai nibbattitvā tathā jīvikaṃ kappaisi| imasmiñ‍ca buddhuppādai pupphachaḍḍakakulai aiva nibbattau uk‍kārasaudhanakammaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti ghāsacchādanamattampi alabhantau|

atha bhagavā pacchimayāmai buddhāciṇṇaṃ mahākaruṇāsamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jitvā tatau vuṭṭhāya buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau sunītassa hadayabbhantarai ghaṭai padīpaṃ viya paj‍jalantaṃ arahattūpanissayaṃ disvā vibhātāya rattiyā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau| yassaṃ vīthiyaṃ sunītau uk‍kārasaudhanakammaṃ karauti, taṃ vīthiṃ paṭipaj‍ji| sunītaupi tattha tattha vighāsuc‍cārasaṅkārādikaṃ rāsiṃ katvā piṭakaisu pakkhipitvā kājainādāya pariharantau bhikkhusaṅghaparivutaṃ satthāraṃ āgacchantaṃ disvā sāraj‍jamānau sambhamākulahadayau gamanamaggaṃ nilīyanaukāsañ‍ca alabhantau kājaṃ bhittipassai ṭhapaitvā aikaina passaina anupavisantau viya bhittiṃ al‍līnau pañ‍jalikau aṭṭhāsi| ‘‘bhittichiddaina apak‍kamitukāmau ahausī’’tipi vadanti|

satthā tassa samīpaṃ patvā ‘‘ayaṃ attanau kusalamūlasañ‍cauditaṃ upagataṃ maṃ sāraj‍jamānau jātiyā kammassa ca nihīnatāya sammukhībhāvampi laj‍jati, handassa vaisāraj‍jaṃ uppādaissāmī’’ti karavīkarutamañ‍junā sakalanagaranin‍nādavara-gambhīraina brahmassaraina ‘‘sunītā’’ti ālapitvā ‘‘kiṃ imāya dukkhajīvikāya pabbajituṃ sakkhissatī’’ti āha| sunītau taina satthu vacanaina amataina viya abhisittau uḷāraṃ pītisaumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintau ‘‘bhagavā, sacai mādisāpi idha pabbaj‍jaṃ labhanti, kasmāhaṃ na pabbajissāmi, pabbājaitha maṃ bhagavā’’ti āha| satthā ‘‘aihi, bhikkhū’’ti āha| sau tāvadaiva aihibhikkhubhāvaina pabbaj‍jaṃ upasampadañ‍ca labhitvā iddhimayapattacīvaradharau vassasaṭṭhikatthairau viya hutvā satthu santikai aṭṭhāsi| bhagavā taṃ vihāraṃ naitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ ācikkhi| sau paṭhamaṃ aṭṭha samāpattiyau, pañ‍ca ca abhiñ‍ñāyau nibbattaitvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taṃ sak‍kādayau daivā brahmānau ca upasaṅkamitvā namassiṃsu| taina vuttaṃ –

‘‘tā  daivatā sattasatā uḷārā, brahmā ca indau upasaṅkamitvā|

ājānīyaṃ jātijarābhibhūtaṃ, sunītaṃ namassanti pasan‍nacittā’’tiādi||

bhagavā taṃyaiva daivasaṅghapurakkhataṃ disvā sitaṃ katvā pasaṃsantau ‘‘tapaina brahmacariyainā’’ti gāthāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| atha naṃ sambahulā bhikkhū sīhanādaṃ nadāpaitukāmā, ‘‘āvusau sunīta, kasmā kulā tvaṃ pabbajitau, kathaṃ vā pabbajitau, kathañ‍ca sac‍cāni paṭivijjhī’’ti pucchiṃsu| sau taṃ sabbaṃ pakāsaintau –

620.

 ‘‘nīcai kulamhi jātauhaṃ, daliddau appabhaujanau|

hīnakammaṃ mamaṃ āsi, ahausiṃ pupphachaḍḍakau||

621.

 ‘‘jigucchitau manussānaṃ, paribhūtau ca vambhitau|

nīcaṃ manaṃ karitvāna, vandissaṃ bahukaṃ janaṃ||

622.

 ‘‘athaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ|

pavisantaṃ mahāvīraṃ, magadhānaṃ puruttamaṃ||

623.

 ‘‘nikkhipitvāna byābhaṅgiṃ, vandituṃ upasaṅkamiṃ|

mamaiva anukampāya, aṭṭhāsi purisuttamau||

624.

 ‘‘vanditvā satthunau pādai, aikamantaṃ ṭhitau tadā|

pabbaj‍jaṃ ahamāyāciṃ, sabbasattānamuttamaṃ||

625.

 ‘‘tatau  kāruṇikau satthā, sabbalaukānukampakau|

‘aihi bhikkhū’ti maṃ āha, sā mai āsūpasampadā||

626. ‘‘sauhaṃ aikau arañ‍ñasmiṃ, viharantau atanditau|

akāsiṃ satthu vacanaṃ, yathā maṃ auvadī jinau||

627.

 ‘‘rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ, pubbajātimanussariṃ|

rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ, dibbacakkhuṃ visaudhayiṃ|

rattiyā pacchimai yāmai, tamaukhandhaṃ padālayiṃ||

628.

 ‘‘tatau  ratyāvivasānai, sūriyuggamanaṃ pati| (jā॰ 1.11.79)|

indau brahmā ca āgantvā, maṃ namassiṃsu pañ‍jalī||

629.

 ‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

yassa tai āsavā khīṇā, dakkhiṇaiyyausi mārisa||

630.

 ‘‘tatau  disvāna maṃ satthā, daivasaṅghapurakkhataṃ|

sitaṃ pātukaritvāna, imamatthaṃ abhāsatha||

631.

 ‘‘tapaina brahmacariyaina, saṃyamaina damaina ca|

aitaina brāhmaṇau hauti, aitaṃ brāhmaṇamuttama’’nti|| –

imāhi gāthāhi sīhanādaṃ nadi|

tattha nīcaiti lāmakai sabbanihīnai| uc‍canīcabhāvau hi nāma sattānaṃ upādāyupādāya, ayaṃ pana sabbanihīnai puk‍kusakulai uppan‍nataṃ dassaintau ‘‘nīcai kulamhi jātau’’ti āha| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘nīcaiti lāmakai sabbanihīnai’’ti| daliddauti duggatau, daliddāpi kaici kadāci ghāsacchādanassa lābhinau, akasiravuttinau haunti, ahaṃ pana sabbakālaṃ kasiravuttitāya hīnau uddhanaṃ upaṭṭhapitaukkhalikau dassanayuttaṃ thaivakampi apassiṃ yaivāti dassaintau ‘‘appabhaujanau’’ti āha| nīcakulikā daliddāpi kaici anīcakammājīvā haunti, mayhaṃ pana na tathāti dassaintau āha ‘‘hīnakammaṃ mamaṃ āsī’’ti| kīdisanti cai? ahausiṃ pupphachaḍḍakau, hatthavikalassa hatthavāti viya upacāravasaināyaṃ imassa samañ‍ñā ahausi yadidaṃ ‘‘pupphachaḍḍakau’’ti| milātapupphasantharavaṇṇatāya vā uk‍kārabhūmiyā aivaṃ vuttau|

jigucchitauti jātiyā caiva kammunā ca hīḷitau| manussānanti manussaihi| paribhūtauti avañ‍ñātau| vambhitauti khuṃsitau| nīcaṃ manaṃ karitvānāti añ‍ñai manussai sinairuṃ viya ukkhipitvā taisaṃ pādapaṃsutaupi attānaṃ nihīnaṃ katvā pavattiyā nīcaṃ nihīnaṃ manaṃ katvā| vandissaṃ  bahukaṃ jananti puthumahājanaṃ diṭṭhadiṭṭhakālai vandiṃ sirasi añ‍jaliṃ karauntau paṇāmiṃ|

athāti adhikārantaradīpanai nipātau| addasāsinti addakkhiṃ| magadhānanti magadhā nāma jānapadinau rājakumārā, taisaṃ nivāsau aikaupi janapadau ruḷhiyā ‘‘magadhāna’’nti vuttau, magadhajanapadassāti atthau| puruttamanti uttamaṃ nagaraṃ|

byābhaṅginti kājaṃ| pabbaj‍jaṃ ahamāyācinti, ‘‘sunīta, pabbajituṃ sakkhissasī’’ti satthārā aukāsai katai ahaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ ayāciṃ| āsūpasampadāti ‘‘aihi, bhikkhū’’ti satthu vacanamattaina āsi upasampadā| yathā maṃ auvadīti ‘‘aivaṃ samathapubbaṅgamaṃ vipassanaṃ bhāvaihī’’ti yathā maṃ auvadi, tathā satthunau vacanaṃ akāsiṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃ| rattiyātiādi tassā paṭipattiyā rasadassanaṃ| tattha pubbainivāsañāṇaṃ  anāgataṃsañāṇañ‍ca bahukic‍canti ‘‘paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ  majjhimaṃ yāma’’nti ac‍cantasaṃyaugavasaina upayaugavacanaṃ vuttaṃ| na tathā āsavakkhayañāṇaṃ aikābhisamayavasaina pavattanatauti ‘‘pacchimai yāmai’’ti bhummavasaina vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| indauti sak‍kau daivarājā| brahmāti mahābrahmā| indabrahmaggahaṇaina añ‍ñaisaṃ kāmadaivānaṃ brahmūnañ‍ca āgamanaṃ vuttamaivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| uk‍kaṭṭhaniddaisau haisa yathā ‘‘rājā āgatau’’ti| namassiṃsūti kāyaina vācāya ca namak‍kāraṃ akaṃsu|

tattha kāyaina kataṃ namak‍kāraṃ dassaintau ‘‘pañ‍jalī’’ti vatvā vācāya kataṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘namau tai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| daivasaṅghapurakkhatanti daivaggahaṇaina upapattidaivabhāvatau brahmānaupi gahitā| sitaṃ pātukaritvānāti attanau auvādassa mahapphalataṃ daivabrahmūnañ‍ca guṇasampattiṃ nissāya satthā sitaṃ pātvākāsi| pātukarauntau ca na añ‍ñai viya dantai vidaṃsaiti, mukhādhānaṃ pana thaukaṃ vivarati, tattakaina ca abhibhūtadibbaphalikamuttarasmiyau avahasitatārakāsasimarīciyau susuk‍kadāṭhasambhavā ghanarasmiyau nikkhamitvā tikkhattuṃ satthu mukhaṃ padakkhiṇaṃ karaunti, taṃ disvā pacchatau gacchantāpi satthā sitaṃ pātvākāsīti sañ‍jānanti|

tapaināti  indriyasaṃvaraina, ‘‘dhutadhammasamādānainā’’ti kaici| saṃyamaināti sīlaina| damaināti pañ‍ñāya| brahmacariyaināti avasiṭṭhasaiṭṭhacariyāya| aitaināti yathāvuttaina tapādinā| brāhmaṇau hauti bāhitapāpabhāvatau| aitanti tapādi yathāvuttaṃ| brāhmaṇamuttamanti uttamaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ, brāhmaṇaisu vā uttamaṃ sabbasaiṭṭhaṃ, ahūti vacanasaisau| brāhmaṇanti vā brahmañ‍ñamāha, aivaṃ uttamaṃ brahmañ‍ñaṃ, na jac‍cādīti adhippāyau| na hi jātikulapadaisagauttasampattiādayau ariyabhāvassa kāraṇaṃ, adhisīlasikkhādayau aiva pana kāraṇaṃ| taināha –

‘‘yathā saṅkāraṭhānasmiṃ, ujjhitasmiṃ mahāpathai|

padumaṃ tattha jāyaitha, sucigandhaṃ manauramaṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ saṅkārabhūtaisu, andhabhūtai puthuj‍janai|

atiraucati pañ‍ñāya, sammāsambuddhasāvakau’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 58-59)|

aivaṃ thairau taihi bhikkhūhi pucchitamatthaṃ imāhi gāthāhi vissaj‍jaintau sīhanādaṃ nadīti|

sunītatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

dvādasakanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

13. tairasanipātau

1. sauṇakauḷivisatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

tairasanipātai  yāhu raṭṭhaitiādikā āyasmatau sauṇassa kauḷivisassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacini| ayaṃ kira anaumadassissa bhagavatau kālai mahāvibhavau saiṭṭhi hutvā upāsakaihi saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gantvā satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā pasan‍namānasau satthu caṅkamanaṭṭhānai sudhāya parikammaṃ kāraitvā nānāvaṇṇaihi pupphaihi santharitvā upari nānāvirāgavatthaihi vitānaṃ bandhāpaisi, tathā satthu bhikkhusaṅghassa ca dīghasālaṃ kāraitvā niyyādaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai haṃsavatīnagarai saiṭṭhikulai nibbatti, sirivaḍḍhautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau vihāraṃ gantvā satthu santikai dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ āraddhavīriyānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā, sayampi taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaintau sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ pavattaitvā paṇidhānamakāsi| satthāpi tassa patthanāya samijjhanabhāvaṃ disvā byākaritvā pak‍kāmi|

saupi yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau parinibbutai kassapadasabalai anuppan‍nai amhākaṃ bhagavati bārāṇasiyaṃ kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau gaṅgātīrai paṇṇasālaṃ karitvā aikaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ taimāsaṃ catūhi pac‍cayaihi sak‍kac‍caṃ upaṭṭhahi| pac‍caikabuddhau vuṭṭhavassau paripuṇṇaparikkhārau gandhamādanamaiva gatau| saupi kulaputtau yāvajīvaṃ tattha puñ‍ñāni katvā tatau cavitvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau amhākaṃ bhagavatau kālai campānagarai usabhasaiṭṭhissa gaihai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| tassa paṭisandhiggahaṇatau paṭṭhāya saiṭṭhissa mahābhaugakkhandhau abhivaḍḍhi| tassa jātadivasai sakalanagarai mahāsak‍kārasampan‍nau ahausi, tassa pubbai pac‍caikabuddhassa satasahassagghanikarattakambalaparic‍cāgaina suvaṇṇavaṇṇau sukhumālatarau ca attabhāvau ahausi, tainassa sauṇauti nāmaṃ akaṃsu| mahatā parivāraina vaḍḍhati, tassa hatthapādatalāni bandhujīvakapupphavaṇṇāni ahaisuṃ, satavihatakappāsassa viya samphassau pādatalaisu maṇikuṇḍalāvaṭṭavaṇṇāni laumāni  jāyiṃsu| vayappattassa tassa tiṇṇaṃ utūnaṃ anucchavikai tayau pāsādai kāraitvā nāṭakāni upaṭṭhāpaisuṃ| sau tattha mahatiṃ sampattiṃ anubhavantau daivakumārau viya paṭivasati|

atha  amhākaṃ satthari sabbañ‍ñutaṃ patvā pavattitavaradhammacak‍kai rājagahaṃ upanissāya viharantai bimbisārarañ‍ñā pak‍kausāpitau asītiyā gāmikasahassaihi saddhiṃ rājagahaṃ āgatau, satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau mātāpitarau anujānāpaitvā sāsanai pabbajitvā laddhūpasampadau satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā, janasaṃsaggapariharaṇatthaṃ sītavanai vasantau ‘‘mama sarīraṃ sukhumālaṃ, na ca sak‍kā sukhainaiva sukhaṃ adhigantuṃ, kāyaṃ kilamaitvā samaṇadhammaṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti ṭhānacaṅkamamaiva adhiṭṭhāya, padhānamanuyuñ‍jantau pādatalaisu phauṭaisu uṭṭhahitaisupi vaidanaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā daḷhaṃ vīriyaṃ karauntau ac‍cāraddhavīriyatāya visaisaṃ nibbattaituṃ asak‍kauntau, ‘‘aivaṃ vāyamantaupi ahaṃ maggaṃ vā phalaṃ vā nibbattaituṃ na sak‍kaumi, kiṃ mai pabbaj‍jāya, hīnāyāvattitvā bhaugai ca bhuñ‍jissāmi, puñ‍ñāni ca karissāmī’’ti cintaisi| satthā tassa cittācāraṃ ñatvā tattha gantvā vīṇūpamauvādaina auvaditvā vīriyasamatāyaujanavidhiṃ dassaintau kammaṭṭhānaṃ saudhaitvā gijjhakūṭaṃ gatau| sauṇatthairaupi satthu sammukhā auvādaṃ labhitvā vīriyasamataṃ yaujaitvā vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattai patiṭṭhāsi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.5.25-53) –

‘‘anaumadassissa muninau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

sudhāya laipanaṃ katvā, caṅkamaṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘nānāvaṇṇaihi pupphaihi, caṅkamaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ|

ākāsai vitānaṃ katvā, bhaujayiṃ buddhamuttamaṃ||

‘‘añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, abhivādaitvāna subbataṃ|

dīghasālaṃ bhagavatau, niyyādaisimahaṃ tadā||

‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

paṭiggahaisi bhagavā, anukampāya cakkhumā||

‘‘paṭiggahaitvāna  sambuddhau, dakkhiṇaiyyau sadaivakai|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

‘‘yau sau haṭṭhaina cittaina, dīghasālaṃ adāsi mai|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘imassa  mac‍cukālamhi, puñ‍ñakammasamaṅginau|

sahassayuttassarathau, upaṭṭhissati tāvadai||

‘‘taina yānainayaṃ pausau, daivalaukaṃ gamissati|

anumaudissarai daivā, sampattai kulasambhavai||

‘‘mahārahaṃ byamhaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, ratanamattikalaipanaṃ|

kūṭāgāravarūpaitaṃ, byamhaṃ ajjhāvasissati||

‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

pañ‍cavīsati kappāni, daivarājā bhavissati||

‘‘sattasattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

yasaudharasanāmā tai, sabbaipi aikanāmakā||

‘‘dvai sampattī anubhautvā, vaḍḍhaitvā puñ‍ñasañ‍cayaṃ|

aṭṭhavīsatikappamhi, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

‘‘tatrāpi byamhaṃ pavaraṃ, vissakammaina māpitaṃ|

dasasaddāvivittaṃ taṃ, puramajjhāvasissati||

‘‘aparimaiyyai itau kappai, bhūmipālau mahiddhikau|

auk‍kākau nāma nāmaina, rājā raṭṭhai bhavissati||

‘‘sauḷasitthisahassānaṃ, sabbāsaṃ pavarā ca sā|

abhijātā khattiyānī, nava puttai janaissati||

‘‘nava puttai janaitvāna, khattiyānī marissati|

taruṇī ca piyā kañ‍ñā, mahaisittaṃ karissati||

‘‘auk‍kākaṃ tausayitvāna, varaṃ kañ‍ñā labhissati|

varaṃ laddhāna sā kañ‍ñā, puttai pabbājayissati||

‘‘pabbājitā  ca tai sabbai, gamissanti naguttamaṃ|

jātibhaidabhayā sabbai, bhaginīhi vasissarai||

‘‘aikā ca kañ‍ñā byādhīhi, bhavissati parikkhatā|

mā nau jāti pabhij‍jīti, nikhaṇissanti khattiyā||

‘‘khattiyau nīharitvāna, tāya saddhiṃ vasissati|

bhavissati tadā bhaidau, auk‍kākakulasambhavau||

‘‘taisaṃ pajā bhavissanti, kauḷiyā nāma jātiyā|

tattha mānusakaṃ bhaugaṃ, anubhaussatinappakaṃ||

‘‘tamhā kāyā cavitvāna, daivalaukaṃ gamissati|

tatrāpi pavaraṃ byamhaṃ, labhissati manauramaṃ||

‘‘daivalaukā  cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

āgantvāna manussattaṃ, sauṇau nāma bhavissati||

‘‘āraddhavīriyau pahitattau, padahaṃ satthu sāsanai|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

‘‘anantadassī bhagavā, gautamau sakyapuṅgavau|

visaisañ‍ñū mahāvīrau, aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaissati||

‘‘vuṭṭhamhi daivai caturaṅgulamhi, tiṇai anilairitaaṅgaṇamhi|

ṭhatvāna yaugassa payuttatādinau, tatauttariṃ pāramatā na vij‍jati||

‘‘uttamai damathai dantau, cittaṃ mai supaṇīhitaṃ|

bhārau mai auhitau sabbau, nibbutaumhi anāsavau||

‘‘aṅgīrasau mahānāgau, abhijātauva kaisarī|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā udānavasaina añ‍ñābyākaraṇavasaina ca –

632.

 ‘‘yāhu raṭṭhai samuk‍kaṭṭhau, rañ‍ñau aṅgassa paddhagū|

svāj‍ja dhammaisu uk‍kaṭṭhau, sauṇau dukkhassa pāragū||

633.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca chindai pañ‍ca jahai, pañ‍ca cuttari bhāvayai|

pañ‍casaṅgātigau bhikkhu, aughatiṇṇauti vuc‍cati||

634.

 ‘‘un‍naḷassa pamattassa, bāhirāsassa bhikkhunau|

sīlaṃ samādhi pañ‍ñā ca, pāripūriṃ na gacchati||

635.

 ‘‘yañhi kic‍caṃ apaviddhaṃ, akic‍caṃ pana karīyati|

un‍naḷānaṃ pamattānaṃ, taisaṃ vaḍḍhanti āsavā||

636.

 ‘‘yaisañ‍ca susamāraddhā, nic‍caṃ kāyagatā sati|

akic‍caṃ tai na saivanti, kic‍cai sātac‍cakārinau|

satānaṃ sampajānānaṃ, atthaṃ gacchanti āsavā||

637.

 ‘‘ujumaggamhi  akkhātai, gacchatha mā nivattatha|

attanā caudayattānaṃ, nibbānamabhihārayai||

638.

 ‘‘ac‍cāraddhamhi  vīriyamhi, satthā laukai anuttarau|

vīṇaupamaṃ karitvā mai, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā|

tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, vihāsiṃ sāsanai ratau||

639.

 ‘‘samathaṃ paṭipādaisiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

640.

 ‘‘naikkhammai adhimuttassa, pavivaikañ‍ca caitasau|

abyābajjhādhimuttassa, upādānakkhayassa ca||

641.

 ‘‘taṇhakkhayādhimuttassa, asammauhañ‍ca caitasau|

disvā āyatanuppādaṃ, sammā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati||

642.

 ‘‘tassa sammā vimuttassa, santacittassa bhikkhunau|

katassa paticayau natthi, karaṇīyaṃ na vij‍jati||

643.

 ‘‘sailau  yathā aikaghanau, vātaina na samīrati|

aivaṃ rūpā rasā saddā, gandhā phassā ca kaivalā||

644.

 ‘‘iṭṭhā dhammā aniṭṭhā ca, nappavaidhainti tādinau|

ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ visañ‍ñuttaṃ, vayañ‍cassānupassatī’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha yāhu raṭṭhai samuk‍kaṭṭhauti yau ahu ahausi aṅgaraṭṭhai asītiyā gāmikasahassaihi bhaugasampattiyā issariyasampattiyā ca sammā ativiya uk‍kaṭṭhau saiṭṭhau| rañ‍ñau aṅgassa paddhagūti catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi parisāya rañ‍janaṭṭhaina rañ‍ñau aṅgādhipatinau bimbisārassa parivārabhūtau gahapativisaisau tassa raṭṭhai kuṭumbikau ahūti yaujaitabbaṃ| svāj‍ja dhammaisu uk‍kaṭṭhauti sau sauṇau aj‍jaitarahi laukuttaradhammaisu uk‍kaṭṭhau jātau, gihikālaipi kaihici uk‍kaṭṭhauyaiva hutvā idāni pabbajitakālaipi uk‍kaṭṭhauyaiva hautīti attānamaiva paraṃ viya dassaiti| dukkhassa pāragūti sakalassa vaṭṭadukkhassa pāraṃ pariyantaṃ gatau, aitaina dhammaisu uk‍kaṭṭhauti avisaisaina vuttaṃ uk‍kaṭṭhabhāvaṃ visaisaiti arahattādhigamadīpanatau|

idāni yāya paṭipattiyā dukkhapāragū jātau, añ‍ñāpadaisaina taṃ dassaintau ‘‘pañ‍ca chindai’’ti gāthamāha| tassatthau – apāyakāmasugatisampāpakāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni purisau satthaina pādai baddharaj‍jukaṃ viya haiṭṭhimaina maggattayaina chindaiyya, rūpārūpabhavasampāpakāni pañ‍ca uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni purisau gīvāya baddharaj‍jukaṃ viya aggamaggaina jahaiyya, chindaiyya, taisaṃ pana uddhambhāgiyasaṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya pañ‍ca saddhādīni indriyāni  uttari bhāvayai  bhāvaiyya| aivaṃbhūtau pana bhikkhu rāgasaṅgau dausamauhamānadiṭṭhisaṅgauti pañ‍can‍naṃ saṅgānaṃ atik‍kamanaina pañ‍casaṅgātigau hutvā, kāmaughau, bhavaughau, diṭṭhaughau, avij‍jaughauti catun‍naṃ aughānaṃ tiṇṇattā aughatiṇṇauti vuc‍cati|

ayañ‍ca aughataraṇapaṭipattisīlādīnaṃ pāripūriyāva hauti, sīlādayau ca mānādippahānaina pāripūriṃ gacchanti, na añ‍ñathāti dassaintau ‘‘un‍naḷassā’’ti gāthamāha| tattha un‍naḷassāti uggatatucchamānassa| mānau  hi un‍namanākāravuttiyā tucchabhāvaina naḷau viyāti ‘‘naḷau’’ti vuc‍cati| pamattassāti sativaussaggaina pamādaṃ āpan‍nassa| bāhirāsassāti bāhiraisu āyatanaisu āsāvatau, kāmaisu avītarāgassāti atthau| sīlaṃ samādhi pañ‍ñā ca, pāripūriṃ na gacchatīti tassa sīlādīnaṃ paṭipakkhasaivinau laukiyaupi tāva sīlādiguṇau pāripūriṃ na gacchati, pagaiva laukuttarau|

tattha kāraṇamāha ‘‘yañhi kic‍ca’’ntiādinā| bhikkhunau hi pabbajitakālatau paṭṭhāya aparimāṇasīlakkhandhagaupanaṃ arañ‍ñavāsau dhutaṅgapariharaṇaṃ bhāvanārāmatāti aivamādi kic‍caṃ nāma| yaihi pana idaṃ yathāvuttaṃ attanau kic‍caṃ, taṃ apaviddhaṃ akaraṇaina chaḍḍitaṃ| akic‍canti pattamaṇḍanaṃ cīvarakāyabandhanaaṃsabaddhachattupāhanatālavaṇṭadhammakaraṇamaṇḍananti aivamādi parikkhāramaṇḍanaṃ pac‍cayabāhuliyanti aivamādi bhikkhunau akic‍caṃ nāma, taṃ kayirati, taisaṃ mānanaḷaṃ ukkhipitvā caraṇaina un‍naḷānaṃ sativaussaggaina pamattānaṃ cattāraupi āsavā vaḍḍhanti|

yaisaṃ pana pañ‍ñādiguṇau vaḍḍhati, tai dassaituṃ ‘‘yaisa’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha susamāraddhāti suṭṭhu paggahitā| kāyagatā satīti, kāyānupassanābhāvanā| akic‍caṃ taiti tai aitaṃ pattamaṇḍanādiakic‍caṃ| na saivantīti na karaunti| kic‍caiti, pabbajitakālatau paṭṭhāya kattabbai aparimāṇasīlakkhandhagaupanādikai| sātac‍cakārinauti satatakārinau taisaṃ satiyā avippavāsaina satānaṃ sātthakasampajañ‍ñaṃ, sappāyasampajañ‍ñaṃ, gaucarasampajañ‍ñaṃ, asammauhasampajañ‍ñanti catūhi sampajañ‍ñaihi sampajānānaṃ, cattāraupi āsavā atthaṃ gacchanti parikkhayaṃ abhāvaṃ gacchantīti atthau|

idāni attanau santikai ṭhitabhikkhūnaṃ auvādaṃ daintau ‘‘ujumaggamhī’’ti gāthamāha| tattha ujumaggamhi akkhātaiti antadvayaparivaj‍janaina kāyavaṅkādippahānaina ca ujukai majjhimapaṭipadābhūtai ariyamaggai satthārā bhāsitai| gacchathāti paṭipaj‍jatha| mā nivattathāti antarā  vausānaṃ māpaj‍jatha| attanā caudayattānanti idha  atthakāmau kulaputtau apāyabhayapac‍cavaikkhaṇādinā  attanāva attānaṃ caudaintau| nibbānamabhihārayaiti, attānaṃ nibbānaṃ abhiharaiyya upanaiyya, yathā naṃ sacchikarauti, tathā paṭipaj‍jaiyyāti atthau|

idāni mayāpi aivamaiva paṭipan‍nanti, attanau paṭipattiṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘ac‍cāraddhamhī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| ac‍cāraddhamhi vīriyamhīti vipassanaṃ bhāvaintaina mayā samādhinā vīriyaṃ samarasaṃ akatvā ativiya vīriyai paggahitai| ac‍cāraddhavīriyatā cassa haiṭṭhā vuttāyaiva| vīṇaupamaṃ karitvā maiti āyasmatau sauṇassa ‘‘yai khau kaici bhagavatau sāvakā āraddhavīriyā viharanti| ahaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau, atha ca pana mai nānupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, tasmāhaṃ vibbhamissāmī’’ti cittai uppan‍nai satthā iddhiyā tassa sammukhai attānaṃ dassaitvā ‘‘kasmā tvaṃ, sauṇa, ‘vibbhamissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaisi, kusalau tvaṃ pubbai agāriyabhūtau vīṇāya tantissarai’’ti pucchitvā taina ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti vuttai ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa? yadā tai vīṇāya tantiyau ac‍cāyatā haunti, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti kammañ‍ñā vāti? nau haitaṃ, bhantai! taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, yadā tai vīṇāya tantiyau atisithilā haunti, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti kammañ‍ñā vāti? nau haitaṃ, bhantai| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, yadā pana tai vīṇāya tantiyau naiva ac‍cāyatā haunti, nātisithilā samai guṇai patiṭṭhitā, api nu tai vīṇā tasmiṃ samayai saravatī vā hauti kammañ‍ñā vāti? aivaṃ, bhantai| aivamaiva khau, sauṇa, ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ uddhac‍cāya saṃvattati, atilīnavīriyaṃ kausaj‍jāya saṃvattati, tasmātiha tvaṃ, sauṇa, vīriyasamataṃ adhiṭṭhaha, indriyānañ‍ca samataṃ paṭivijjhā’’ti aivaṃ vīṇaṃ upamaṃ katvā pavattitaina vīṇaupamauvādaina mayhaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi| tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvāti tassa bhagavatau vacanaṃ vīṇaupamauvādaṃ sutvā antarā uppan‍naṃ vibbhamitukāmataṃ pahāya satthu sāsanai ratau abhiratau vihariṃ|

viharantau ca samathaṃ paṭipādaisiṃ vīriyasamataṃ yaujaintau saddhāpañ‍ñānaṃ viya samādhivīriyānaṃ samarasataṃ uppādaintau jhānādhiṭṭhānaṃ vipassanāsamādhiṃ  sampādaisiṃ vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaisiṃ| tattha payaujanaṃ āha ‘‘uttamatthassa pattiyā’’ti| uttamatthassa pattiyāti arahattādhigamāyāti atthau|

idāni yathā paṭipan‍nassa samathavipassanā sampaj‍jiṃsu, taṃ  añ‍ñāpadaisaina dassaintau ‘‘naikkhammai’’tiādimāha| tattha naikkhammaiti pabbaj‍jādikai kāmanissaraṇai| adhimuttassāti tattha nin‍napauṇapabbhārabhāvaina  yuttappayuttassa, paṭhamaṃ tāva pabbaj‍jābhimukhau hutvā kāmai pahāya pabbajitvā ca sīlavisaudhanaṃ arañ‍ñavāsau dhutaṅgapariharaṇaṃ bhāvanābhiyaugauti aivamādīsu anavaj‍jadhammaisu yuttappayuttassāti atthau| pavivaikañ‍ca caitasauti caitasau pavivaikañ‍ca adhimuttassa aivaṃ naikkhammādhimuttassa satau catuk‍kapañ‍cakajjhānānaṃ nibbattanaina vivaikai yuttassa payuttassa| abyābajjhādhimuttassāti abyābajjhai nidukkhatāya adhimuttassa jhānasamāpattiyau nibbattaitvā samathasukhai yuttappayuttassa| upādānakkhayassa cāti catun‍nampi upādānānaṃ khayantai arahattai adhimuttassa| bhummatthai hi aitaṃ sāmivacanaṃ| taṃ yathādhigataṃ jhānaṃ pādakaṃ katvā arahattādhigamāya vipassanaṃ anuyuñ‍jantassāti atthau|

taṇhakkhayādhimuttassāti taṇhā khīyati aitthāti taṇhakkhayau, nibbānaṃ, tasmiṃ adhimuttassa upādiṃ bhayatau, anupādiñ‍ca khaimatau dassanaina niraudhai nin‍napauṇapabbhārassa| asammauhañ‍ca caitasauti asammauhasampajañ‍ñavasaina cittassa asammauhapavattiṃ sammauhasamucchindanaina vā cittassa asammauhabhūtaṃ ariyamaggaṃ adhimuttassa| disvā āyatanuppādanti cakkhādīnaṃ āyatanānaṃ yathāsakapac‍cayaihi khaṇai khaṇai uppādaṃ, tappaṭipakkhatau niraudhañ‍ca vipassanāpañ‍ñāsahitāya maggapañ‍ñāya disvā dassanahaitu sammā cittaṃ vimuc‍catīti sammā haitunā ñāyaina maggapaṭipāṭiyā sabbāsavatau cittaṃ vimuc‍cati|

‘‘tassa sammā vimuttassā’’tiādīsu ayaṃ saṅkhaipatthau – tassa vuttanayaina sammadaiva sabbasaṃkilaisatau vimuttassa, tatau aiva ac‍cantupasamaina santacittassa khīṇāsavabhikkhunau katassa kusalassa akusalassa vā upacayau natthi maggainaiva samugghātitattā, pariñ‍ñādibhaidaṃ karaṇīyaṃ  na vij‍jati katakic‍cattā| aivaṃ bhūtassa yathā aikaghanau sailau pabbatau pakativātaina na samīrati na saṃkampati, aivaṃ iṭṭhā ca aniṭṭhā ca rūpādayau ārammaṇadhammā tādinau tādibhāvappattassa ṭhitaṃ anaijaṃ pahīnasabbasaukatāya visaṃyuttaṃ cittaṃ nappavaidhanti na cālainti| assa ca ārammaṇadhammassa kālaina kālaṃ phalasamāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jitvā vipassantau vayaṃ niraudhaṃ khaṇai khaṇai bhij‍janasabhāvaṃ anupassatīti añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi|

sauṇakauḷivisatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

tairasanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

14. cuddasakanipātau

1. khadiravaniyaraivatatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

cuddasakanipātai  yadā ahantiādikā āyasmatau khadiravaniyaraivatatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? kāmañ‍cimassa thairassa gāthā haiṭṭhā aikakanipātai (thairagā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1. khadiravaniyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā) āgatā| tattha panassa attanau bhāginaiyyaisu satijananamattaṃ dassitanti tassā aikakanipātai saṅgahau katau| imā pana thairassa pabbajitakālatau paṭṭhāya yāva parinibbānā paṭipattipakāsitā gāthā imasmiṃ cuddasakanipātai saṅgahaṃ āraupitā| tattha aṭṭhuppatti haiṭṭhā vuttāyaiva| ayaṃ pana visaisau – thairau kira arahattaṃ patvā kālaina kālaṃ satthu dhammasaināpatippabhūtīnaṃ mahāthairānañ‍ca upaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā katipāhamaiva tattha vasitvā khadiravanamaiva pac‍cāgantvā phalasamāpattisukhaina brahmavihāraihi ca vītināmaiti| aivaṃ gacchantai kālai jiṇṇau vuḍḍhau vayau anuppattau ahausi| sau aikadivasaṃ buddhupaṭṭhānaṃ gacchantau antarāmaggai sāvatthiyā avidūrai arañ‍ñai vasi| taina ca samayaina caurā nagarai katakammā ārakkhamanussaihi anubandhā palāyantā thairassa samīpai gahitabhaṇḍaṃ chaḍḍaitvā palāyiṃsu| manussā anudhāvantā thairassa samīpai bhaṇḍaṃ disvā thairaṃ bandhitvā ‘‘caurau’’ti sañ‍ñāya gahaitvā rañ‍ñau dassaisuṃ, ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, caurau’’ti| rājā thairaṃ muñ‍cāpaitvā, ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, tumhaihi idaṃ caurikakammaṃ kataṃ vā, nau vā’’ti pucchi| thairau kiñ‍cāpi jātitau paṭṭhāya attanā tādisaṃ na katapubbaṃ, taṃ pabbajitatau paṭṭhāya pana akatabhāvassa, sabbasau kilaisānaṃ samucchin‍nattā tādisassa karaṇai abhabbatāya pakāsanatthaṃ samīpai ṭhitānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ rañ‍ñau ca dhammaṃ daisaintau –

645.

 ‘‘yadā ahaṃ pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

nābhijānāmi saṅkappaṃ, anariyaṃ dausasaṃhitaṃ||

646.

 ‘‘‘imai hañ‍ñantu vajjhantu, dukkhaṃ pappauntu pāṇinau’|

saṅkappaṃ nābhijānāmi, imasmiṃ dīghamantarai||

647.

 ‘‘maittañ‍ca  abhijānāmi, appamāṇaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

anupubbaṃ paricitaṃ, yathā buddhaina daisitaṃ||

648.

 ‘‘sabbamittau sabbasakhau, sabbabhūtānukampakau|

maittacittañ‍ca bhāvaimi, abyāpaj‍jaratau sadā||

649.

 ‘‘asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, cittaṃ āmaudayāmahaṃ|

brahmavihāraṃ bhāvaimi, akāpurisasaivitaṃ||

650.

 ‘‘avitak‍kaṃ samāpan‍nau, sammāsambuddhasāvakau|

ariyaina tuṇhībhāvaina, upaitau hauti tāvadai||

651.

 ‘‘yathāpi  pabbatau sailau, acalau suppatiṭṭhitau|

aivaṃ mauhakkhayā bhikkhu, pabbatauva na vaidhati||

652.

 ‘‘anaṅgaṇassa pausassa, nic‍caṃ sucigavaisinau|

vālaggamattaṃ pāpassa, abbhamattaṃva khāyati||

653.

 ‘‘nagaraṃ yathā pac‍cantaṃ, guttaṃ santarabāhiraṃ|

aivaṃ gaupaitha attānaṃ, khaṇau vau mā upac‍cagā||

654.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, nibbisaṃ bhatakau yathā||

655.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, sampajānau patissatau||

656.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, bhavanaitti samūhatā||

657.

 ‘‘yassa catthāya pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayau||

658.

 ‘‘sampādaithappamādaina, aisā mai anusāsanī|

handāhaṃ parinibbissaṃ vippamuttaumhi sabbadhī’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tatthāyaṃ  apubbapadavaṇṇanā imasmiṃ dīghamantaraiti, yadā ahaṃ pabbajitaumhi, tatau paṭṭhāya ayañ‍ca mai carimakālau, aitasmiṃ dīghamantarai kālai ‘‘idaṃ mayhaṃ hautū’’ti abhijjhāvasaina vā, ‘‘imai sattā hañ‍ñantū’’tiādinā byāpādavasaina vā anariyaṃ dausasaṃhitaṃ saṅkappaṃ nābhijānāmīti yaujanā|

maittañ‍ca  abhijānāmīti, mij‍jati siniyhati aitāyāti maittā, abyāpādau| maittā aitissā atthīti maittā, maittābhāvanā maittābrahmavihārau, taṃ maittaṃ| ca-saddaina karuṇaṃ muditaṃ upaikkhañ‍cāti itarabrahmavihārai saṅgaṇhāti| abhijānāmīti, abhimukhatau jānāmi| adhigatañhi jhānaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇassa abhimukhaṃ hauti| kīdisanti āha ‘‘appamāṇa’’ntiādi| tañhi yathā buddhaina bhagavatā daisitaṃ, tathā anaudissakapharaṇavasaina aparimāṇasattārammaṇatāya appamāṇaṃ| paguṇabalavabhāvāpādanaina suṭṭhu bhāvitattā subhāvitaṃ| paṭhamaṃ maittā, tatau karuṇā, tatau muditā, pacchā upaikkhāti aivaṃ anupubbaṃ anuk‍kamaina paricitaṃ āsaivitaṃ, bahulīkataṃ abhijānāmīti yaujanā|

sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ mittau, sabbai vā tai mayhaṃ mittāti sabbamittau| maittañhi  bhāvaintau sattānaṃ piyau hauti| sabbasakhauti, aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| sabbabhūtānukampakauti, sabbasattānaṃ anuggaṇhanakau| maittacittañ‍ca bhāvaimīti, maittāya sahitaṃ sampayuttaṃ cittaṃ visaisatau bhāvaimi, vaḍḍhaimi, pakāsaimi vā akathaintaipi bhāvanāya uk‍kaṃsagatabhāvatau| ‘‘maittaṃ cittañ‍ca bhāvaimī’’ti vā pāṭhau| tassatthau haiṭṭhā vuttanayauva| abyāpaj‍jaratauti, abyāpaj‍jai sattānaṃ hitūpasaṃhārai abhiratau| sadāti, sabbakālaṃ, taina tattha sātac‍cakiriyaṃ dassaiti|

asaṃhīranti na saṃhīraṃ, āsan‍napac‍catthikaina rāgaina anākaḍḍhaniyaṃ| asaṃkuppanti na kuppaṃ, dūrapac‍catthikaina byāpādaina akaupiyaṃ, aivaṃbhūtaṃ katvā mama maittacittaṃ āmaudayāmi abhippamaudayāmi brahmavihāraṃ bhāvaimi| akāpurisasaivitanti, kāpurisaihi nīcajanaihi asaivitaṃ, akāpurisaihi vā ariyaihi buddhādīhi saivitaṃ brahmaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ niddausaṃ maittādivihāraṃ bhāvaimi vaḍḍhaimīti atthau|

aivaṃ  attuddaisavasaina pañ‍cahi gāthāhi attanau paṭipattiṃ dassaitvā idāni taṃ añ‍ñāpadaisaina dassaintau ‘‘avitak‍ka’’ntiādinā catassau gāthā abhāsi| tattha avitak‍kaṃ samāpan‍nauti, vitak‍kavirahitaṃ dutiyādijhānaṃ samāpan‍nau, aitaina thairau brahmavihārabhāvanāya añ‍ñāpadaisaina attanā dutiyādijhānādhigamamāha| yasmā panāyaṃ thairau tamaiva jhānaṃ pādakaṃ katvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā aikāsanainaiva arahattaṃ gaṇhi, tasmā tamatthaṃ añ‍ñāpadaisainaiva dassaintau ‘‘avitak‍kaṃ samāpan‍nau’’ti vatvā ‘‘sammāsambuddhasāvakau| ariyaina tuṇhībhāvaina, upaitau hauti tāvadai’’ti āha| tattha vacīsaṅkhārābhāvatau avitak‍kāvicārā samāpatti ‘‘ariyau tuṇhībhāvau’’ti vadanti| ‘‘san‍nipatitānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ dhammī vā kathā ariyau  vā tuṇhībhāvau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.273) pana vacanatau yā kāci samāpatti ariyau tuṇhībhāvau nāma| idha pana catutthajjhānikā aggaphalasamāpatti adhippaitā|

idāni tassādhigatattā laukadhammaihi akampanīyataṃ upamāya pakāsaintau ‘‘yathāpi pabbatau’’ti gāthamāha| tattha yathāpi pabbatau sailauti, yathā silāmayau aikaghanasailau pabbatau, na paṃsupabbatau na missakapabbatauti atthau| acalau suppatiṭṭhitauti, suṭṭhu patiṭṭhitamūlau pakativātaihi acalau akampanīyau hauti, tasmā arahattaṃ nibbānañ‍ca aivaṃ mauhakkhayā bhikkhu, pabbatauva na vaidhatīti mauhassa anavasaisappahānā, mauhamūlakattā ca sabbākusalānaṃ pahīnasabbākusalau bhikkhu yathā sau pabbatau pakativātaihi, aivaṃ laukadhammaihi na vaidhati na kampati, mauhakkhayauti vā yasmā arahattaṃ nibbānañ‍ca vuc‍cati, tasmā mauhakkhayāti mauhakkhayassa haitu nibbānassa arahattassa ca adhigatattā catūsu ariyasac‍caisu suppatiṭṭhitau asamāpan‍nakālaipi pabbatau viya na vaidhati, pagaiva samāpan‍nakālaiti adhippāyau|

idāni pāpaṃ nāmaitaṃ asucisīlau aiva samācarati, na ca sucisīlau, sucisīlassa pana taṃ aṇumattampi bhāriyaṃ hutvā upaṭṭhātīti dassaintau ‘‘anaṅgaṇassā’’tiādigāthamāha| tassatthau – rāgādiaṅgaṇābhāvatau anaṅgaṇassa sabbakālaṃ sucianavaj‍jadhammai aiva gavaisantassa sappurisassa vālaggamattaṃ kaisaggamattaṃ pāpassa laisamattampi sakalaṃ laukadhātuṃ pharitvā ṭhitaṃ abbhamattaṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti, tasmā na aivarūpai kammai mādisā āsaṅkitabbāti adhippāyau|

yasmā nik‍kilaisaisupi andhabālā aivarūpai apavādai samuṭṭhāpainti, tasmā atthakāmaihi sak‍kac‍caṃ attā rakkhitabbauti auvādaṃ daintau ‘‘nagaraṃ yathā’’tiādigāthamāha| tassatthau – yathā pana pac‍cantanagaravāsīhi manussaihi pac‍cantaṃ nagaraṃ dvārapākārādīni thirāni karauntaihi saantaraṃ, uddāpaparikhādīni thirāni karauntaihi sabāhiranti santarabāhiraṃ guttaṃ karīyati, aivaṃ tumhaihipi satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā ajjhattikāni cha dvārāni pidahitvā dvārarakkhitaṃ satiṃ avissaj‍jaitvā yathā gayhamānāni bāhirāni cha āyatanāni ajjhattikāni upaghātāya saṃvattanti, tathā aggahaṇaina tānipi thirāni katvā taisaṃ appavaisāya dvārarakkhitaṃ satiṃ appahāya vicarantā attānaṃ gaupaitha| kasmā? khaṇau vau mā upac‍cagā| yau hi aivaṃ attānaṃ na gaupaiti, taṃ puggalaṃ buddhuppādakkhaṇau, manussattabhāvakkhaṇau, majjhimadaisai uppattikkhaṇau, sammādiṭṭhiyā paṭiladdhakkhaṇau, chan‍naṃ āyatanānaṃ avaikal‍lakkhaṇauti sabbaupi ayaṃ khaṇau atik‍kamati, sau khaṇau tumhai mā atik‍kamatūti|

aivaṃ  thairau imāya gāthāya sarājikaṃ parisaṃ bhikkhū ca auvaditvā puna maraṇai jīvitai ca attanau samacittataṃ katakic‍catañ‍ca pakāsaintau ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇa’’ntiādimāha| taṃ haiṭṭhā vuttatthamaiva (thairagā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.607)|

aivaṃ pana vatvā attanau parinibbānakālaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ disvā saṅkhaipainaiva naisaṃ auvādaṃ datvā parinibbānaṃ pavaidaintau ausānagāthamāha| tattha sampādaithappamādaināti sampādaitabbaṃ dānasīlādiṃ appamādaina sampādaitha, diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikapabhaidai gahaṭṭhavattai sīlānurakkhaṇai samathaanuyaugai vipassanābhāvanāya ca  appamattā hautha| aisā mai anusāsanīti dānasīlādīsu na pamaj‍jathāti aisā mama anusiṭṭhi auvādau|

aivaṃ  sikhāpattaṃ parahitapaṭipattiṃ dīpaitvā attahitapaṭipattiyāpi matthakaṃ gaṇhantau ‘‘handāhaṃ parinibbissaṃ, vippamuttaumhi sabbadhī’’ti āha| tattha vippamuttaumhi sabbadhīti sabbasau kilaisaihi bhavaihi ca vippamuttau amhi, tasmā aikaṃsaina parinibbāyissāmīti|

aivaṃ pana vatvā ākāsai pal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā paj‍jalantau anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyi|

khadiravaniyaraivatatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. gaudattatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

yathāpi bhaddautiādikā āyasmatau gaudattatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ satthavāhakulai nibbattau| gaudattauti nāmaina vayappattau pitari kālaṅkatai kuṭumbaṃ saṇṭhapaintau pañ‍cahi sakaṭasataihi bhaṇḍaṃ ādāya aparāparaṃ sañ‍caritvā vāṇij‍jaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti yathāvibhavaṃ puñ‍ñānipi karauti| sau aikadivasaṃ antarāmaggai dhurai yuttagauṇai vahituṃ asak‍kauntai patitai manussaisu taṃ vuṭṭhāpaituṃ asak‍kauntaisu sayamaiva gantvā taṃ naṅguṭṭhai gāḷhaṃ vijjhi| gauṇau ‘‘ayaṃ asappurisau mama balābalaṃ ajānantau gāḷhaṃ vijjhatī’’ti kuddhau manussavācāya, ‘‘bhau gaudatta, ahaṃ aittakaṃ kālaṃ attanau balaṃ aniguhantau tuyhaṃ  bhāraṃ vahiṃ, aj‍ja pana asamatthabhāvaina patitaṃ maṃ ativiya bādhasi, hautu, itau cavitvā nibbattanibbattaṭṭhānai taṃ bādhaituṃ samatthau paṭisattu bhavaiyya’’nti patthanānurūpaina ak‍kausi| taṃ sutvā gaudattau ‘‘aivaṃ nāma sattai bādhaitvā kiṃ imāya jīvikāyā’’ti saṃvaigajātau sabbaṃ vibhavaṃ pahāya añ‍ñatarassa mahāthairassa santikai pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ patvā samāpattisukhaina vītināmaintau aikadivasaṃ attanau santikaṃ upagatānaṃ gahaṭṭhapabbajitānaṃ ariyagaṇānaṃ laukadhammai ārabbha dhammaṃ kathaintau –

659.

 ‘‘yathāpi  bhaddau ājañ‍ñau, dhurai yuttau dhurassahau|

mathitau atibhāraina, saṃyugaṃ nātivattati||

660.

 ‘‘aivaṃ pañ‍ñāya yai tittā, samuddau vārinā yathā|

na parai atimañ‍ñanti, ariyadhammauva pāṇinaṃ||

661.

 ‘‘kālai  kālavasaṃ pattā, bhavābhavavasaṃ gatā|

narā dukkhaṃ nigacchanti, taidha saucanti māṇavā||

662.

 ‘‘un‍natā sukhadhammaina, dukkhadhammaina caunatā|

dvayaina bālā hañ‍ñanti, yathābhūtaṃ adassinau||

663.

 ‘‘yai ca dukkhai sukhasmiñ‍ca, majjhai sibbinimac‍cagū|

ṭhitā tai indakhīlauva, na tai un‍nataaunatā||

664.

 ‘‘na haiva lābhai nālābhai, na yasai na ca kittiyā|

na nindāyaṃ pasaṃsāya, na tai dukkhai sukhamhi ca||

665.

 ‘‘sabbattha tai na limpanti, udabinduva paukkharai|

sabbattha sukhitā dhīrā, sabbattha aparājitā||

666.

 ‘‘dhammaina ca alābhau yau, yau ca lābhau adhammikau|

alābhau dhammikau saiyyau, yañ‍cai lābhau adhammikau||

667.

 ‘‘yasau ca appabuddhīnaṃ, viñ‍ñūnaṃ ayasau ca yau|

ayasauva saiyyau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, na yasau appabuddhinaṃ||

668.

 ‘‘dummaidhaihi pasaṃsā ca, viñ‍ñūhi garahā ca yā|

garahāva saiyyau viñ‍ñūhi, yañ‍cai bālappasaṃsanā||

669.

 ‘‘sukhañ‍ca kāmamayikaṃ, dukkhañ‍ca pavivaikiyaṃ|

pavivaikadukkhaṃ saiyyau, yañ‍cai kāmamayaṃ sukhaṃ||

670.

 ‘‘jīvitañ‍ca  adhammaina, dhammaina maraṇañ‍ca yaṃ|

maraṇaṃ dhammikaṃ saiyyau, yañ‍cai jīvai adhammikaṃ||

671.

 ‘‘kāmakaupappahīnā  yai, santacittā bhavābhavai|

caranti laukai asitā, natthi taisaṃ piyāpiyaṃ||

672.

 ‘‘bhāvayitvāna baujjhaṅgai, indriyāni balāni ca|

pappuyya paramaṃ santiṃ, parinibbantināsavā’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha ājañ‍ñauti, usabhājānīyau| dhurai yuttauti, sakaṭadhurai yaujitau| dhurassahauti, dhuravāhau| gāthāsukhatthañ‍caittha dvisakāratau niddaisau katau, sakaṭabhāraṃ vahituṃ samatthauti atthau| mathitau atibhāraināti, atibhāraina garubhāraina pīḷitau| ‘‘madditau’’tipi pāḷi, sau aivatthau| saṃyuganti, attanau khandhai ṭhapitaṃ yugaṃ nātivattati na atik‍kāmaiti, sammā yau uddharitvā dhuraṃ chaḍḍaitvā na tiṭṭhati| aivanti yathā sau dhaurayhau attanau bhadrājānīyatāya attanau dhīravīratāya attanau bhāraṃ nātivattati na paric‍cajati, aivaṃ yai vārinā viya mahāsamuddau laukiyalaukuttarāya pañ‍ñāya tittā dhātā paripuṇṇā, tai parai nihīnapañ‍ñai na atimañ‍ñanti, na paribhavanti| tattha  kāraṇamāha ‘‘ariyadhammauva pāṇina’’nti, pāṇinaṃ sattaisu ayaṃ ariyānaṃ dhammau yadidaṃ taisaṃ pañ‍ñāya pāripūriṃ gatattā lābhādinā attānuk‍kaṃsanaṃ viya alābhādinā paraisaṃ avambhanaṃ|

aivaṃ pañ‍ñāpāripūriyā ariyānaṃ sukhavihāraṃ dassaitvā tadabhāvatau anariyānaṃ dukkhavihāraṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘kālai’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha kālaiti lābhālābhādinā samaṅgībhūtakālai| kālavasaṃ pattāti lābhādikālassa ca vasaṃ upagatā, lābhādinā saumanassitā alābhādinā ca daumanassitāti atthau| bhavābhavavasaṃ gatāti bhavassa abhavassa ca vasaṃ upagatā vuddhihāniyau anuvattantā tai| narā dukkhaṃ nigacchanti, taidha saucanti māṇavāti tai narā ‘‘māṇavā’’ti laddhanāmā sattā lābhālābhādivasaina vuddhihānivasaina anuraudhapaṭiviraudhaṃ āpan‍nā idhalaukai saucanti, paralaukai ca nirayādidukkhaṃ gacchanti pāpuṇantīti atthau|

‘‘un‍natā’’tiādināpi  laukadhammavasaina sattānaṃ anatthappattimaiva dassaiti| tattha un‍natā sukhadhammaināti sukhahaitunā sukhapac‍cayaina bhaugasampattiādinā un‍natiṃ gatā, bhaugamadādinā mattāti atthau| dukkhadhammaina caunatāti dukkhahaitunā dukkhapac‍cayaina bhaugavipattiādinā nihīnataṃ gatā dāliddiyādinā kāpañ‍ñataṃ pattā| dvayaināti yathāvuttaina un‍natiaunatidvayaina lābhālābhādidvayaina  vā bālaputhuj‍janā hañ‍ñanti, anuraudhapaṭiviraudhavasaina vibādhīyanti pīḷiyanti| kasmā? yathābhūtaṃ adassinau yasmā tai dhammasabhāvaṃ yāthāvatau nabbhañ‍ñaṃsu, pariñ‍ñātakkhandhā pahīnakilaisā ca na haunti, tasmāti atthau| ‘‘yathābhūtaṃ adassanā’’tipi paṭhanti, adassanahaitūti atthau| yai ca dukkhai sukhasmiñ‍ca, majjhai sibbinimac‍cagūti yai pana ariyā dukkhavaidanāya sukhavaidanāya majjhattatāvaidanāya ca tappaṭibaddhaṃ chandarāgabhūtaṃ sibbiniṃ taṇhaṃ aggamaggādhigamaina ac‍cagū atik‍kamiṃsu, tai indakhīlau viya vātaihi laukadhammaihi asampakampiyā ṭhitā, na tai un‍nataaunatā, kadācipi un‍natā vā aunatā vā na haunti sabbasau anunayapaṭighābhāvatau|

aivaṃ vaidanādhiṭṭhānaṃ arahatau anupalaipaṃ dassaitvā idāni laukadhammai vibhajitvā sabbatthakamaivassa anupalaipaṃ dassaintau ‘‘na haivā’’tiādimāha| tattha lābhaiti cīvarādīnaṃ pac‍cayānaṃ paṭilābhai| alābhaiti taisaṃyaiva appaṭilābhai apagamai| na yasaiti parivārahāniyaṃ akittiyañ‍ca| kittiyāti parammukhā kittanai patthaṭayasatāyaṃ| nindāyanti sammukhā garahāyaṃ| pasaṃsāyanti, pac‍cakkhatau guṇābhitthavanai| dukkhaiti dukkhai uppan‍nai| sukhaiti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau|
