sabbatthāti  sabbasmiṃ yathāvuttai aṭṭhavidhaipi laukadhammai, sabbattha vā rūpādikai visayai tai khīṇāsavā na limpanti sabbasau pahīnakilaisattā| yathā kiṃ? udabinduva paukkharai yathā kamaladalai jalabindu al‍līyitvā ṭhitampi taina na limpati, jalabindunā ca kamaladalaṃ, añ‍ñadatthu visaṃsaṭṭhamaiva, aivamaitaipi upaṭṭhitai lābhādikai, āpāthagatai rūpādiārammaṇai ca visaṃsaṭṭhā aivaṃ| tatau aiva dhīrā paṇḍitā sabbattha lābhādīsu ñāṇamukhaina piyanimittānaṃ saukādīnañ‍ca abhāvatau sukhitā  lābhādīhi ca anabhibhavanīyatau sabbattha aparājitāva haunti|

idāni lābhālābhādīsu saiyyaṃ niddhāraitvā dassaintau ‘‘dhammainā’’tiādimāha| tattha dhammaina ca alābhau yauti yau dhammaṃ rakkhantassa taṃnimittaṃ alābhau lābhābhāvau, lābhahāni| yau ca lābhau adhammikau adhammaina añ‍ñāyaina buddhapaṭikuṭṭhaina vidhinā uppan‍nau, taisu dvīsu alābhau dhammikau dhammāvahau saiyyau, yādisaṃ lābhaṃ parivaj‍jantassa akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, tādisau alābhau pāsaṃsatarau atthāvahau| yañ‍cai lābhau adhammikauti yau lābhau adhammaina uppan‍nau, sau na saiyyauti adhippāyau|

yasau  ca appabuddhīnaṃ, viñ‍ñūnaṃ ayasau ca yauti yau appabuddhīnaṃ duppañ‍ñānaṃ vasaina puggalassa yasau labbhati, yau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ paṇḍitānaṃ vasaina ayasau yasahāni| imaisu dvīsu ayasauva saiyyau viñ‍ñūnaṃ| tai hissa yathā akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, aivaṃ yasahāniṃ icchaiyyuṃ, tathā ca bhabbajātikau taṃ aguṇaṃ pahāya guṇai patiṭṭhaiyya| na yasau appabuddhīnanti duppañ‍ñānaṃ vasaina yasau saiyyau hauti, tai hi abhūtaguṇābhibyāhāravasaināpi naṃ uppādaiyyuṃ, sau cassa idha caiva viñ‍ñūgarahādinā samparāyai ca duggatiyaṃ dukkhaparik‍kilaisādinā anatthāvahau| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘lābhau silaukau sak‍kārau, micchāladdhau ca yau yasau’’ti (su॰ ni॰ 440) ‘‘sak‍kārau kāpurisaṃ hantī’’ti (cūḷava॰ 335; a॰ ni॰ 4.68) ca|

dummaidhaihīti, nippañ‍ñaihi| yañ‍cai bālappasaṃsanāti bālaihi aviddasūhi yā nāma pasaṃsanā|

kāmamayikanti vatthukāmamayaṃ, kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppan‍naṃ| dukkhañ‍ca pavivaikiyanti pavivaikatau nibbattaṃ kāyakilamathavasaina pavattaṃ visamāsanupatāpādihaitukaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ, taṃ pana nirāmisavivaṭṭūpanissayatāya viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsā| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘pavivaikadukkhaṃ saiyyau’’ti|

jīvitañ‍ca  adhammaināti adhammaina jīvikakappanaṃ jīvitahaitu adhammacaraṇaṃ| dhammaina maraṇaṃ nāma ‘‘imaṃ nāma pāpaṃ akarauntaṃ taṃ māraissāmī’’ti kainaci vuttai māraintaipi tasmiṃ pāpaṃ akatvā dhammaṃ avikaupaintassa dhammahaitumaraṇaṃ dhammikaṃ saiyyauti tādisaṃ maraṇaṃ dhammatau anapaitattā dhammikaṃ saggasampāpanatau nibbānupanissayatau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ pāsaṃsataraṃ| tathā hi vuttaṃ –

‘‘cajai dhanaṃ aṅgavarassa haitu, aṅgaṃ cajai jīvitaṃ rakkhamānau|

aṅgaṃ dhanaṃ jīvitañ‍cāpi sabbaṃ, cajai narau dhammamanussarantau’’ti|| (jā॰ 2.21.470)|

yañ‍cai jīvai adhammikanti purisau yaṃ dhammatau apaitaṃ jīvikaṃ jīvaiyya, taṃ na saivaiyya viñ‍ñūhi garahitattā apāyasampāpanatau cāti adhippāyau|

idāni  yathāvuttaṃ khīṇāsavānaṃ anupalaipaṃ kāraṇatau dassaintau ‘‘kāmakaupapahīnā’’tiādigāthamāha|

tattha kāmakaupapahīnāti ariyamaggaina sabbasauva pahīnā anuraudhapaṭiviraudhā| santacittā bhavābhavaiti khuddakai caiva mahantai ca bhavai anavasaisapahīnakilaisapariḷāhatāya vūpasantacittā| laukaiti khandhādilaukai| asitāti taṇhādiṭṭhinissayavasaina anissitā| natthi taisaṃ piyāpiyanti taisaṃ khīṇāsavānaṃ katthaci lābhādikai rūpādivisayai ca piyaṃ vā apiyaṃ vā natthi, taṃnimittānaṃ kilaisānaṃ sabbasau samucchin‍nattā|

idāni yāya bhāvanāya tai aivarūpā jātā, taṃ dassaitvā anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā daisanāya kūṭaṃ gaṇhantau ‘‘bhāvayitvānā’’ti ausānagāthamāha| tattha pappuyyāti, pāpuṇitvā| saisaṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayamaiva| imā aiva ca gāthā thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇāpi ahaisuṃ|

gaudattatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

cuddasakanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

15. sauḷasakanipātau

1. añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

sauḷasakanipātai  aisa bhiyyautiādikā āyasmatau añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai haṃsavatīnagarai gahapatimahāsālakulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ patvā aikadivasaṃ satthu santikai dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ attanau sāsanai paṭhamaṃ paṭividdhadhammarattañ‍ñūnaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā sayampi taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaintau satasahassabhikkhuparivārassa satthunau sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ pavattaitvā paṇidhānaṃ akāsi| satthāpissa anantarāyataṃ disvā bhāviniṃ sampattiṃ byākāsi| sau yāvajīvaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā satthari parinibbutai caitiyai patiṭṭhāpiyamānai antaucaitiyai ratanagharaṃ kāraisi, caitiyaṃ parivāraitvā sahassaratanagghiyāni ca kāraisi|

sau aivaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā, tatau cavitvā, daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai mahākālau nāma kuṭumbikau hutvā aṭṭhakarīsamattai khaittai sāligabbhaṃ phālaitvā, gahitasālitaṇḍulaihi asambhin‍nakhīrapāyāsaṃ sampādaitvā, tattha madhusappisak‍karādayau pakkhipitvā, buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa adāsi| sāligabbhaṃ phālaitvā gahitagahitaṭṭhānaṃ puna pūrati, puthukakālai puthukaggaṃ nāma adāsi| lāyanai lāyanaggaṃ, vaiṇikaraṇai vaiṇaggaṃ, kalāpādikaraṇai kalāpaggaṃ, khalaggaṃ, bhaṇḍaggaṃ, minaggaṃ, kauṭṭhagganti; aivaṃ aikasassai nava vārai aggadānaṃ nāma adāsi| tampi sassaṃ atiraikataraṃ sampan‍naṃ ahausi|

aivaṃ yāvajīvaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā, tatau cutau daivalaukai nibbattitvā daivaisu ca manussaisu ca saṃsarantau, amhākaṃ bhagavatau uppattitau puraitaramaiva  kapilavatthunagarassa avidūrai dauṇavatthunāmakai brāhmaṇagāmai brāhmaṇamahāsālakulai nibbatti| tassa kauṇḍañ‍ñauti gauttatau āgataṃ nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau tayau vaidai uggahaitvā lakkhaṇamantaisu ca pāraṃ agamāsi| taina samayaina amhākaṃ baudhisattau  tusitapuratau cavitvā kapilavatthupurai suddhaudanamahārājassa  gaihai nibbatti| tassa nāmaggahaṇadivasai aṭṭhuttarasataisu brāhmaṇaisu upanītaisu yai aṭṭha brāhmaṇā lakkhaṇapariggahaṇatthaṃ mahātalaṃ upanītā| sau taisu sabbanavakau hutvā, mahāpurisassa lakkhaṇanipphattiṃ disvā, ‘‘aikaṃsaina ayaṃ buddhau bhavissatī’’ti niṭṭhaṃ gantvā mahāsattassa abhinikkhamanaṃ udikkhantau vicarati|

baudhisattaupi khau mahatā parivāraina vaḍḍhamānau anuk‍kamaina vuddhippattau ñāṇaparipākaṃ gantvā aikūnatiṃsatimai vassai mahābhinikkhamanaṃ nikkhamantau anaumānadītīrai pabbajitvā anuk‍kamaina uruvailaṃ gantvā padhānaṃ padahi| tadā kauṇḍañ‍ñau māṇavau mahāsattassa pabbajitabhāvaṃ sutvā, lakkhaṇapariggāhakabrāhmaṇānaṃ puttaihi vappamāṇavādīhi saddhiṃ attapañ‍camau pabbajitvā, anuk‍kamaina baudhisattassa santikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā, chabbassāni taṃ upaṭṭhahantau tassa auḷārikāhāraparibhaugaina nibbin‍nau apak‍kamitvā isipatanaṃ agamāsi| atha khau baudhisattau auḷārikāhāraparibhaugaina laddhakāyabalau vaisākhapuṇṇamāyaṃ baudhirukkhamūlai aparājitapal‍laṅkai nisin‍nau tiṇṇaṃ mārānaṃ matthakaṃ madditvā, abhisambuddhau hutvā, sattasattāhaṃ baudhimaṇḍaiyaiva vītināmaitvā, pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ ñāṇaparipākaṃ ñatvā, āsāḷhīpuṇṇamāyaṃ isipatanaṃ gantvā, taisaṃ dhammacak‍kapavattanasuttantaṃ (mahāva॰ 13 ādayau; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1081) daisaisi| daisanāpariyausānai kauṇḍañ‍ñatthairau aṭṭhārasahi brahmakauṭīhi saddhiṃ sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhāsi| atha pañ‍camiyaṃ pakkhassa anattalakkhaṇasuttantadaisanāya (mahāva॰ 20; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.59) arahattaṃ sacchākāsi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.1.596-612) –

‘‘padumuttarasambuddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ vināyakaṃ|

buddhabhūmimanuppattaṃ, paṭhamaṃ addasaṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘yāvatā baudhiyā mūlai, yakkhā sabbai samāgatā|

sambuddhaṃ parivāraitvā, vandanti pañ‍jalīkatā||

‘‘sabbai daivā tuṭṭhamanā, ākāsai sañ‍caranti tai|

buddhau ayaṃ anuppattau, andhakāratamaunudau||

‘‘taisaṃ  hāsaparaitānaṃ, mahānādau avattatha|

kilaisai jhāpayissāma, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

‘‘daivānaṃ giramañ‍ñāya, vācāsabhimudīrihaṃ|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, ādibhikkhamadāsahaṃ||

‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

daivasaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

‘‘sattāhaṃ  abhinikkhamma, baudhiṃ ajjhagamaṃ ahaṃ|

idaṃ mai paṭhamaṃ bhattaṃ, brahmacārissa yāpanaṃ||

‘‘tusitā hi idhāgantvā, yau mai bhikkhaṃ upānayi|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇautha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

sabbai daivai abhibhautvā, tidivaṃ āvasissati||

‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, manussattaṃ gamissati|

sahassadhā cak‍kavattī, tattha raj‍jaṃ karissati||

‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘tidasā sau cavitvāna, manussattaṃ gamissati|

agārā pabbajitvāna, chabbassāni vasissati||

‘‘tatau sattamakai vassai, buddhau sac‍caṃ kathaissati|

kauṇḍañ‍ñau nāma nāmaina, paṭhamaṃ sacchikāhiti||

‘‘nikkhantainānupabbajiṃ, padhānaṃ sukataṃ mayā|

kilaisai jhāpanatthāya, pabbajiṃ, anagāriyaṃ||

‘‘abhigantvāna sabbañ‍ñū, buddhau laukai sadaivakai|

isināmai migārañ‍ñai, amatabhairimāhani||

‘‘sau dāni pattau amataṃ, santipadamanuttaraṃ|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

atha  naṃ satthā aparabhāgai jaitavanamahāvihārai bhikkhusaṅghamajjhai pañ‍ñattavarabuddhāsanai nisin‍nau paṭhamaṃ paṭividdhadhammabhāvaṃ dīpaintau,  ‘‘aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, mama sāvakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ rattañ‍ñūnaṃ yadidaṃ añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 1.188) aitadaggai ṭhapaisi| sau dvīhi aggasāvakaihi attani karīyamānaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ gāmantasaināsanai ākiṇṇavihārañ‍ca pariharitukāmau, vivaikābhiratiyā viharitukāmau ca attanau santikaṃ upagatānaṃ gahaṭṭhapabbajitānaṃ paṭisanthārakaraṇampi papañ‍caṃ mañ‍ñamānau satthāraṃ āpucchitvā himavantaṃ pavisitvā chaddantaihi nāgaihi upaṭṭhiyamānau chaddantadahatīrai dvādasa vassāni vasi| aivaṃ tattha vasantaṃ thairaṃ aikadivasaṃ sak‍kau daivarājā  upasaṅkamitvā vanditvā ṭhitau aivamāha – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, ayyau dhammaṃ daisaitū’’ti| thairau tassa catusac‍cagabbhaṃ tilakkhaṇāhataṃ suñ‍ñatapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ nānānayavicittaṃ amataugadhaṃ buddhalīlāya dhammaṃ daisaisi| taṃ sutvā sak‍kau attanau pasādaṃ pavaidaintau –

673.

 ‘‘aisa bhiyyau pasīdāmi, sutvā dhammaṃ mahārasaṃ|

virāgau daisitau dhammau, anupādāya sabbasau’’ti|| – paṭhamaṃ gāthamāha|

tattha aisa bhiyyau pasīdāmi, sutvā dhammaṃ mahārasanti yadipi anaikavāraṃ satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā tattha abhippasan‍nau| idāni pana tumhaihi kathitaṃ nānānayavicittatāya asaicanakatāya ca mahārasaṃ dhammaṃ sutvā aisau ahaṃ tatau bhiyyau pasīdāmi| virāgau daisitau dhammau, anupādāya sabbasauti sabbasaṃkilaisatau sabbasaṅkhāratau ca viraj‍janatau virāgajananatau virāgau| tatau aiva rūpādīsu kañ‍ci dhammaṃ anupādāya aggahaitvā vimuttisādhanavasaina pavattattā sabbasau anupādāya daisitau|

aivaṃ sak‍kau daivarājā thairassa daisanaṃ thaumaitvā thairaṃ abhivādaitvā sakaṭṭhānamaiva gatau| athaikadivasaṃ thairau micchāvitak‍kaihi abhibhuyyamānānaṃ aikac‍cānaṃ puthuj‍janānaṃ cittācāraṃ disvā tappaṭipakkhabhūtañ‍cassa anuk‍kamaṃ anussaritvā, attanau ca sabbasau tatau vinivattitamānasataṃ āvaj‍jaitvā tadatthaṃ dīpaitvā –

674.

 ‘‘bahūni  laukai citrāni, asmiṃ pathavimaṇḍalai|

mathainti mañ‍ñai saṅkappaṃ, subhaṃ rāgūpasaṃhitaṃ||

675.

 ‘‘rajamuhatañ‍ca  vātaina, yathā maighaupasammayai|

aivaṃ sammanti saṅkappā, yadā pañ‍ñāya passatī’’ti|| – dvai gāthā abhāsi|

tattha bahūni laukai citrānīti rūpādivasaina tatthāpi nīlapītādivasaina itthipurisādivasaina ca anaikāni laukai cittavicittāni ārammaṇajātāni| asmiṃ pathavimaṇḍalaiti pac‍cakkhabhūtaṃ manussalaukaṃ sandhāya vadati| mathainti mañ‍ñai saṅkappanti taj‍jaṃ purisavāyāmasahitaṃ araṇisahitaṃ viya aggiṃ ayaunisaumanasikārābhisaṅkhātāni micchāsaṅkappāni mathainti mañ‍ñai mathaintāni viya tiṭṭhanti| kīdisaṃ? subhaṃ rāgūpasaṃhitaṃ, kāmavitak‍kanti atthau| sau hi subhākāraggahaṇaina ‘‘subhau’’ti vauharīyati|

rajamuhatañ‍ca  vātaināti ca-iti nipātamattaṃ| yathā gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai vātaina ūhataṃ uṭṭhitaṃ rajaṃ mahāmaighau vassantau upasammayai, vūpasamaiyya| aivaṃ sammanti saṅkappā, yadā pañ‍ñāya passatīti yadā ariyasāvakau tāni laukacitrāni samudayatau, assādatau, ādīnavatau, nissaraṇatau ca yathābhūtaṃ pañ‍ñāya passati, atha yathā taṃ rajaṃ uhataṃ maighaina, aivaṃ sammanti pañ‍ñāya sabbaipi micchāsaṅkappā| na hi uppan‍nāya sammādiṭṭhiyā micchāsaṅkappā patiṭṭhaṃ labhanti| yathā pana pañ‍ñāya passati, taṃ dassaintau –

676.

 ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cāti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

atha nibbindati dukkhai, aisa maggau visuddhiyā||

677.

 ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhāti…pai॰… aisa maggau visuddhiyā||

678.

 ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattāti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

atha nibbindati dukkhai, aisa maggau visuddhiyā’’ti|| –

tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha  sabbai saṅkhārāti chaḷārammaṇasaṅgahā sabbai taibhūmakā pañ‍cakkhandhā| anic‍cāti ‘‘ādimajjhaantavantatau, anic‍cantikatau, tāvakālikatau, tattha tattha bhij‍janatau na nic‍cā’’ti yadā vipassanāpañ‍ñāya passati| atha nibbindati dukkhaiti atha imasmiṃ vaṭṭadukkhai nibbindati, nibbindantau dukkhaparijānanādividhinā sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati| aisa maggau visuddhiyāti aisa yathāvuttau vipassanāvidhi ñāṇadassanavisuddhiyā, ac‍cantavisuddhiyā ca maggau adhigamupāyau|

dukkhāti  sappaṭibhayatau, udayabbayasampaṭipīḷanatau, dukkhamatau, sukhapaṭikkhaipatau ca dukkhā| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

sabbai dhammā anattāti sabbaipi catubhūmakā dhammā anattā| idha pana taibhūmakadhammāva gahaitabbā| tai hi asāratau, avasavattanatau, suñ‍ñatau, attapaṭikkhaipatau ca anattāti vipassitabbā| saisaṃ purimasadisamaiva|

aivaṃ vipassanāvidhiṃ dassaitvā taina vidhinā katakic‍caṃ attānaṃ añ‍ñaṃ viya katvā dassaintau –

679.

 ‘‘buddhānubuddhau  yau thairau, kauṇḍañ‍ñau tibbanik‍kamau|

pahīnajātimaraṇau, brahmacariyassa kaivalī||

680.

 ‘‘aughapāsau daḷhakhilau, pabbatau duppadālayau|

chaitvā khilañ‍ca pāsañ‍ca, sailaṃ bhaitvāna dubbhidaṃ|

tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau jhāyī, muttau sau mārabandhanā’’ti|| –

gāthādvayamāha|

tattha buddhānubuddhauti buddhānaṃ anubuddhau, sammāsambuddhaihi bujjhitāni sac‍cāni taisaṃ daisanānusāraina bujjhatīti atthau| thiraihi asaikkhaihi sīlasārādīhi saman‍nāgatauti, thairau| kauṇḍañ‍ñauti gauttakittanaṃ| tibbanik‍kamauti daḷhavīriyau, thiraparak‍kamau| jātimaraṇānaṃ pahīnakāraṇattā pahīnajātimaraṇau| brahmacariyassa kaivalīti maggabrahmacariyassa anavasaisaṃ, anavasaisatau vā maggabrahmacariyassa pāripūrakau, atha vā kaivalī nāma kilaisaihi asammissatāya maggañāṇaṃ phalañāṇañ‍ca, taṃ imasmiṃ  atthīti kaivalī| yasmā pana tadubhayampi maggabrahmacariyassa vasaina hauti na añ‍ñathā, tasmā ‘‘brahmacariyassa kaivalī’’ti vuttaṃ|

aughapāsauti ‘‘kāmaughau, bhavaughau, diṭṭhaughau, avij‍jaughau’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 1156; vibha॰ 938) aivaṃ vuttā cattārau aughā – ‘‘antalikkhacarau pāsau, yvāyaṃ carati mānasau’’ti (mahāva॰ 33; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.151) aivaṃ vuttau rāgapāsau ca| daḷhakhilauti ‘‘satthari kaṅkhati, dhammai kaṅkhati, saṅghai kaṅkhati, sikkhāya kaṅkhati, sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.185; a॰ ni॰ 5.205) aivaṃ vuttau daḷhau thirau pañ‍cavidhau caitaukhilau ca| pacurajanaihi padālaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyatāya duppadālayau| tatau aiva pabbatasadisatāya pabbatauti ca saṅkhaṃ gatau| ‘‘dukkhai añ‍ñāṇa’’ntiādinā (vibha॰ 226; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.2) vā nayaina vuttau añ‍ñāṇappabhaidau ca| iti aitaṃ sabbaṃ chaitvā khilañ‍ca pāsañ‍cāti aitaisu catubbidhaisu saṃkilaisadhammaisu yau khilañ‍ca pāsañ‍ca ariyamaggañāṇāsinā chinditvā| sailaṃ bhaitvāna dubbhidanti yaina kainaci ñāṇaina chindituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ añ‍ñāṇasailaṃ vajirūpamañāṇaina  chinditvā, cattāraupi aughai taritvā, taisaṃ paratīrai nibbānai ṭhitattā tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau| ārammaṇūpanijjhānalakkhaṇaina lakkhaṇūpanijjhānalakkhaṇaināti duvidhainapi jhāyī; muttau sau mārabandhanāti sau aivarūpau khīṇāsavau sabbasmāpi kilaisamārabandhanā muttau vippamuttau visaṃyuttauti| attānamaiva sandhāya thairau vadati|

athaikadivasaṃ  thairau, attanau saddhivihārikaṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ akalyāṇamittasaṃsaggaina kusītaṃ hīnavīriyaṃ uddhataṃ un‍naḷaṃ viharantaṃ disvā, iddhiyā tattha gantvā, taṃ ‘‘mā, āvusau, aivaṃ kari, akalyāṇamittai pahāya kalyāṇamittai saivantau samaṇadhammaṃ karauhī’’ti auvadi| sau thairassa vacanaṃ nādiyi| thairau tassa anādiyanaina dhammasaṃvaigappattau puggalādhiṭṭhānāya kathāya micchāpaṭipattiṃ garahantau sammāpaṭipattiṃ vivaikavāsañ‍ca pasaṃsantau –

681.

 ‘‘uddhatau capalau bhikkhu, mittai āgamma pāpakai|

saṃsīdati mahaughasmiṃ, ūmiyā paṭikuj‍jitau||

682.

 ‘‘anuddhatau  acapalau, nipakau saṃvutindriyau|

kalyāṇamittau maidhāvī, dukkhassantakarau siyā||

683.

 ‘‘kālapabbaṅgasaṅkāsau, kisau dhamanisanthatau|

mattañ‍ñū an‍napānasmiṃ, adīnamanasau narau||

684.

 ‘‘phuṭṭhau ḍaṃsaihi makasaihi, arañ‍ñasmiṃ brahāvanai|

nāgau saṅgāmasīsaiva, satau tatrādhivāsayai||

685-6.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ…pai॰… sampajānau patissatau||

687.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā…pai॰… bhavanaitti samūhatā||

688.

 ‘‘yassa catthāya pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, kiṃ mai saddhivihārinā’’ti|| –

imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha uddhatauti uddhac‍cayuttau asamāhitau vikkhittacittau| capalauti pattacīvaramaṇḍanādinā cāpalyaina saman‍nāgatau laulapakatikau| mittai āgamma pāpakaiti akalyāṇamittai nissāya samaṇadhammaṃ akarauntau| saṃsīdati mahaughasmiṃ, ūmiyā paṭikuj‍jitauti yathā mahāsamuddai patitapurisau samuddavīcīhi autthaṭau sīsaṃ ukkhipituṃ alabhantau tatthaiva saṃsīdati, aivaṃ saṃsāramahaughasmiṃ paribbhamantau kaudhupāyāsaūmiyā paṭikuj‍jitau autthaṭau vipassanāvasaina pañ‍ñāsīsaṃ ukkhipituṃ alabhantau tatthaiva saṃsīdati|

nipakauti  nipuṇau, attatthaparatthaisu kusalau| saṃvutindriyauti manacchaṭṭhānaṃ indriyānaṃ saṃvaraṇaina pihitindriyau| kalyāṇamittauti kalyāṇaihi mittaihi saman‍nāgatau| maidhāvīti dhammaujapañ‍ñāya  samaṅgībhūtau| dukkhassantakarau siyāti sau tādisau sakalassāpi vaṭṭadukkhassa antakarau bhavaiyya|

kālapabbaṅgasaṅkāsautiādi vivaikābhiratikittanaṃ| nābhinandāmītiādi pana katakic‍cabhāvadassanaṃ| taṃ sabbaṃ haiṭṭhā (thairagā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.607) vuttatthamaiva| ausānai pana  kiṃ mai saddhivihārināti attanau saddhivihārikaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| tasmā aidisaina dubbacaina anādaraina saddhivihārinā kiṃ mai payaujanaṃ aikavihārauyaiva mayhaṃ ruc‍catīti atthau|

aivaṃ pana vatvā chaddantadahamaiva gatau| tattha dvādasa vassāni vasitvā upakaṭṭhai parinibbānai satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā parinibbānaṃ anujānāpaitvā tatthaiva gantvā parinibbāyi|

añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. udāyitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

manussabhūtantiādikā āyasmatau udāyitthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinitvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kapilavatthusmiṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā udāyīti laddhanāmau vayappattau satthu ñātisamāgamai buddhānubhāvaṃ disvā, paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| tayau hi imai udāyitthairā amac‍caputtau pubbai āgatau kāḷudāyī, kauvariyaputtau lāludāyī, ayaṃ brāhmaṇaputtau mahāudāyīti| svāyaṃ aikadivasaṃ satthārā saitavāraṇaṃ sabbālaṅkārapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ mahājanaina pasaṃsiyamānaṃ aṭṭhuppattiṃ katvā nāgaupamasuttantai (a॰ ni॰ 6.43) daisitai daisanāpariyausānai attanau ñāṇabalānurūpaṃ satthu guṇai anussaritvā, buddhārammaṇāya pītiyā samussāhitamānasau ‘‘ayaṃ mahājanau imaṃ tiracchānagataṃ nāgaṃ pasaṃsati, na buddhamahānāgaṃ| handāhaṃ buddhamahāgandhahatthinau guṇai pākaṭai karissāmī’’ti satthāraṃ thaumaintau –

689.

 ‘‘manussabhūtaṃ sambuddhaṃ, attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ|

iriyamānaṃ brahmapathai, cittassūpasamai rataṃ||

690.

 ‘‘yaṃ  manussā namassanti, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ|

daivāpi taṃ namassanti, iti mai arahatau sutaṃ||

691.

 ‘‘sabbasaṃyaujanātītaṃ, vanā nibbanamāgataṃ|

kāmaihi naikkhammarataṃ, muttaṃ sailāva kañ‍canaṃ||

692.

 ‘‘sa vai ac‍caruci nāgau, himavāvañ‍ñai siluc‍cayai|

sabbaisaṃ nāganāmānaṃ, sac‍canāmau anuttarau||

693.

 ‘‘nāgaṃ vau kittayissāmi, na hi āguṃ karauti sau|

saurac‍caṃ avihiṃsā ca, pādā nāgassa tai duvai||

694.

 ‘‘sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, caraṇā nāgassa taiparai|

saddhāhatthau mahānāgau, upaikkhāsaitadantavā||

695.

 ‘‘sati gīvā sirau pañ‍ñā, vīmaṃsā dhammacintanā|

dhammakucchisamāvāsau, vivaikau tassa vāladhi||

696.

 ‘‘sau jhāyī assāsaratau, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitau|

gacchaṃ samāhitau nāgau, ṭhitau nāgau samāhitau||

697.

 ‘‘sayaṃ samāhitau nāgau, nisin‍naupi samāhitau|

sabbattha saṃvutau nāgau, aisā nāgassa sampadā||

698.

 ‘‘bhuñ‍jati anavaj‍jāni, sāvaj‍jāni na bhuñ‍jati|

ghāsamacchādanaṃ laddhā, san‍nidhiṃ parivaj‍jayaṃ||

699.

 ‘‘saṃyaujanaṃ aṇuṃ thūlaṃ, sabbaṃ chaitvāna bandhanaṃ|

yaina yainaiva gacchati, anapaikkhauva gacchati||

700.

 ‘‘yathāpi udakai jātaṃ, puṇḍarīkaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

naupalippati tauyaina, sucigandhaṃ manauramaṃ||

701.

 ‘‘tathaiva ca laukai jātau, buddhau laukai viharati|

naupalippati laukaina, tauyaina padumaṃ yathā||

702.

 ‘‘mahāgini paj‍jalitau, anāhāraupasammati|

aṅgāraisu ca santaisu, nibbutauti pavuc‍cati||

703.

 ‘‘atthassāyaṃ viñ‍ñāpanī, upamā viñ‍ñūhi daisitā|

viñ‍ñissanti mahānāgā, nāgaṃ nāgaina daisitaṃ||

704.

 ‘‘vītarāgau  vītadausau, vītamauhau anāsavau|

sarīraṃ vijahaṃ nāgau, parinibbissatyanāsavau’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha  manussabhūtanti manussaisu bhūtaṃ, nibbattaṃ; manussattabhāvaṃ vā pattaṃ| satthā hi āsavakkhayañāṇādhigamaina sabbagativimuttaupi carimattabhāvai gahitapaṭisandhivasaina ‘‘manussau’’tvaiva vauharīyatīti| guṇavasaina pana daivānaṃ atidaivau, brahmānaṃ atibrahmā| sambuddhanti sayamaiva bujjhitabbabuddhavantaṃ| attadantanti attanāyaiva dantaṃ| bhagavā hi attanāyaiva uppāditaina ariyamaggaina cakkhutaupi…pai॰… manataupi uttamaina damathaina dantau| samāhitanti aṭṭhavidhaina samādhinā maggaphalasamādhinā ca samāhitaṃ| iriyamānaṃ brahmapathaiti catubbidhaipi brahmavihārapathai, brahmai vā saiṭṭhai phalasamāpattipathai samāpaj‍janavasaina pavattamānaṃ| kiñ‍cāpi bhagavā na sabbakālaṃ yathāvuttai brahmapathai iriyati, tattha iriyasāmatthiyaṃ pana tan‍nin‍natañ‍ca upādāya ‘‘iriyamāna’’nti vuttaṃ| cittassūpasamai ratanti cittassa upasamahaitubhūtai sabbasaṅkhārasamathai, nibbānai, abhirataṃ| yaṃ manussā namassanti, sabbadhammāna pāragunti yaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ sabbaisaṃ khandhāyatanādidhammānaṃ abhiñ‍ñāpāragū, pariñ‍ñāpāragū, pahānapāragū, bhāvanāpāragū, sacchikiriyapāragū, samāpattipāragūti chadhā pāraguṃ paramuk‍kaṃsagatasampattiṃ khattiyapaṇḍitādayau manussā namassanti| dhammānudhammapaṭipattiyā pūjaintā kāyaina vācāya manasā ca tan‍nin‍nā tappauṇā tappabbhārā haunti| daivāpi taṃ namassantīti na kaivalaṃ manussā aiva, atha khau aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu daivāpi taṃ namassanti| iti mai arahatau sutanti aivaṃ mayā ārakattādīhi kāraṇaihi arahatau, bhagavatau, dhammasaināpatiādīnañ‍ca ‘‘satthā daivamanussāna’’ntiādikaṃ vadantānaṃ santikai aivaṃ sutanti dassaiti|

sabbasaṃyaujanātītanti sabbāni dasapi saṃyaujanāni yathārahaṃ catūhi maggaihi saha vāsanāya atik‍kantaṃ| vanā nibbanamāgatanti kilaisavanatau tabbirahitaṃ nibbanaṃ upagataṃ| kāmaihi naikkhammaratanti sabbasau kāmaihi nikkhamitvā pabbaj‍jājhānavipassanādibhaidai naikkhammai abhirataṃ| muttaṃ sailāva kañ‍cananti asāratau nissaṭasārasabhāvattā sailatau nissaṭakañ‍canasadisaṃ daivāpi taṃ namassantīti yaujanā|

sa  vai ac‍caruci nāgauti sau aikaṃsatau āguṃ na karauti, punabbhavaṃ na gacchati; nāgau viya balavāti| ‘‘nāgau’’ti laddhanāmau sammāsambuddhau, ac‍carucīti  attanau kāyaruciyā ñāṇaruciyā ca sadaivakaṃ laukaṃ atik‍kamitvā ruci, saubhi| yathā kiṃ? himavāvañ‍ñai siluc‍cayai, yathā hi himavā pabbatarājā attanau thiragarumahāsārabhāvādīhi guṇaihi añ‍ñai pabbatai atiraucati, aivaṃ atiraucatīti atthau| sabbaisaṃ nāganāmānanti ahināgahatthināgapurisanāgānaṃ, saikhāsaikhapac‍caikabuddhanāgānaṃ vā| sac‍canāmauti sac‍cainaiva nāganāmau| taṃ pana sac‍canāmataṃ ‘‘na hi āguṃ karautī’’tiādinā sayamaiva vakkhati|

idāni buddhanāgaṃ avayavatau ca dassaintau nāmatau tāva dassaituṃ ‘‘na hi āguṃ karauti sau’’ti āha| yasmā āguṃ, pāpaṃ, sabbaina sabbaṃ na karauti, tasmā nāgauti atthau| saurac‍canti sīlaṃ| avihiṃsāti karuṇā| tadubhayaṃ sabbassapi guṇarāsissa pubbaṅgamanti, katvā buddhanāgassa purimapādabhāvau tassa yuttauti āha ‘‘pādā nāgassa tai duvai’’ti|

aparapādabhāvaina vadantau ‘‘sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, caraṇā nāgassa taiparai’’ti āha| ‘‘tyāparai’’ti vā pāṭhau| tai aparaitvaiva padavibhāgau| anavaj‍jadhammānaṃ ādānai saddhā hatthau aitassāti, saddhāhatthau| suparisuddhavaidanā ñāṇappabhaidā upaikkhā saitadantā tai aitassa atthīti, upaikkhāsaitadantavā|

uttamaṅgaṃ pañ‍ñā, tassā adhiṭṭhānaṃ satīti āha ‘‘sati gīvā sirau pañ‍ñā’’ti| vīmaṃsā dhammacintanāti yathā khāditabbākhāditabbassa sauṇḍāya parāmasanaṃ ghāyanañ‍ca hatthināgassa vīmaṃsā nāma hauti, aivaṃ buddhanāgassa kusalādidhammacintanā vīmaṃsā| samā vasanti aitthāti, samāvāsau, bhājanaṃ kucchi aiva samāvāsau, abhiñ‍ñāsamathānaṃ ādhānabhāvatau samathavipassanāsaṅkhātau dhammau kucchisamāvāsau aitassāti dhammakucchisamāvāsau| vivaikauti upadhivivaikau| tassāti buddhanāgassa| vāladhi, pariyausānaṅgabhāvatau|

jhāyīti ārammaṇūpanijjhānaina ca jhāyanasīlau| assāsaratauti paramassāsabhūtai nibbānai ratau| ajjhattaṃ susamāhitauti visayajjhattai  phalasamāpattiyaṃ suṭṭhu samāhitau tadidaṃ samādhānaṃ suṭṭhu sabbakālikanti dassaituṃ ‘‘gacchaṃ samāhitau nāgau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| bhagavā hi savāsanassa uddhac‍cassa pahīnattā vikkhaipābhāvatau nic‍caṃ samāhitauva| tasmā yaṃ yaṃ iriyāpathaṃ kappaiti, taṃ taṃ samāhitauva kappaisīti|

sabbatthāti, sabbasmiṃ gaucarai, sabbasmiñ‍ca dvārai sabbasau pihitavutti| taināha – ‘‘sabbaṃ kāyakammaṃ ñāṇapubbaṅgamaṃ  ñāṇānuparivatta’’ntiādi (naitti॰ 15)| aisā nāgassa sampadāti aisā ‘‘na hi āguṃ karauti sau’’tiādinā ‘‘sambuddha’’ntiādinā aiva vā yathāvuttā vakkhamānā ca buddhagandhahatthinau sampatti guṇaparipuṇṇā|

bhuñ‍jati  anavaj‍jānīti sammājīvassa uk‍kaṃsapāramippattiyā bhuñ‍jati agarahitabbāni, micchājīvassa sabbasau savāsanānañ‍ca pahīnattā sāvaj‍jāni garahitabbāni na bhuñ‍jati anavaj‍jāni bhuñ‍jantau ca san‍nidhiṃ parivaj‍jayaṃ bhuñ‍jatīti yaujanā|

saṃyaujananti vaṭṭadukkhaina saddhiṃ santānaṃ saṃyaujanatau vaṭṭai ausīdāpanasamatthaṃ dasavidhampi saṃyaujanaṃ| aṇuṃ thūlanti khuddakañ‍caiva mahantañ‍ca| sabbaṃ chaitvāna bandhananti maggañāṇaina anavasaisaṃ kilaisabandhanaṃ chinditvā| yaina yaināti yaina yaina disābhāgaina|

yathā hi udakai jātaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ udakai pavaḍḍhati naupalippati tauyaina, anupalaipasabhāvattā, tathaiva laukai jātau buddhau laukai viharati, naupalippati laukaina taṇhādiṭṭhimānalaipābhāvatauti yaujanā|

ginīti aggi| anāhārauti anindhanau|

atthassāyaṃ viñ‍ñāpanīti satthu guṇasaṅkhātassa upamaiyyatthassa viñ‍ñāpanī, pakāsanī ayaṃ nāgūpamā| viñ‍ñūhīti satthu paṭividdhacatusac‍cadhammaṃ parijānantaihi attānaṃ sandhāya vadati| viñ‍ñissantītiādi kāraṇavacanaṃ, yasmā nāgaina mayā daisitaṃ nāgaṃ tathāgatagandhahatthiṃ mahānāgā khīṇāsavā attanau visayai ṭhatvā vijānissanti, tasmā añ‍ñaisaṃ puthuj‍janānaṃ ñāpanatthaṃ ayaṃ upamā amhaihi bhāsitāti adhippāyau|

sarīraṃ  vijahaṃ nāgau, parinibbissatyanāsavauti baudhimūlai saupādisaisaparinibbānaina anāsavau sammāsambuddhanāgau, idāni sarīraṃ attabhāvaṃ vijahantau khandhaparinibbānaina parinibbāyissatīti|

aivaṃ cuddasahi upamāhi maṇḍaitvā, sauḷasahi gāthāhi, catusaṭṭhiyā pādaihi satthu guṇai vaṇṇaintau anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā daisanaṃ niṭṭhāpaisi|

udāyitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

sauḷasakanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

16. vīsatinipātau

1. adhimuttatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

vīsatinipātai  yañ‍ñatthaṃ vātiādikā āyasmatau aparassa adhimuttatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau atthadassissa bhagavatau kālai vibhavasampan‍nai kulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthari parinibbutai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ upaṭṭhahantau mahādānāni pavattaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai āyasmatau saṃkic‍catthairassa bhaginiyā kucchimhi nibbatti, adhimuttautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau mātulatthairassa santikai pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau sāmaṇairabhūmiyaṃyaiva ṭhitau arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.4.84-88) –

‘‘nibbutai laukanāthamhi, atthadassīnaruttamai|

upaṭṭhahiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

‘‘nimantaitvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ, ujubhūtaṃ samāhitaṃ|

ucchunā maṇḍapaṃ katvā, bhaujaisiṃ saṅghamuttamaṃ||

‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ athamānusaṃ|

sabbai sattai abhibhaumi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ucchudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā samāpattisukhaina vītināmaintau upasampaj‍jitukāmau ‘‘mātaraṃ āpucchissāmī’’ti mātu santikaṃ gacchantau antarāmaggai daivatāya balikammakaraṇatthaṃ maṃsapariyaisanaṃ carantaihi pañ‍casataihi cauraihi samāgacchi| caurā ca taṃ aggahaisuṃ ‘‘daivatāya bali  bhavissatī’’ti| sau cauraihi gahitaupi abhītau acchambhī vippasan‍namukhauva aṭṭhāsi| taṃ disvā cauragāmaṇiacchariyabbhutacittajātau pasaṃsantau –

705.

 ‘‘yañ‍ñatthaṃ  vā dhanatthaṃ vā, yai hanāma mayaṃ purai|

avasaisaṃ bhayaṃ hauti, vaidhanti vilapanti ca||

706.

 ‘‘tassa tai natthi bhītattaṃ, bhiyyau vaṇṇau pasīdati|

kasmā na paridaivaisi, aivarūpai mahabbhayai’’ti|| – dvai gāthā abhāsi|

tattha yañ‍ñatthanti yajanatthaṃ daivatānaṃ balikammakaraṇatthaṃ vā| vā-saddau vikappanatthau| dhanatthanti sāpataiyyaharaṇatthaṃ| yai hanāma mayaṃ puraiti yai sattai mayaṃ pubbai hanimha| atītatthai hi idaṃ vattamānavacanaṃ| avasaiti avasai asairikai katvā| tanti taisaṃ| ‘‘avasaisanti’’pi paṭhanti| amhaihi gahitaisu taṃ aikaṃ ṭhapaitvā avasaisānaṃ; ayamaiva vā pāṭhau| bhayaṃ hautīti maraṇabhayaṃ hauti| yaina tai vaidhanti vilapanti,cittutrāsaina vaidhanti, ‘‘sāmi, tumhākaṃ idañ‍cidañ‍ca dassāma, dāsā bhavissāmā’’tiādikaṃ vadantā vilapanti|

tassa taiti yau tvaṃ amhaihi daivatāya balikammatthaṃ jīvitā vauraupaitukāmaihi ukkhittāsikaihi santaj‍jitau, tassa tai| bhītattanti bhītabhāvau, bhayanti atthau| bhiyyau vaṇṇau pasīdatīti pakativaṇṇatau uparipi tai mukhavaṇṇau vippasīdati| thairassa kira tadā ‘‘sacai imai māraissanti, idānaivāhaṃ anupādāya parinibbāyissāmi, dukkhabhārau vigacchissatī’’ti uḷāraṃ pītisaumanassaṃ uppaj‍ji| aivarūpai mahabbhayaiti aidisai mahati maraṇabhayai upaṭṭhitai| haituatthai vā aitaṃ bhummavacanaṃ|

idāni thairau cauragāmaṇissa paṭivacanadānamukhaina dhammaṃ daisaintau –

707.

 ‘‘natthi caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, anapaikkhassa gāmaṇi|

atik‍kantā bhayā sabbai, khīṇasaṃyaujanassa vai||

708.

 ‘‘khīṇāya bhavanaittiyā, diṭṭhai dhammai yathātathai|

na bhayaṃ maraṇai hauti, bhāranikkhaipanai yathā||

709.

 ‘‘suciṇṇaṃ brahmacariyaṃ mai, maggau cāpi subhāvitau|

maraṇai mai bhayaṃ natthi, raugānamiva saṅkhayai||

710.

 ‘‘suciṇṇaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ mai, maggau cāpi subhāvitau|

nirassādā bhavā diṭṭhā, visaṃ pitvāva chaḍḍitaṃ||

711.

 ‘‘pāragū anupādānau, katakic‍cau anāsavau|

tuṭṭhau āyukkhayā hauti, muttau āghātanā yathā||

712.

 ‘‘uttamaṃ dhammataṃ pattau, sabbalaukai anatthikau|

ādittāva gharā muttau, maraṇasmiṃ na saucati||

713.

 ‘‘yadatthi saṅgataṃ kiñ‍ci, bhavau vā yattha labbhati|

sabbaṃ anissaraṃ aitaṃ, iti vuttaṃ mahaisinā||

714.

 ‘‘yau taṃ tathā pajānāti, yathā buddhaina daisitaṃ|

na gaṇhāti bhavaṃ kiñ‍ci, sutattaṃva ayauguḷaṃ||

715.

 ‘‘na mai hauti ‘ahausi’nti, ‘bhavissa’nti na hauti mai|

saṅkhārā vigamissanti, tattha kā paridaivanā||

716.

 ‘‘suddhaṃ dhammasamuppādaṃ, suddhaṃ saṅkhārasantatiṃ|

passantassa yathābhūtaṃ, na bhayaṃ hauti gāmaṇi||

717.

 ‘‘tiṇakaṭṭhasamaṃ laukaṃ, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

mamattaṃ sau asaṃvindaṃ, ‘natthi mai’ti na saucati||

718.

 ‘‘uk‍kaṇṭhāmi sarīraina, bhavainamhi anatthikau|

sauyaṃ bhij‍jissati kāyau, añ‍ñau ca na bhavissati||

719.

 ‘‘yaṃ  vau kic‍caṃ sarīraina, taṃ karautha yadicchatha|

na mai tappac‍cayā tattha, dausau paimañ‍ca haihitī’’ti|| –

imā gāthā abhāsi|

720.

 ‘‘tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

satthāni nikkhipitvāna, māṇavā aitadabravu’’nti|| –

ayaṃ saṅgītikāraihi vuttagāthā| itau aparā tissau caurānaṃ, thairassa ca vacanapaṭivacanagāthā –

721.

 ‘‘kiṃ  bhadantai karitvāna, kau vā ācariyau tava|

kassa sāsanamāgamma, labbhatai taṃ asaukatā||

722.

 ‘‘sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī, jinau ācariyau mama|

mahākāruṇikau satthā, sabbalaukatikicchakau||

723.

 ‘‘taināyaṃ  daisitau dhammau, khayagāmī anuttarau|

tassa sāsanamāgamma, labbhatai taṃ asaukatā||

724.

 ‘‘sutvāna caurā isinau subhāsitaṃ, nikkhippa satthāni ca āvudhāni ca|

tamhā ca kammā viramiṃsu aikai, aikai ca pabbaj‍jamaraucayiṃsu||

725.

 ‘‘tai pabbajitvā sugatassa sāsanai, bhāvaitva baujjhaṅgabalāni paṇḍitā|

udaggacittā sumanā katindriyā, phusiṃsu nibbānapadaṃ asaṅkhata’’nti|| –

imāpi saṅgītikāraihi vuttagāthā|

tattha natthi caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, anapaikkhassa, gāmaṇīti gāmaṇi, apaikkhāya, taṇhāya, abhāvaina anapaikkhassa mādisassa, lauhitasabhāvau pubbau viya, caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ natthi, daumanassābhāvāpadaisaina bhayābhāvaṃ vadati| taināha ‘‘atik‍kantā bhayā sabbai’’ti| atik‍kantā bhayā sabbaiti khīṇasaṃyaujanassa arahatau pañ‍cavīsati mahābhayā, añ‍ñai ca sabbaipi bhayā aikaṃsaina atik‍kantā atītā, apagatāti atthau|

diṭṭhai dhammai yathātathaiti catusac‍cadhammai pariñ‍ñāpahānasacchikiriyabhāvanāvasaina maggapañ‍ñāya yathābhūtaṃ diṭṭhai| maraṇaiti maraṇahaitu| bhāranikkhaipanai yathāti yathā kauci purisau sīsai ṭhitaina mahatā garubhāraina saṃsīdantau tassa nikkhaipanai, apanayanai na bhāyati, aivaṃ sampadamidanti atthau| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘bhārā  havai pañ‍cakkhandhā, bhārahārau ca puggalau|

bhārādānaṃ dukhaṃ laukai, bhāranikkhaipanaṃ sukha’’nti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.22)|

suciṇṇanti suṭṭhu caritaṃ| brahmacariyanti, sikkhattayasaṅgahaṃ sāsanabrahmacariyaṃ| tatau aiva maggau cāpi subhāvitau aṭṭhaṅgikau ariyamaggaupi sammadaiva bhāvitau| raugānamiva saṅkhayaiti yathā bahūhi raugaihi abhibhūtassa āturassa raugānaṃ saṅkhayai pītisaumanassamaiva hauti, aivaṃ khandharaugasaṅkhayai maraṇai mādisassa bhayaṃ natthi|

nirassādā bhavā diṭṭhāti tīhi dukkhatāhi abhibhūtā, aikādasahi aggīhi ādittā, tayau bhavā nirassādā, assādarahitā, mayā diṭṭhā| visaṃ pitvāva chaḍḍitanti pamādavasaina visaṃ pivitvā tādisaina payaugaina chaḍḍitaṃ viya maraṇai mai bhayaṃ natthīti atthau|

muttau  āghātanā yathāti yathā cauraihi māraṇatthaṃ āghātanaṃ nītau kainaci upāyaina tatau muttau haṭṭhatuṭṭhau hauti, aivaṃ saṃsārapāraṃ, nibbānaṃ, gatattā pāragū, catūhipi upādānaihi anupādānau, pariñ‍ñādīnaṃ sauḷasan‍naṃ kic‍cānaṃ katattā katakic‍cau kāmāsavādīhi anāsavau, āyukkhayā āyukkhayahaitu tuṭṭhau saumanassikau hauti|

uttamanti saiṭṭhaṃ| dhammatanti, dhammasabhāvaṃ| arahattai siddhai sijjhanahaitu iṭṭhādīsu tādibhāvaṃ| sabbalaukaiti sabbalaukasmimpi, dīghāyukasukhabahulatādivasaina saṃyuttaipi laukai| anatthikauti, anapaikkhau| ādittāva gharā muttauti yathā kauci purisau samantatau ādittatau paj‍jalitatau gaihatau nissaṭau, tatau nissaraṇanimittaṃ na saucati, aivaṃ khīṇāsavau maraṇanimittaṃ na saucati|

yadatthi saṅgataṃ kiñ‍cīti yaṃkiñ‍ci imasmiṃ laukai atthi, vij‍jati, upalabbhati saṅgataṃ, sattaihi saṅkhāraihi vā samāgamau, samaudhānaṃ| ‘‘saṅkhata’’ntipi pāṭhau, tassa yaṃkiñ‍ci pac‍cayaihi samac‍ca sambhuyya kataṃ, paṭic‍casamuppan‍nanti atthau| bhavau vā yattha labbhatīti yasmiṃ sattanikāyai yau upapattibhavau labbhati| sabbaṃ anissaraṃ aitanti sabbamaitaṃ issararahitaṃ, na aittha kainaci ‘‘aivaṃ hautū’’ti issariyaṃ vattaituṃ sak‍kā| iti vuttaṃ mahaisināti  ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti aivaṃ vuttaṃ mahaisinā sammāsambuddhaina| tasmā ‘‘anissaraṃ aita’’nti pajānantau maraṇasmiṃ na saucatīti yaujanā|

na  gaṇhāti bhavaṃ kiñ‍cīti yau ariyasāvakau ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’tiādinā (dha॰ pa॰ 277) yathā buddhaina bhagavatā daisitaṃ, tathā taṃ bhavattayaṃ vipassanāpañ‍ñāsahitāya maggapañ‍ñāya pajānāti| sau yathā kauci purisau sukhakāmau divasaṃ santattaṃ ayauguḷaṃ hatthaina na gaṇhāti, aivaṃ kiñ‍ci khuddakaṃ vā mahantaṃ vā bhavaṃ na gaṇhāti, na tattha taṇhaṃ karautīti atthau|

na mai hauti ‘‘ahausi’’nti ‘‘atītamaddhānaṃ ahaṃ īdisau ahausi’’nti attadiṭṭhivasaina na mai cittappavatti atthi diṭṭhiyā sammadaiva ugghāṭitattā, dhammasabhāvassa ca sudiṭṭhattā| ‘‘bhavissa’’nti na hauti maiti tatau aiva ‘‘anāgatamaddhānaṃ ahaṃ aidisau kathaṃ nu khau bhavissaṃ bhavaiyya’’nti aivampi mai na hauti| saṅkhārā vigamissantīti aivaṃ pana hauti ‘‘yathāpac‍cayaṃ pavattamānā saṅkhārāva, na aittha kauci attā vā attaniyaṃ vā, tai ca khau vigamissanti, vinassissanti, khaṇai khaṇai bhij‍jissantī’’ti| tattha kā paridaivanāti aivaṃ passantassa mādisassa tattha saṅkhāragatai kā nāma paridaivanā|

suddhanti kaivalaṃ, attasāraina asammissaṃ| dhammasamuppādanti pac‍cayapac‍cayuppan‍nadhammasamuppattiṃ avij‍jādipac‍cayaihi saṅkhārādidhammamattappavattiṃ| saṅkhārasantatinti kilaisakammavipākappabhaidasaṅkhārapabandhaṃ| passantassa yathābhūtanti saha vipassanāya maggapañ‍ñāya yāthāvatau jānantassa|

tiṇakaṭṭhasamaṃ laukanti yathā arañ‍ñai apariggahai tiṇakaṭṭhai kainaci gayhamānai aparassa ‘‘mayhaṃ santakaṃ ayaṃ gaṇhatī’’ti na hauti, aivaṃ sau asāmikatāya tiṇakaṭṭhasamaṃ saṅkhāralaukaṃ yadā pañ‍ñāya passati, sau tattha mamattaṃ asaṃvindaṃ asaṃvindantau alabhantau akarauntau| natthi maiti ‘‘ahu vata sauhaṃ, taṃ mai natthī’’ti na saucati|

uk‍kaṇṭhāmi sarīraināti asārakaina abhinudaina dukkhaina akatañ‍ñunā asuciduggandhajaigucchapaṭik‍kūlasabhāvaina iminā kāyaina uk‍kaṇṭhāmi imaṃ kāyaṃ  nibbindantau aivaṃ tiṭṭhāmi| bhavainamhi anatthikauti sabbainapi bhavaina anatthikau amhi, na kiñ‍ci bhavaṃ patthaimi| sauyaṃ bhij‍jissati kāyauti ayaṃ mama kāyau idāni tumhākaṃ payaugaina añ‍ñathā vā añ‍ñattha bhij‍jissati| añ‍ñau ca na bhavissatīti añ‍ñau kāyau mayhaṃ āyatiṃ na bhavissati, punabbhavābhāvatau|

yaṃ vau kic‍caṃ sarīraināti yaṃ tumhākaṃ iminā sarīraina payaujanaṃ, taṃ karautha yadicchatha, icchatha cai| na mai tappac‍cayāti, taṃ nimittaṃ  imassa sarīrassa tumhaihi yathicchitakic‍cassa karaṇahaitu| tatthāti taisu karauntaisu ca akarauntaisu ca| dausau paimañ‍ca haihitīti yathāk‍kamaṃ paṭighau anunayau na bhavissati, attanau bhavai apaikkhāya sabbasau pahīnattāti adhippāyau| añ‍ñapac‍cayā añ‍ñattha ca paṭighānunayaisu asantaisupi tappac‍cayā, ‘‘tatthā’’ti vacanaṃ yathādhigatavasaina vuttaṃ|

tassāti adhimuttatthairassa| taṃ vacananti ‘‘natthi caitasikaṃ dukkha’’ntiādikaṃ maraṇai bhayābhāvādidīpakaṃ, tatau aiva abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā| māṇavāti caurā| caurā hi ‘‘māṇavā’’ti vuc‍canti ‘‘māṇavaihi saha gacchanti katakammaihi akatakammaihipī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.149) viya|

kiṃ  bhadantai karitvānāti, bhantai, kiṃ nāma tapaukammaṃ katvā| kau vā tava ācariyau kassa sāsanaṃ, auvādaṃ nissāya ayaṃ asaukatā maraṇakālai saukābhāvau labbhatīti aitaṃ atthaṃ abravuṃ, pucchāvasaina kathaisuṃ, bhāsiṃsu|

taṃ sutvā thairau taisaṃ paṭivacanaṃ daintau ‘‘sabbañ‍ñū’’tiādimāha| tattha sabbañ‍ñūti paraupadaisaina vinā sabbapakāraina sabbadhammāvabaudhanasamatthassa ākaṅkhāpaṭibaddhavuttinau anāvaraṇañāṇassa adhigamaina atītādibhaidaṃ sabbaṃ jānātīti, sabbañ‍ñū| tainaiva samantacakkhunā sabbassa dassanatau sabbadassāvī| yamhi anāvaraṇañāṇaṃ, tadaiva sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ, natthaiva asādhāraṇañāṇapāḷiyā viraudhau visayuppattimukhaina añ‍ñaihi asādhāraṇabhāvadassanatthaṃ aikassaiva ñāṇassa dvidhā vuttattā| yaṃ panaittha vattabbaṃ, taṃ itivuttakavaṇṇanāyaṃ (itivu॰ aṭṭha॰ 38) vitthāratau vuttamaivāti tattha vuttanayainaiva  vaiditabbaṃ| pañ‍can‍nampi mārānaṃ vijayatau jinau, hīnādivibhāgabhin‍nai sabbasmiṃ sattanikāyai adhimuttavuttitāya mahatiyā karuṇāya saman‍nāgatattā mahākāruṇikau, diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaparamatthaihi yathārahaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ anusāsanatau satthā, tatau aiva sabbalaukassa kilaisaraugatikicchanatau sabbalaukatikicchakau, sammāsambuddhau ācariyau mamāti yaujanā| khayagāmīti nibbānagāmī|

aivaṃ thairaina satthu sāsanassa ca guṇai pakāsitai paṭiladdhasaddhā aikac‍cai caurā pabbajiṃsu, aikac‍cai upāsakattaṃ pavaidaisuṃ| tamatthaṃ dīpaintau dhammasaṅgāhakā ‘‘sutvāna caurā’’tiādinā dvai gāthā abhāsiṃsu| tattha  isinauti adhisīlasikkhādīnaṃ aisanaṭṭhaina isinau, adhimuttatthairassa| nikkhippāti pahāya| satthāni ca āvudhāni cāti asiādisatthāni caiva dhanukalāpādiāvudhāni ca| tamhā ca kammāti tatau caurakammatau|

tai pabbajitvā sugatassa sāsanaiti tai caurā saubhanagamanatādīhi sugatassa bhagavatau sāsanai pabbaj‍jaṃ upagantvā| bhāvanāvisaisādhigatāya audagyalakkhaṇāya pītiyā saman‍nāgamaina udaggacittā| sumanāti saumanassappattā| katindriyāti bhāvitindriyā| phusiṃsūti aggamaggādhigamaina asaṅkhataṃ nibbānaṃ adhigacchiṃsu| adhimuttau kira caurai nibbisaivanai katvā, tai tatthaiva ṭhapaitvā, mātu santikaṃ gantvā, mātaraṃ āpucchitvā, pac‍cāgantvā taihi saddhiṃ upajjhāyassa santikaṃ gantvā, pabbājaitvā upasampadaṃ akāsi| atha taisaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ ācikkhi, tai nacirassaiva arahattai patiṭṭhahiṃsu| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘pabbajitvā…pai॰… asaṅkhata’’nti|

adhimuttatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. pārāpariyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

samaṇassa ahu cintātiādikā āyasmatau pārāpariyatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarassa brāhmaṇamahāsālassa puttau hutvā  nibbatti| tassa vayappattassa gauttavasaina pārāpariyautvaiva samañ‍ñā ahausi| sau tayau vaidai uggahaitvā brāhmaṇasippaisu nipphattiṃ gatau| aikadivasaṃ satthu dhammadaisanākālai jaitavanavihāraṃ gantvā parisapariyantai nisīdi| satthā tassa ajjhāsayaṃ aulaukaitvā indriyabhāvanāsuttaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 3.453) daisaisi| sau taṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbaji| taṃ suttaṃ uggahaitvā tadatthamanucintaisi| yathā pana anucintaisi, svāyamatthau gāthāsu aiva āvi bhavissati| sau tathā anuvicintaintau āyatanamukhaina vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pattau| aparabhāgai attanā cintitākāraṃ pakāsaintau –

726.

 ‘‘samaṇassa ahu cintā, pārāpariyassa bhikkhunau|

aikakassa nisin‍nassa, pavivittassa jhāyinau||

727.

 ‘‘kimānupubbaṃ  purisau, kiṃ vataṃ kiṃ samācāraṃ|

attanau kic‍cakārīssa, na ca kañ‍ci vihaiṭhayai||

728.

 ‘‘indriyāni manussānaṃ, hitāya ahitāya ca|

arakkhitāni ahitāya, rakkhitāni hitāya ca||

729.

 ‘‘indriyānaiva sārakkhaṃ, indriyāni ca gaupayaṃ|

attanau kic‍cakārīssa, na ca kañ‍ci vihaiṭhayai||

730.

 ‘‘cakkhundriyaṃ cai rūpaisu, gacchantaṃ anivārayaṃ|

anādīnavadassāvī, sau dukkhā na hi muc‍cati||

731.

 ‘‘sautindriyaṃ  cai saddaisu, gacchantaṃ anivārayaṃ|

anādīnavadassāvī, sau dukkhā na hi muc‍cati||

732.

 ‘‘anissaraṇadassāvī, gandhai cai paṭisaivati|

na sau muc‍cati dukkhamhā, gandhaisu adhimucchitau||

733.

 ‘‘ambilaṃ madhuraggañ‍ca, tittakaggamanussaraṃ|

rasataṇhāya gadhitau, hadayaṃ nāvabujjhati||

734.

 ‘‘subhānyappaṭikūlāni, phauṭṭhabbāni anussaraṃ|

rattau rāgādhikaraṇaṃ, vividhaṃ vindatai dukhaṃ||

735.

 ‘‘manaṃ caitaihi dhammaihi, yau na sak‍kauti rakkhituṃ|

tatau naṃ dukkhamanvaiti, sabbaihaitaihi pañ‍cahi||

736.

 ‘‘pubbalauhitasampuṇṇaṃ, bahussa kuṇapassa ca|

naravīrakataṃ vagguṃ, samuggamiva cittitaṃ||

737.

 ‘‘kaṭukaṃ madhurassādaṃ, piyanibandhanaṃ dukhaṃ|

khuraṃva madhunā littaṃ, ul‍lihaṃ nāvabujjhati||

738.

 ‘‘itthirūpai itthisarai, phauṭṭhabbaipi ca itthiyā|

itthigandhaisu sārattau, vividhaṃ vindatai dukhaṃ||

739.

 ‘‘itthisautāni sabbāni, sandanti pañ‍ca pañ‍casu|

taisamāvaraṇaṃ kātuṃ, yau sak‍kauti vīriyavā||

740.

 ‘‘sau atthavā sau dhammaṭṭhau, sau dakkhau sau vicakkhaṇau|

karaiyya ramamānaupi, kic‍caṃ dhammatthasaṃhitaṃ||

741.

 ‘‘athau sīdati sañ‍ñuttaṃ, vaj‍jai kic‍caṃ niratthakaṃ|

na taṃ kic‍canti mañ‍ñitvā, appamattau vicakkhaṇau||

742.

 ‘‘yañ‍ca atthaina sañ‍ñuttaṃ, yā ca dhammagatā rati|

taṃ samādāya vattaitha, sā hi vai uttamā rati||

743.

 ‘‘uc‍cāvacaihupāyaihi, paraisamabhijigīsati|

hantvā vadhitvā atha saucayitvā, ālaupati sāhasā yau paraisaṃ||

744.

 ‘‘tacchantau  āṇiyā āṇiṃ, nihanti balavā yathā|

indriyānindriyaihaiva, nihanti kusalau tathā||

745.

 ‘‘saddhaṃ  vīriyaṃ samādhiñ‍ca, satipañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

pañ‍ca pañ‍cahi hantvāna, anīghau yāti brāhmaṇau||

746.

 ‘‘sau  atthavā sau dhammaṭṭhau, katvā vākyānusāsaniṃ|

sabbaina sabbaṃ buddhassa, sau narau sukhamaidhatī’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha samaṇassāti pabbajitassa| ahūti ahausi| cintāti dhammacintā dhammavicāraṇā| pārāpariyassāti pārāparagauttassa| ‘‘pārācariyassā’’tipi paṭhanti| bhikkhunauti saṃsārai bhayaṃ ikkhanasīlassa| aikakassāti asahāyassa, aitaina kāyavivaikaṃ dassaiti| pavivittassāti pavivaikahaitunā kilaisānaṃ vikkhambhanaina vivaikaṃ āraddhassa, aitaina cittavivaikaṃ dassaiti| taināha ‘‘jhāyinau’’ti| jhāyinauti jhāyanasīlassa, yaunisaumanasikāraisu yuttassāti atthau| sabbamaitaṃ thairau attānaṃ paraṃ viya katvā vadati|

‘‘kimānupubba’’ntiādinā taṃ cintanaṃ dassaiti| tattha paṭhamagāthāyaṃ tāva kimānupubbanti anupubbaṃ anuk‍kamau, anupubbamaiva vakkhamānaisu vatasamācāraisu kau anuk‍kamau, kaina anuk‍kamaina tai paṭipaj‍jitabbāti atthau| purisau kiṃ vataṃ kiṃ samācāranti atthakāmau purisau samādiyitabbaṭṭhaina ‘‘vata’’nti laddhanāmaṃ, kīdisaṃ sīlaṃ samācāraṃ, samācarantau, attanau kic‍cakārī kattabbakārī assa, kañ‍ci sattaṃ na ca vihaiṭhayai, na bādhaiyyāti atthau| attanau kic‍caṃ nāma samaṇadhammau, saṅkhaipatau sīlasamādhipañ‍ñā, taṃ sampādaintassa paravihaiṭhanāya laisaupi natthi tāya sati samaṇabhāvassaiva abhāvatau|

yathāha bhagavā – ‘‘na hi pabbajitau parūpaghātī, na samaṇau hauti paraṃ vihaiṭhayantau’’ti (dha॰ pa॰ 184)| aittha ca vataggahaṇaina vārittasīlaṃ gahitaṃ, samācāraggahaṇaina samācaritabbatau cārittasīlaina saddhiṃ jhānavipassanādi, tasmā vārittasīlaṃ padhānaṃ| tatthāpi ca yasmā indriyasaṃvarai siddhai sabbaṃ sīlaṃ surakkhitaṃ, sugaupitamaiva hauti, tasmā indriyasaṃvarasīlaṃ tāva dassaitukāmau indriyānaṃ arakkhaṇai rakkhaṇai ca ādīnavānisaṃsai vibhāvaintau  ‘‘indriyāni manussāna’’ntiādimāha| tattha indriyānīti rakkhitabbadhammanidassanaṃ, tasmā cakkhādīni cha indriyānīti vuttaṃ hauti| manussānanti rakkhaṇayaugyapuggalanidassanaṃ| hitāyāti atthāya| ahitāyāti anatthāya| hauntīti  vacanasaisau| kathaṃ pana tāniyaiva hitāya ca ahitāya hauntīti āha ‘‘rakkhitānī’’tiādi| tassatthau – yassa cakkhādīni indriyāni satikavāṭaina apihitāni, tassa rūpādīsu abhijjhādipāpadhammapavattiyā dvārabhāvatau anatthāya pihitāni, tadabhāvatau atthāya saṃvattantīti|

indriyānaiva  sārakkhanti yasmā indriyasaṃvarau paripuṇṇau sīlasampadaṃ paripūraiti, sīlasampadā paripuṇṇā samādhisampadaṃ paripūraiti, samādhisampadā paripuṇṇā pañ‍ñāsampadaṃ paripūraiti, tasmā indriyārakkhā attahitapaṭipattiyāva mūlanti dassaintau āha ‘‘indriyānaiva sārakkha’’nti| satipubbaṅgamaina ārakkhaina saṃrakkhantau yaunisaumanasikāraina indriyāni aiva tāva sammadaiva rakkhantau, yathā akusalacaurā taihi taihi dvāraihi pavisitvā cittasantānai kusalaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ na vilumpanti, tathā tāni pidahantauti atthau| sārakkhanti ca saṃ-saddassa sābhāvaṃ katvā vuttaṃ, ‘‘sārāgau’’tiādīsu viya| ‘‘saṃrakkha’’nti ca pāṭhau| indriyāni ca gaupayanti tassaiva pariyāyavacanaṃ, pariyāyavacanai payaujanaṃ naittiaṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| ‘‘attanau kic‍cakārīssā’’ti iminā attahitapaṭipattiṃ dassaiti, ‘‘na ca kañ‍ci vihaiṭhayai’’ti iminā parahitapaṭipattiṃ, ubhayaināpi vā attahitapaṭipattimaiva dassaiti parāvihaiṭhanassāpi attahitapaṭipattibhāvatau| atha vā padadvayainapi attahitapaṭipattiṃ dassaiti puthuj‍janassa saikkhassa ca parahitapaṭipattiyāpi attahitapaṭipattibhāvatau|

aivaṃ rakkhitāni indriyāni hitāya hauntīti vaudānapakkhaṃ saṅkhaipainaiva dassaitvā, arakkhitāni ahitāya hauntīti saṃkilaisapakkhaṃ pana vibhajitvā dassaintau ‘‘cakkhundriyaṃ cai’’tiādimāha| tattha cakkhundriyaṃ cai rūpaisu, gacchantaṃ anivārayaṃ| anādīnavadassāvīti yau nīlapītādibhaidaisu iṭṭhāniṭṭhaisu rūpāyatanaisu gacchantaṃ yathāruci pavattantaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ anivārayaṃ, anivārayantau appaṭibāhantau tathāpavattiyaṃ ādīnavadassāvī na hauti cai, diṭṭhadhammikaṃ samparāyikañ‍ca ādīnavaṃ dausaṃ na passati cai| ‘‘gacchantaṃ nivārayai anissaraṇadassāvī’’ti ca pāṭhau| tattha yau ‘‘diṭṭhai diṭṭhamattaṃ bhavissatī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.95) vuttavidhinā diṭṭhamattaiyaiva ṭhatvā satisampajañ‍ñavasaina rūpāyatanai pavattamānau tattha nissaraṇadassāvī nāma| vuttavipariyāyaina anissaraṇadassāvī daṭṭhabbau| sau dukkhā na hi muc‍catīti sau aivarūpau puggalau vaṭṭadukkhatau na muc‍cataiva| aittha ca cakkhundriyassa anivāraṇaṃ  nāma yathā taina dvāraina abhijjhādayau pāpadhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tathā pavattanaṃ, taṃ pana atthatau satisampajañ‍ñassa anuṭṭhāpanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| saisindriyaisupi aisaiva nayau| adhimucchitauti adhimuttataṇhāya mucchaṃ āpan‍nau| ambilanti ambilarasaṃ| madhuragganti madhurarasakauṭṭhāsaṃ| tathā tittakaggaṃ| anussaranti assādavasaina taṃ taṃ rasaṃ anuvicintaintau| ganthitauti rasataṇhāya tasmiṃ tasmiṃ rasai ganthitau bandhau| ‘‘gadhitau’’ti ca paṭhanti, gaidhaṃ āpan‍nauti atthau| hadayaṃ nāvabujjhatīti ‘‘dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’’ti pabbaj‍jādikkhaṇai uppan‍naṃ cittaṃ na jānāti na sal‍lakkhaiti, sāsanassa hadayaṃ abbhantaraṃ anavaj‍jadhammānaṃ sammaddanarasataṇhāya gadhitau nāvabujjhati na jānāti, na paṭipaj‍jatīti atthau|

subhānīti sundarāni| appaṭikūlānīti manauramāni, iṭṭhāni| phauṭṭhabbānīti upādiṇṇānupādiṇṇappabhaidai phassai| rattauti raj‍janasabhāvaina rāgaina rattau| rāgādhikaraṇanti rāgahaitu| vividhaṃ vindatai dukhanti rāgapariḷāhādivasaina diṭṭhadhammikañ‍ca nirayasantāpādivasaina abhisamparāyañ‍ca nānappakāraṃ dukkhaṃ paṭilabhati|

manaṃ caitaihīti manañ‍ca aitaihi rūpārammaṇādīhi dhammārammaṇappabhaidaihi ca| nanti puggalaṃ| sabbaihīti sabbaihi pañ‍cahipi| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yau puggalau manaṃ, manaudvāraṃ, aitaihi yathāvuttaihi rūpādīhi pañ‍cahi dhammaihi dhammārammaṇappabhaidatau ca| tattha pavattanakapāpakammanivāraṇaina rakkhituṃ, gaupituṃ na sak‍kauti, tatau tassa arakkhaṇatau naṃ puggalaṃ taṃnimittaṃ dukkhaṃ anvaiti, anugacchati, anugacchantañ‍ca aitaihi pañ‍cahipi rūpārammaṇādīhi chaṭṭhārammaṇaina saddhiṃ sabbaihipi ārammaṇappac‍cayabhūtaihi anugacchatīti| aittha cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyañ‍ca asampattaggāhibhāvatau ‘‘gacchantaṃ  anivāraya’’nti vuttaṃ itaraṃ sampattaggāhīti ‘‘gandhai cai paṭisaivatī’’tiādinā vuttaṃ| tatthāpi ca rasataṇhā ca phauṭṭhabbataṇhā ca sattānaṃ visaisatau balavatīti ‘‘rasataṇhāya gadhitau, phauṭṭhabbāni anussarantauti’’ vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

aivaṃ aguttadvārassa puggalassa chahi dvāraihi chasupi ārammaṇaisu asaṃvaranimittaṃ uppaj‍janakadukkhaṃ dassaitvā svāyamasaṃvarau yasmā sarīrasabhāvānavabaudhaina hauti, tasmā sarīrasabhāvaṃ vicinantau ‘‘pubbalauhitasampuṇṇa’’ntiādinā gāthādvayamāha| tassatthau  – sarīraṃ nāmaitaṃ pubbaina lauhitaina ca sampuṇṇaṃ bharitaṃ añ‍ñaina ca pittasaimhādinā bahunā kuṇapaina, tayidaṃ naravīraina naraisu chaikaina sippācariyaina kataṃ vaggu maṭṭhaṃ lākhāparikammādinā cittitaṃ, antau pana gūthādiasucibharitaṃ samuggaṃ viya chavimattamanauharaṃ bālajanasammauhaṃ dukkhasabhāvatāya nirayādidukkhatāpanatau ca kaṭukaṃ, parikappasambhavaina amūlakaina assādamattaina madhuratāya madhurassādaṃ, tatau aiva piyabhāvanibandhanaina piyanibandhanaṃ, dussahatāya appatītatāya ca dukhaṃ, īdisai sarīrai assādalaubhaina mahādukkhaṃ pac‍canubhuyyamānaṃ anavabujjhantau laukau madhuragiddhau khuradhārālaihakapurisau viya daṭṭhabbauti|

idāni  aitai cakkhādīnaṃ gaucarabhūtā rūpādayau vuttā, tai visaisatau purisassa itthipaṭibaddhā kamanīyāti tattha saṃvarau kātabbauti dassaintau ‘‘itthirūpai’’tiādimāha| tattha itthirūpaiti itthiyā catusamuṭṭhānikarūpāyatanasaṅkhātai vaṇṇai| api ca yau kauci itthiyā nivatthassa alaṅkārassa vā gandhavaṇṇakādīnaṃ vā piḷandhanamālānaṃ vā kāyapaṭibaddhau vaṇṇau purisassa cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa ārammaṇabhāvāya upakappati, sabbamaitaṃ ‘‘itthirūpa’’ntvaiva vaiditabbaṃ| itthisaraiti itthiyā gītalapitahasitaruditasaddai| api ca itthiyā nivatthavatthassapi alaṅkataalaṅkārassapi itthipayauganipphāditā vaiṇuvīṇāsaṅkhapaṇavādīnampi saddā idha itthisaraggahaṇaina gahitāti vaiditabbā| sabbaupaisau purisassa cittaṃ ākaḍḍhatīti| ‘‘itthirasai’’ti pana pāḷiyā catusamuṭṭhānikarasāyatanavasaina vuttaṃ| itthiyā kiṃkārapaṭissāvitādivasaina assavarasau caiva paribhaugarasau  ca itthirasauti aikai| yau pana itthiyā auṭṭhamaṃsasammakkhitakhaiḷādirasau, yau ca tāya purisassa din‍nayāgubhattādīnaṃ rasau, sabbaupaisau ‘‘itthirasau’’tvaiva vaiditabbau| phauṭṭhabbaipi ca itthiyā kāyasamphassau, itthisarīrārūḷhānaṃ vatthālaṅkāramālādīnaṃ phassau ‘‘itthiphauṭṭhabbau’’tvaiva vaiditabbau| aittha ca yaisaṃ itthirūpai itthisaraiti pāḷi, taisaṃ api-saddaina itthirasasaṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| itthigandhaisūti itthiyā catusamuṭṭhānikagandhāyatanaisu| itthiyā sarīragandhau nāma duggandhau| aikac‍cā hi itthī assagandhinī hauti, aikac‍cā maiṇḍagandhinī, aikac‍cā saidagandhinī, aikac‍cā sauṇitagandhinī, tathāpi tāsu andhabālau raj‍jataiva| cak‍kavattinau pana itthiratanassa kāyatau candanagandhau vāyati, mukhatau  uppalagandhau, ayaṃ na sabbāsaṃ hautīti, itthiyā sarīrai ārūḷhau āgantukau anulimpanādigandhau ‘‘itthigandhau’’ti vaiditabbau| sārattauti suṭṭhu rattau gadhitau mucchitau, idaṃ pana padaṃ ‘‘itthirūpai’’tiādīsupi yaujaitabbaṃ| vividhaṃ vindatai dukhanti itthirūpādīsu sarāganimittaṃ diṭṭhadhammikaṃ vadhabandhanādivasaina samparāyikaṃ pañ‍cavidhabandhanādivasaina nānappakāraṃ dukkhaṃ paṭilabhati|

itthisautāni sabbānīti itthiyā rūpādiārammaṇāni sabbāni anavasaisāni pañ‍ca taṇhāsautāni sandanti| pañ‍casūti purisassa pañ‍casu dvāraisu| taisanti taisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ sautānaṃ| āvaraṇanti saṃvaraṇaṃ, yathā asaṃvarau na uppaj‍jati, aivaṃ satisampajañ‍ñaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhapaitvā saṃvaraṃ pavattaituṃ yau sak‍kauti, sau vīriyavā āraddhavīriyau akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāyāti atthau|

aivaṃ rūpādigaucarai pabbajitassa paṭipattiṃ dassaitvā idāni gahaṭṭhassa dassaituṃ ‘‘sau atthavā’’tiādi  vuttaṃ| tattha sau atthavā sau dhammaṭṭhau, sau dakkhau sau vicakkhaṇauti sau puggalau imasmiṃ laukai atthavā, buddhimā, dhammai ṭhitau, dhammai dakkhau, dhammai chaikau, analasau vā vicakkhaṇau iti kattabbatāsu kusalau nāma| karaiyya ramamānaupi, kic‍caṃ dhammatthasaṃhitanti gaiharatiyā ramamānaupi dhammatthasaṃhitaṃ dhammatau atthatau ca anapaitamaiva taṃ taṃ kattabbaṃ| anuppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ uppādanaṃ, uppan‍nānaṃ paripālanaṃ, paribhaugañ‍ca karaiyya, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ, aviraudhaina, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ, abādhanaina, tivaggatthaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyyāti adhippāyau| ayañ‍ca nayau yaisaṃ sammāpaṭipattiaviraudhaina tivaggatthassa vasaina vattati bimbisāramahārājādīnaṃ viya, taisaṃ vasaina vuttau| na yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci vasaināti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

athau sīdati sañ‍ñuttanti yadi idhalaukai supasaṃhitaṃ diṭṭhadhammikaṃ atthaṃ pariggahaitvā ṭhitaṃ| vaj‍jai kic‍caṃ niratthakanti samparāyikattharahitaṃ anatthupasaṃhitaṃ kic‍caṃ sacaipi vissaj‍jaiyya paric‍cajaiyya| na taṃ kic‍canti mañ‍ñitvā, appamattau vicakkhaṇauti satiavippavāsaina appamattau vicāraṇapañ‍ñāsambhavaina vicakkhaṇau anatthupasaṃhitaṃ, taṃ kic‍caṃ mayā na kātabbanti mañ‍ñitvā vivaj‍jaiyya|

vivaj‍jaitvā pana yañ‍ca atthaina sañ‍ñuttaṃ, yā ca dhammagatā rati| taṃ samādāya vattaithāti yaṃkiñ‍ci diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikappabhaidaina atthaina  hitaina saṃyuttaṃ tadubhayahitāvahaṃ, yā ca adhikusaladhammagatā samathavipassanāsahitā rati, tadubhayaṃ sammā ādiyitvā pariggahaṃ katvā vattaiyya| ‘‘sabbaṃ ratiṃ dhammarati jinātī’’ti (dha॰ pa॰ 354) vacanatau sā hi aikaṃsaina uttamatthassa pāpanatau uttamā rati nāma|

yaṃ pana kāmaratisaṃyuttaṃ kic‍caṃ niratthakanti vuttaṃ, tassā anatthupasaṃhitabhāvaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘uc‍cāvacaihī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha uc‍cāvacaihīti mahantaihi caiva khuddakaihi ca| upāyaihīti nayaihi| paraisamabhijigīsatīti paraisaṃ santakaṃ āharituṃ icchati, parai vā sabbathā hāpaiti, jināpaiti paraṃ hantvā, vadhitvā atha saucayitvā, ālaupati sāhasā yau paraisaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yau puggalau kāmahaitu parai hanantau, ghātaintau, saucaintau sandhicchaidasandhiruhanapasayhāvahārādīhi nānupāyaihi paraisaṃ santakaṃ harituṃ vāyamantau sāhasākāraṃ karauti, ālaupati, jigīsati sāpataiyyavasaina parai hāpaiti, tassa taṃ kic‍caṃ kāmaratisan‍nissitaṃ anatthupasaṃhitaṃ aikantanihīnanti| aitaina tappaṭipakkhatau dhammagatāya ratiyā aikaṃsatau uttamabhāvaṃyaiva vibhāvaiti|

idāni  yaṃ ‘‘taisamāvaraṇaṃ kātuṃ yau sak‍kautī’’ti indriyānaṃ āvaraṇaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ upāyaina saha vibhāvaintau ‘‘tacchantau āṇiyā āṇiṃ, nihanti balavā yathā’’ti āha| yathā balavā kāyabalaina, ñāṇabalaina ca saman‍nāgatau tacchakau rukkhadaṇḍagataṃ āṇiṃ nīharitukāmau tatau balavatiṃ āṇiṃ kauṭaintau tatau nīharati, tathā kusalau bhikkhu cakkhādīni indriyāni vipassanābalaina nihantukāmau indriyaihi aiva nihanti|

katamaihi panāti āha ‘‘saddha’’ntiādi| tassatthau – adhimaukkhalakkhaṇaṃ saddhaṃ, paggahalakkhaṇaṃ vīriyaṃ, avikkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ samādhiṃ, upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇaṃ satiṃ, dassanalakkhaṇaṃ pañ‍ñanti imānipi vimuttiparipācakāni pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvaintau vaḍḍhaintau aitaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi cakkhādīni pañ‍cindriyāni anunayapaṭighādikilaisuppattiyā dvārabhāvavihanaina hantvā, ariyamaggaina tadupanissayai kilaisai samucchinditvā, tatau aiva anīghau niddukkhau brāhmaṇau anupādisaisaparinibbānamaiva yāti upagacchatīti|

sau atthavāti sau yathāvuttau brāhmaṇau uttamatthaina saman‍nāgatattā atthavā, taṃ sampāpakai dhammai ṭhitattā dhammaṭṭhau| sabbaina sabbaṃ anavasaisaina vidhinā anavasaisaṃ buddhassa bhagavatau vākyabhūtaṃ anusāsaniṃ katvā yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jitvā ṭhitau| tatau aiva sau narau uttamapurisau nibbānasukhañ‍ca aidhati, brūhaiti, vaḍḍhaitīti|

aivaṃ  thairaina attanau cintitākāravibhāvanāvasaina paṭipattiyā pakāsitattā idamaiva cassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

pārāpariyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. tailakānitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

cirarattaṃ vatātāpītiādikā āyasmatau tailakānitthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā imasmiṃ buddhuppādai satthu abhijātitau puraitaraṃyaiva sāvatthiyaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā tailakānīti laddhanāmau vayappattau haitusampan‍natāya kāmai jigucchantau gharāvāsaṃ pahāya paribbājakapabbaj‍jaṃ  pabbajitvā vivaṭṭajjhāsayau ‘‘kau sau pāraṅgatau laukai’’tiādinā vimaukkhapariyaisanaṃ  caramānau tai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇai upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchati, tai na sampāyanti| sau taina anārādhitacittau vicarati| atha amhākaṃ bhagavati laukai uppaj‍jitvā pavattitavaradhammacak‍kai laukahitaṃ karauntai aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattai patiṭṭhāti| sau aikadivasaṃ bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ nisin‍nau attanā adhigatavisaisaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā tadanusāraina attanau paṭipattiṃ anussaritvā taṃ sabbaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ācikkhantau –

747.

 ‘‘cirarattaṃ vatātāpī, dhammaṃ anuvicintayaṃ|

samaṃ cittassa nālatthaṃ, pucchaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇai||

748.

 ‘‘kau sau pāraṅgatau laukai, kau pattau amataugadhaṃ|

kassa dhammaṃ paṭicchāmi, paramatthavijānanaṃ||

749.

 ‘‘antauvaṅkagatau āsi, macchauva ghasamāmisaṃ|

baddhau mahindapāsaina, vaipacityasurau yathā||

750.

 ‘‘añchāmi naṃ na muñ‍cāmi, asmā saukapariddavā|

kau mai bandhaṃ muñ‍caṃ laukai, sambaudhiṃ vaidayissati||

751.

 ‘‘samaṇaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vā kaṃ, ādisantaṃ pabhaṅgunaṃ|

kassa dhammaṃ paṭicchāmi, jarāmac‍cupavāhanaṃ||

752.

 ‘‘vicikicchākaṅkhāganthitaṃ, sārambhabalasañ‍ñutaṃ|

kaudhappattamanatthaddhaṃ, abhijappappadāraṇaṃ||

753.

 ‘‘taṇhādhanusamuṭṭhānaṃ, dvai ca pan‍narasāyutaṃ|

passa aurasikaṃ bāḷhaṃ, bhaitvāna yadi tiṭṭhati||

754.

 ‘‘anudiṭṭhīnaṃ  appahānaṃ, saṅkappaparataijitaṃ|

taina viddhau pavaidhāmi, pattaṃva mālutairitaṃ||

755.

 ‘‘ajjhattaṃ mai samuṭṭhāya, khippaṃ pac‍cati māmakaṃ|

chaphassāyatanī kāyau, yattha sarati sabbadā||

756.

 ‘‘taṃ  na passāmi taikicchaṃ, yau maitaṃ sal‍lamuddharai|

nānāraj‍jaina satthaina, nāñ‍ñaina vicikicchitaṃ||

757.

 ‘‘kau mai asatthau avaṇau, sal‍lamabbhantarapassayaṃ|

ahiṃsaṃ sabbagattāni, sal‍laṃ mai uddharissati||

758.

 ‘‘dhammappati  hi sau saiṭṭhau, visadausappavāhakau|

gambhīrai patitassa mai, thalaṃ pāṇiñ‍ca dassayai||

759.

 ‘‘rahadaihamasmi augāḷhau, ahāriyarajamattikai|

māyāusūyasārambha, thinamiddhamapatthaṭai||

760.

 ‘‘uddhac‍camaighathanitaṃ, saṃyaujanavalāhakaṃ|

vāhā vahanti kuddiṭṭhiṃ, saṅkappā rāganissitā||

761.

 ‘‘savanti sabbadhi sautā, latā ubbhij‍ja tiṭṭhati|

tai sautai kau nivāraiyya, taṃ lataṃ kau hi chaicchati||

762.

 ‘‘vailaṃ karautha bhaddantai, sautānaṃ san‍nivāraṇaṃ|

mā tai manaumayau sautā, rukkhaṃva sahasā luvai||

763.

 ‘‘aivaṃ mai bhayajātassa, apārā pāramaisatau|

tāṇau pañ‍ñāvudhau satthā, isisaṅghanisaivitau||

764.

 ‘‘saupānaṃ sugataṃ suddhaṃ, dhammasāramayaṃ daḷhaṃ|

pādāsi vuyhamānassa, mā bhāyīti ca mabravi||

765.

 ‘‘satipaṭṭhānapāsādaṃ, āruyha pac‍cavaikkhisaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ pubbai amañ‍ñissaṃ, sak‍kāyābhirataṃ pajaṃ||

766.

 ‘‘yadā ca maggamaddakkhiṃ, nāvāya abhirūhanaṃ|

anadhiṭṭhāya attānaṃ, titthamaddakkhimuttamaṃ||

767.

 ‘‘sal‍laṃ attasamuṭṭhānaṃ, bhavanaittippabhāvitaṃ|

aitaisaṃ appavattāya, daisaisi maggamuttamaṃ||

768.

 ‘‘dīgharattānusayitaṃ, cirarattamadhiṭṭhitaṃ|

buddhau maipānudī ganthaṃ, visadausappavāhanau’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha  cirarattaṃ vatāti cirakālaṃ vata| ātāpīti vīriyavā vimaukkhadhammapariyaisanai āraddhavīriyau| dhammaṃ anuvicintayanti ‘‘kīdisau nu khau vimaukkhadhammau, kathaṃ vā adhigantabbau’’ti vimuttidhammaṃ anuvicinantau gavaisantau| samaṃ cittassa nālatthaṃ, pucchaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇaiti tai tai nānātitthiyai samaṇabrāhmaṇai vimuttidhammaṃ pucchantau pakatiyā anupasantasabhāvassa cittassa samaṃ vūpasamabhūtaṃ vaṭṭadukkhavissaraṇaṃ ariyadhammaṃ nālatthaṃ nādhigacchanti atthau|

kau  sau pāraṅgatautiādi pucchitākāradassanaṃ| tattha kau sau pāraṅgatau laukaiti imasmiṃ laukai titthakārapaṭiñ‍ñaisu samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu kau nu khau sau saṃsārassa pāraṃ nibbānaṃ upagatau| kau pattau amataugadhanti nibbānapatiṭṭhaṃ vimaukkhamaggaṃ kau pattau adhigatauti atthau| kassa dhammaṃ paṭicchāmīti kassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā auvādadhammaṃ paṭiggaṇhāmi paṭipaj‍jāmi| paramatthavijānananti paramatthassa vijānanaṃ, aviparītappavattinivattiyau pavaidaintanti atthau|

antauvaṅkagatau āsīti vaṅkaṃ vuc‍cati diṭṭhigataṃ manauvaṅkabhāvatau, sabbaipi vā kilaisā, antauti pana hadayavaṅkassa antau, hadayabbhantaragatakilaisavaṅkau vā ahausīti atthau| macchauva ghasamāmisanti āmisaṃ ghasantau khādantau macchau viya, gilabaḷisau macchau viyāti adhippāyau| baddhau mahindapāsaina, vaipacityasurau yathāti mahindassa sak‍kassa pāsaina baddhau yathā vaipacitti asurindau asairivihārī mahādukkhappattau, aivamahaṃ pubbai kilaisapāsaina baddhau āsiṃ, asairivihārī mahādukkhappattauti adhippāyau|

añchāmīti ākaḍḍhāmi| nanti kilaisapāsaṃ| na muñ‍cāmīti na maucaimi| asmā saukapariddavāti imasmā saukaparidaivavaṭṭatau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yathāpāsaina baddhau migau sūkarau vā maucanupāyaṃ ajānantau paripphandamānau taṃ āviñchantau bandhanaṃ daḷhaṃ karauti, aivaṃ ahaṃ pubbai  kilaisapāsaina paṭimuk‍kau maucanupāyaṃ ajānantau kāyasañ‍caitanādivasaina paripphandamānau taṃ na maucaisiṃ, añ‍ñadatthu taṃ daḷhaṃ karauntau saukādinā paraṃ kilaisaṃ aiva pāpuṇinti| kau mai bandhaṃ muñ‍caṃ laukai, sambaudhiṃ vaidayissatīti imasmiṃ laukai aitaṃ kilaisabandhanaina bandhaṃ muñ‍cantau sambujjhati aitaināti ‘‘sambaudhī’’ti laddhanāmaṃ vimaukkhamaggaṃ kau mai vaidayissati ācikkhissatīti atthau| ‘‘bandhamuñ‍ca’’ntipi paṭhanti, bandhā, bandhassa vā maucakaṃ sambaudhinti yaujanā|

ādisantanti daisaintaṃ| pabhaṅgunanti pabhañ‍janaṃ kilaisānaṃ viddhaṃsanaṃ, pabhaṅgunaṃ vā dhammappavattiṃ ādisantaṃ kathaintaṃ jarāya mac‍cunau ca pavāhanaṃ kassa dhammaṃ paṭicchāmi| ‘‘paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti vā pāṭhau, sau aivatthau| vicikicchākaṅkhāganthitanti ‘‘ahausiṃ nu khau ahamatītamaddhāna’’ntiādinayappavattāya (ma॰ ni॰ 1.18; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.20) vicikicchāya āsappanaparisappanākāravuttiyā kaṅkhāya ca ganthitaṃ| sārambhabalasañ‍ñutanti karaṇuttariyakaraṇalakkhaṇaina balappattaina sārambhaina yuttaṃ| kaudhappattamanatthaddhanti sabbattha kaudhaina yuttamanasā  thaddhabhāvaṃ gataṃ abhijappappadāraṇaṃ| icchitālābhādivasaina hi taṇhā sattānaṃ cittaṃ padālaintī viya pavattati| dūrai ṭhitassāpi vijjhanupāyatāya taṇhāva dhanu samupatiṭṭhati uppaj‍jati aitasmāti taṇhādhanusamuṭṭhānaṃ, diṭṭhisal‍laṃ| taṃ pana yasmā vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, dasavatthukā micchādiṭṭhīti tiṃsappabhaidaṃ, tasmā vuttaṃ ‘‘dvai ca pan‍narasāyuta’’nti, dvikkhattuṃ pan‍narasabhaidavantanti atthau| passa aurasikaṃ bāḷhaṃ, bhaitvāna yadi tiṭṭhatīti yaṃ urasambandhanīyatāya aurasikaṃ bāḷhaṃ balavataraṃ bhaitvāna hadayaṃ vinivijjhitvā tasmiṃyaiva hadayai tiṭṭhati, taṃ passāti attānamaiva ālapati|

anudiṭṭhīnaṃ appahānanti anudiṭṭhibhūtānaṃ saisadiṭṭhīnaṃ appahānakāraṇaṃ| yāva hi sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi santānatau na vigacchati, tāva sassatadiṭṭhiādīnaṃ appahānamaivāti| saṅkappaparataijitanti saṅkappaina micchāvitak‍kaina parai parajanai nissayalakkhaṇaṃ patipatitai taijitaṃ ussāhitaṃ| taina viddhau pavaidhāmīti taina diṭṭhisal‍laina yathā hadayaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati, aivaṃ viddhau pavaidhāmi saṅkappāmi sassatucchaidādivasaina itau citau ca parivaṭṭāmi| pattaṃva mālutairitanti mālutaina vāyunā airitaṃ vaṇṭatau muttaṃ dumapattaṃ viya|

ajjhattaṃ mai samuṭṭhāyāti yathā laukai sal‍laṃ nāma bāhiratau uṭṭhāya ajjhattaṃ nimmathaitvā bādhati, na aivamidaṃ| idaṃ pana ajjhattaṃ mai mama attabhāvai samuṭṭhāya sau attabhāvasañ‍ñitau chaphassāyatanakāyau yathā khippaṃ sīghaṃ pac‍cati, ḍayhati| yathā kiṃ? aggi viya sanissayaḍāhakau taṃyaiva māmakaṃ mama santakaṃ attabhāvaṃ ḍahantau yattha uppan‍nau, tatthaiva sarati pavattati|

taṃ na passāmi taikicchanti tādisāya tikicchāya niyuttatāya taikicchaṃ  sal‍lakattaṃ bhisak‍kaṃ taṃ na passāmi| yau maitaṃ sal‍lamuddharaiti yau bhisak‍kau aitaṃ diṭṭhisal‍laṃ kilaisasal‍lañ‍ca uddharaiyya, uddharantau ca nānāraj‍jaina raj‍jusadisasaṅkhātāya aisanisalākāya pavaisaitvāna satthaina kantitvā nāñ‍ñaina mantāgadappayaugaina vicikicchitaṃ sal‍laṃ tikicchituṃ sak‍kāti āharitvā yaujaitabbaṃ| vicikicchitanti, ca nidassanamattamaitaṃ| sabbassapi kilaisasal‍lassa vasaina atthau vaiditabbau|

asatthauti sattharahitau| avaṇauti vaṇaina vinā| abbhantarapassayanti abbhantarasaṅkhātaṃ hadayaṃ nissāya ṭhitaṃ| ahiṃsanti apīḷaintau| ‘‘ahiṃsā’’ti ca pāṭhau, ahiṃsāya apīḷanaināti  atthau| ayañhaittha saṅkhaipatthau – kau nu khau kiñ‍ci satthaṃ aggahaitvā vaṇañ‍ca akarauntau tatau aiva sabbagattāni abādhaintau mama hadayabbhantaragataṃ pīḷājananatau antau tudanatau antau ruddhanatau ca paramatthainaiva sal‍labhūtaṃ kilaisasal‍laṃ uddharissatīti|

aivaṃ dasahi gāthāhi pubbai attanā cintitākāraṃ dassaitvā punapi taṃ pakārantaraina dassaituṃ ‘‘dhammappati hi sau saiṭṭhau’’tiādimāha| tattha dhammappatīti dhammanimittaṃ dhammahaitu| hīti nipātamattaṃ| sau saiṭṭhauti sau puggalau uttamau| visadausappavāhakauti yau mayhaṃ rāgādikilaisassa pavāhakau ucchin‍nakau| gambhīrai patitassa mai, thalaṃ pāṇiñ‍ca dassayaiti kau nu khau atigambhīrai saṃsāramahaughai patitassa mayhaṃ ‘‘mā bhāyī’’ti assāsaintau nibbānathalaṃ taṃsampāpakaṃ ariyamaggahatthañ‍ca dassaiyya|

rahadaihamasmi  augāḷhauti mahati saṃsārarahadai ahamasmi sasīsaṃ nimuj‍janavasaina autiṇṇau anupaviṭṭhau| ahāriyarajamattikaiti apanaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyau rāgādirajau mattikā kaddamau aitassāti ahāriyarajamattikau, rahadau| tasmiṃ rahadasmiṃ| ‘‘ahāriyarajamantikai’’ti vā pāṭhau, antikai ṭhitarāgādīsu dun‍nīharaṇīyarāgādirajaiti atthau| santadausapaṭicchādanalakkhaṇā māyā, parasampattiasahanalakkhaṇā usūyā, karaṇuttariyakaraṇalakkhaṇau sārambhau, cittālasiyalakkhaṇaṃ thinaṃ, kāyālasiyalakkhaṇaṃ middhanti imai pāpadhammā patthaṭā yaṃ rahadaṃ, tasmiṃ māyāusūyasārambhathinamiddhamapatthaṭai, makārau caittha padasandhikarau vuttau| yathāvuttaihi imaihi pāpadhammaihi patthaṭaiti atthau|

uddhac‍camaighathanitaṃ, saṃyaujanavalāhakanti vacanavipal‍lāsaina vuttaṃ, bhantasabhāvaṃ uddhac‍caṃ maighathanitaṃ maighagaj‍jitaṃ aitaisanti uddhac‍camaighathanitā| dasavidhā saṃyaujanā  aiva valāhakā aitaisanti saṃyaujanavalāhakā| vāhā mahāudakavāhasadisā rāganissitā micchāsaṅkappā asubhādīsu ṭhitā kuddiṭṭhiṃ maṃ vahanti apāyasamuddamaiva uddissa kaḍḍhantīti atthau|

savanti sabbadhi sautāti taṇhāsautau, diṭṭhisautau, mānasautau, avij‍jāsautau, kilaisasautauti imai pañ‍capisautā cakkhudvārādīnaṃ vasaina sabbaisu rūpādīsu ārammaṇaisu savanatau ‘‘rūpataṇhā…pai॰… dhammataṇhā’’tiādinā (vibha॰ 204, 232) sabbabhāgaihi vā savanatau sabbadhi savanti| latāti palivaiṭhanaṭṭhaina saṃsibbanaṭṭhaina latā viyāti latā, taṇhā| ubbhij‍ja tiṭṭhatīti chahi dvāraihi ubbhij‍jitvā rūpādīsu ārammaṇaisu tiṭṭhati| tai sautaiti taṇhādikai  sautai mama santānai sandantai maggasaitubandhanaina kau purisavisaisau nivāraiyya, taṃ latanti taṇhālataṃ, maggasatthaina kau chaicchati chindissati|

vailaṃ karauthāti taisaṃ sautānaṃ vailaṃ saituṃ karautha san‍nivāraṇaṃ| bhaddantaiti ālapanākāradassanaṃ| mā tai manaumayau sautauti udakasautau auḷārikau, tassa bālamahājanainapi saituṃ  katvā nivāraṇaṃ sak‍kā| ayaṃ pana manaumayau sautau sukhumau dun‍nivāraṇau| sau yathā udakasautau vaḍḍhantau kūlai ṭhitaṃ rukkhaṃ pātaitvāva nāsaiti, aivaṃ tumhai apāyatīrai ṭhitai tattha sahasā pātaitvā apāyasamuddaṃ pāpaintau mā luvai mā vināsaiyya mā anayabyasanaṃ pāpaiyyāti atthau|

aivaṃ ayaṃ thairau purimattabhāvai parimadditasaṅkhārattā ñāṇaparipākaṃ gatattā pavattidukkhaṃ upadhāraintau yathā vicikicchādikai saṃkilaisadhammai pariggaṇhi, tamākāraṃ dassaitvā idāni jātasaṃvaigau kiṃkusalagavaisī satthu santikaṃ gatau yaṃ visaisaṃ adhimuc‍ci, taṃ dassaintau ‘‘aivaṃ mai bhayajātassā’’tiādimāha| tattha aivaṃ mai bhayajātassāti aivaṃ vuttappakāraina saṃsārai jātabhayassa apārā aurimatīratau sappaṭibhayatau saṃsāravaṭṭatau ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau muñ‍caiyya’’nti pāraṃ nibbānaṃ, aisatau gavaisatau, tāṇau sadaivakassa laukassa tāṇabhūtau kilaisasamucchaidanī pañ‍ñā āvudhau aitassāti pañ‍ñāvudhau| diṭṭhadhammikādiatthaina sattānaṃ yathārahaṃ anusāsanatau satthā, isisaṅghaina aggasāvakādiariyapuggalasamūhaina nisaivitau payirupāsitau isisaṅghanisaivitau, saupānanti daisanāñāṇaina suṭṭhu katattā abhisaṅkhatattā sukataṃ, upak‍kilaisavirahitatau suddhaṃ, saddhāpañ‍ñādisārabhūtaṃ dhammasāramayaṃ  paṭipakkhaihi acalanīyatau daḷhaṃ, vipassanāsaṅkhātaṃ saupānaṃ mahaughaina vuyhamānassa mayhaṃ satthā pādāsi, dadantau ca ‘‘iminā tai sautthi bhavissatī’’ti samassāsaintau mā bhāyīti ca abravi, kathaisi|

satipaṭṭhānapāsādanti taina vipassanāsaupānaina kāyānupassanādinā laddhabbacatubbidhasāmañ‍ñaphalavisaisaina catubhūmisampan‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānapāsādaṃ āruhitvā pac‍cavaikkhisaṃ catusac‍cadhammaṃ maggañāṇaina patiavaikkhiṃ paṭivijjhiṃ| yaṃ taṃ pubbai amañ‍ñissaṃ, sak‍kāyābhirataṃ pajanti aivaṃ paṭividdhasac‍cau yaṃ sak‍kāyai ‘‘ahaṃ mamā’’ti abhirataṃ pajaṃ titthiyajanaṃ taina parikappitaattānañ‍ca pubbai sāratau amañ‍ñissaṃ| yadā ca maggamaddakkhiṃ, nāvāya abhirūhananti ariyamagganāvāya abhiruhanūpāyabhūtaṃ yadā vipassanāmaggaṃ yāthāvatau addakkhiṃ| tatau paṭṭhāya taṃ titthiyajanaṃ attānañ‍ca anadhiṭṭhāya cittai aṭṭhapaitvā aggahaitvā titthaṃ nibbānasaṅkhātassa amatamahāpārassa  titthabhūtaṃ ariyamaggadassanaṃ sabbaihi maggaihi sabbaihi kusaladhammaihi uk‍kaṭṭhaṃ addakkhiṃ, yāthāvatau apassinti atthau|

aivaṃ attanau anuttaraṃ maggādhigamaṃ pakāsaitvā idāni tassa daisakaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ thaumaintau ‘‘sal‍laṃ attasamuṭṭhāna’’ntiādimāha| tattha sal‍lanti diṭṭhimānādikilaisasal‍laṃ| attasamuṭṭhānanti ‘‘aha’’nti mānaṭṭhānatāya ‘‘attā’’ti ca laddhanāmai attabhāvai sambhūtaṃ| bhavanaittippabhāvitanti bhavataṇhāsamuṭṭhitaṃ bhavataṇhāsan‍nissayaṃ| sā hi diṭṭhimānādīnaṃ sambhavau| aitaisaṃ appavattāyāti yathāvuttānaṃ pāpadhammānaṃ appavattiyā anuppādāya| daisaisi maggamuttamanti uttamaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, tadupāyañ‍ca vipassanāmaggaṃ kathaisi|

dīgharattānusayitanti anamataggai saṃsārai cirakālaṃ santānai anu anu sayitaṃ kāraṇalābhaina uppaj‍janārahabhāvaina thāmagataṃ, tatau ca cirarattaṃ adhiṭṭhitaṃ santānaṃ ajjhāruyha ṭhitaṃ| ganthanti abhijjhākāyaganthādiṃ mama santānai ganthabhūtaṃ kilaisavisadausaṃ pavāhanau buddhau bhagavā attanau daisanānubhāvaina apānudī parijahāpaisi, ganthaisu hi anavasaisatau pahīnaisu appahīnau nāma kilaisau natthīti|

tailakānitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. raṭṭhapālatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

passa  cittakatantiādikā āyasmatau raṭṭhapālatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau uppattitau puraitaramaiva haṃsavatīnagarai gahapatimahāsālakulai nibbattitvā vayappattau pitu ac‍cayaina gharāvāsai patiṭṭhitau ratanakauṭṭhāgārakammikaina dassitaṃ aparimāṇaṃ kulavaṃsānugataṃ dhanaṃ disvā ‘‘imaṃ aittakaṃ dhanarāsiṃ mayhaṃ pituayyakapayyakādayau attanā saddhiṃ gahaitvā gantuṃ nāsakkhiṃsu, mayā pana gahaitvā gantuṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti cintaitvā kapaṇaddhikādīnaṃ mahādānaṃ adāsi| sau abhiñ‍ñālābhiṃ aikaṃ tāpasaṃ upaṭṭhahantau taina daivalaukādhipac‍cai niyaujitau yāvajīvaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā tatau cutau  daivalaukai nibbattitvā dibbasampattiṃ anubhavantau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā tatau cutau manussalaukai bhin‍naṃ raṭṭhaṃ sandhāraituṃ samatthassa kulassa aikaputtakau hutvā nibbatti|

taina  ca samayaina padumuttarau bhagavā laukai uppaj‍jitvā pavattitavaradhammacak‍kau vainaiyyasattai nibbānamahānagarasaṅkhātaṃ khaimantabhūmiṃ sampāpaisi| atha sau kulaputtau anuk‍kamaina viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ upāsakaihi saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gantvā satthāraṃ dhammaṃ daisaintaṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau parisapariyantai nisīdi| taina khau pana samayaina satthā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ saddhāpabbajitānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi| taṃ disvā sau pasan‍namānasau tadatthāya cittaṃ ṭhapaitvā satasahassabhikkhuparivutassa bhagavatau mahatā sak‍kāraina sattāhaṃ mahādānaṃ pavattaitvā paṇidhānaṃ akāsi| satthā tassa anantarāyaina ijjhanabhāvaṃ disvā ‘‘anāgatai gautamassa nāma sammāsambuddhassa sāsanai saddhāpabbajitānaṃ aggau bhavissatī’’ti byākāsi| sau satthāraṃ bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca vanditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| sau tattha yāvatāyukaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā tatau cavitvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau itau dvainavutai kappai phussassa bhagavatau kālai satthu vaimātikabhātikaisu tīsu rājaputtaisu satthāraṃ upaṭṭhahantaisu taisaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyāya kic‍caṃ akāsi|

aivaṃ tattha tattha bhavai taṃ taṃ bahuṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kururaṭṭhai thul‍lakauṭṭhikanigamai raṭṭhapālasaiṭṭhinau gaihai nibbatti, tassa bhin‍naṃ raṭṭhaṃ sandhāraituṃ samatthai kulai nibbattattā raṭṭhapālauti  vaṃsānugatamaiva nāmaṃ ahausi| sau mahatā parivāraina vaḍḍhantau anuk‍kamaina yaubbanapattau mātāpitūhi patirūpaina dāraina saṃyaujitau mahantai ca yasai patiṭṭhāpitau dibbasampattisadisaṃ sampattiṃ pac‍canubhauti| atha bhagavā kururaṭṭhai janapadacārikaṃ carantau thul‍lakauṭṭhikaṃ anupāpuṇi| taṃ sutvā raṭṭhapālau kulaputtau satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitukāmau sattāhaṃ bhattacchaidaṃ katvā kicchaina kasiraina mātāpitarau anujānāpaitvā satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yācitvā satthu āṇattiyā añ‍ñatarassa thairassa santikai pabbajitvā yaunisaumanasikāraina kammaṃ karauntau vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.2.97-111) –

‘‘padumuttarassa  bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

varanāgau mayā din‍nau, īsādantau urūḷhavā||

‘‘saitacchattau pasaubhitau, sakappanau sahatthipau|

agghāpaitvāna taṃ sabbaṃ, saṅghārāmaṃ akārayiṃ||

‘‘catupañ‍ñāsasahassāni, pāsādai kārayiṃ ahaṃ|

mahaughadānaṃ karitvāna, niyyādaisiṃ mahaisinau||

‘‘anumaudi  mahāvīrau, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

sabbai janai hāsayantau, daisaisi amataṃ padaṃ||

‘‘taṃ mai buddhau viyākāsi, jalajuttaranāmakau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

‘‘catupañ‍ñāsasahassāni, pāsādai kārayī ayaṃ|

kathayissāmi vipākaṃ, suṇautha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘aṭṭhārasasahassāni, kūṭāgārā bhavissarai|

byamhuttamamhi nibbattā, sabbasauṇṇamayā ca tai||

‘‘pañ‍ñāsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

aṭṭhapañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

aḍḍhai kulai mahābhaugai, nibbattissati tāvadai||

‘‘sau pacchā pabbajitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

raṭṭhapālauti nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

‘‘padhānapahitattau sau, upasantau nirūpadhi|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

‘‘uṭṭhāya abhinikkhamma, jahitā bhaugasampadā|

khaiḷapiṇḍaiva bhaugamhi, paimaṃ mayhaṃ na vij‍jati||

‘‘vīriyaṃ  mai dhuradhaurayhaṃ, yaugakkhaimādhivāhanaṃ|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā satthāraṃ anujānāpaitvā mātāpitarau passituṃ thul‍lakauṭṭhikaṃ gantvā, tattha sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya carantau pitu nivaisanai ābhidausikaṃ kummāsaṃ labhitvā taṃ amataṃ viya paribhuñ‍jantau, pitarā nimantitau svātanāya adhivāsaitvā, dutiyadivasai pitu nivaisanai piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā alaṅkatapaṭiyattai itthāgārajanai upagantvā ‘‘kīdisā nāma tā, ayyaputta, accharāyau, yāsaṃ tvaṃ haitu brahmacariyaṃ carasī’’tiādīni (ma॰ ni॰ 2.301) vatvā, palaubhanakammaṃ kātuṃ āraddhai tassa adhippāyaṃ parivattaitvā anic‍catādipaṭisaṃyuttaṃ dhammaṃ kathaintau –

769.

 ‘‘passa  cittakataṃ bimbaṃ, arukāyaṃ samussitaṃ|

āturaṃ bahusaṅkappaṃ, yassa natthi dhuvaṃ ṭhiti||

770.

 ‘‘passa cittakataṃ rūpaṃ, maṇinā kuṇḍalaina ca|

aṭṭhiṃ tacaina aunaddhaṃ, saha vatthaihi saubhati||

771.

 ‘‘alattakakatā pādā, mukhaṃ cuṇṇakamakkhitaṃ|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

772.

 ‘‘aṭṭhāpadakatā kaisā, naittā añ‍janamakkhitā|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

773.

 ‘‘añ‍janīva navā cittā, pūtikāyau alaṅkatau|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

774.

 ‘‘audahi  migavau pāsaṃ, nāsadā vāguraṃ migau|

bhutvā nivāpaṃ gacchāma, kandantai migabandhakai||

775.

 ‘‘chin‍nau  pāsau migavassa, nāsadā vāguraṃ migau|

bhutvā nivāpaṃ gacchāma, saucantai migaluddakai’’ti|| – imā gāthā abhāsi|

tattha cittakatanti cittaṃ kataṃ cittakataṃ, vatthābharaṇamālādīhi vicittaṃ katanti atthau| bimbanti dīghādibhāvaina yuttaṭṭhānaisu dīghādīhi aṅgapac‍caṅgaihi maṇḍitaṃ attabhāvaṃ| arukāyanti navan‍naṃ vaṇamukhānaṃ laumakūpānañ‍ca vasaina vissandamānaasuciṃ, sabbasau ca arubhūtaṃ vaṇabhūtaṃ arūnaṃ vā kāyaṃ| samussitanti tīhi aṭṭhisataihi samussitaṃ| āturanti sabbakālaṃ iriyāpathantarādīhi pariharitabbatāya nic‍caṃ gilānaṃ| bahusaṅkappanti bālajanaina abhūtaṃ āraupaitvā bahudhā saṅkappitabbaṃ| yassa natthi dhuvaṃ ṭhitīti yassa kāyassa dhuvabhāvau ṭhitisabhāvau natthi, aikaṃsatau bhaidanavikiraṇaviddhaṃsanadhammauyaiva| taṃ passāti samīpai ṭhitaṃ janaṃ, attānamaiva vā sandhāya vadati|

rūpanti sarīraṃ| sarīrampi hi ‘‘aṭṭhiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca, nhāruñ‍ca paṭic‍ca, maṃsañ‍ca paṭic‍ca, cammañ‍ca paṭic‍ca, ākāsau parivāritau ‘rūpantvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.306) rūpanti vuc‍cati| maṇinā kuṇḍalaina cāti sīsūpagādiābharaṇagataina maṇinā kuṇḍalaina cittakataṃ| aṭṭhiṃ tacaina aunaddhanti al‍lacammaina pariyaunaddhaṃ atiraikatisatapabhaidaṃ aṭṭhiṃ passāti yaujanā| kuṇḍalaina cāti ca-saddaina saisābharaṇālaṅkārai saṅgaṇhāti| saha vatthaihi saubhatīti tayidaṃ rūpaṃ maṇinā cittakatampi vatthaihi paṭicchāditamaiva saubhati, na apaṭicchāditanti  atthau| yai pana ‘‘aṭṭhitacainā’’ti paṭhanti, taisaṃ aṭṭhitacainaṃ aunaddhaṃ saubhati, aunaddhattā aṭṭhitacaināti atthau|

alattakakatāti alattakaina katarañ‍janā lākhāya saṃrañ‍jitā| pādāti caraṇā| mukhaṃ cuṇṇakamakkhitanti mukhaṃ cuṇṇakaina makkhitaṃ, yaṃ maṇḍanamanuyuttā sāsapakak‍kaina mukhapīḷakādīni haritvā lauṇamattikāya duṭṭhalauhitaṃ haritvā mukhacuṇṇakavilaipanaṃ karaunti, taṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| alanti bālassa andhaputhuj‍janassa nau  ca pāragavaisinau vaṭṭābhiratassa mauhāya sammauhanāya samatthaṃ tassa cittaṃ mauhaituṃ pariyattaṃ, pāragavaisinau pana vivaṭṭābhiratassa nau alaṃ na pariyattaṃ|

aṭṭhāpadakatāti  aṭṭhapadākāraina katā sañ‍citā purimabhāgai kaisai kappaitvā nalāṭassa paṭicchādanavasaina katā kaisaracanā aṭṭhapadaṃ nāma, yaṃ ‘‘alaka’’ntipi vuc‍cati| naittā añ‍janamakkhitāti ubhaupi nayanāni antau dvīsu antaisu ca yathā añ‍janacchāyā dissati, aivaṃ añ‍jitañ‍janāni|

añ‍janīva navā cittā, pūtikāyau alaṅkatauti yathā añ‍janī añ‍jananāḷikā navā abhinavā mālākammamakaradantādivasaina cittā bahi maṭṭhā uj‍jalā dassanīyā, antau pana na dassanīyā hauti, aivamaiva tāsaṃ kāyau nhānabbhañ‍janavatthālaṅkāraihi alaṅkatau bahi uj‍jalau, antau pana pūti nānappakāraasucīhi bharitau tiṭṭhatīti atthau|
